Isaya 1

1 Citabu cici cina wulolose we zinghani zono Mulungu yamugubulila Isaya u mwana wa Amozi, mu vilo vya Uziya, na Yotamu, na Ahazi, na Hezekiya, lono wali watemi wa Yuda na Yelusalemi.

Mulungu yakuwakalipila wanhu wakwe

2 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Muhulicize ta nimulonjelenyi anye nyo wulanga ne yinze!

Yo nene mono nawalisa i wana,

kanyhi nghawawilusa, ninga alu,

wakondya kumbedegala.

3 Hamba ne zing'ombe,

nha zimumanyile mono yazisawile,

ne zindogowe nazo,

zimanyile manyi manyi hanhu hono mutwa wawo kazidimilaga.

Ninga woyi hee!

I wanhu wangu Waisilayeli si wamanyile yaye,

hamba kwizuka du bwete.”

4 Holeka, alu mokongomezwa,

anye nyo likungugo lyono lina wubi,

anye nyo wanhu wono mwendile mafupo,

kanyhi muli wabi!

Ninga mumanye nha vyono,

u wubi wenyu wuwo,

womukulunhyenyi hasi!

Hambi anye nha mwamulema Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu mono yelile we Waisilayeli.

Mwakondya kumupitucila mugongo.

5 Ninga lu, ho so cici mukujendelela kumubedegala?

Ane nghuwona, mukali mwendile mucile kulipicizwa.

Alu mutwe wako Isilayeli,

wakondya kutuga mazeya gose,

nhumbula yako nayo,

hamonga no mutima wako,

vyose vyatimba.

6 Na ku wulawa ku zinzayo ze magulu gako,

sunga ko ku mutwe, musina vilungo,

langa mozili nghomele du, ne madali,

ne malonda gono gawolile,

gono si maminywe,

hamba kuwopwa na bandeji,

hamba kudejefywa ne mafuta yaye.

7 Ne yisi yenyu yaweza yitongo,

ne makaya genyu gatakamizwa no moto.

Ne wajenzi wayisola yisi yenyu ha meso genyu,

no kuyongomeza,

hamonga na vyose vyono vili mumo.

8 Nalu likaya lya Yelusalemi du lili wene, lyo lisigaye,

nalyo lyasigala nhe kaya lyono lipilimwe ne walugu,

lyono lyeza lisina hamba wutazi,

lyeza nha cilingo co mugunda we mizabibu kanyhi nha cilingo

cono cili mu mugunda we matanga mugati.

9 Na hono Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulungu we Mihanga yali yalece kusigaza mumo wanhu wanji womi,

mbeka, mhali Yelusalemi yongomezezwe lukulu,

nha vyono Sodoma na Gomola zongomezwa.

10 Nagwe Yelusalemi,

i walongola wako hamonga ne wanhu wako,

wose nha wabi wehwana ne wanhu wa Sodoma na Gomola.

Alu muhulicize zono Muwaha Mulungu yakumulonjelenyi.

Muteje likutu kuli zono Mulungu wetu yakumwijizenyi.

11 Yakutya nhavi, “Alu mwene, anye mukwihwesa vyono 'ne,

nghugasaka gago makumbiko genyu,

gono mukundavizya?

Ane nha nakondya kucilwa ne vilavywa vyenyu vinyanywe vye zi nduwilu,

vyono mukundavizya nha makumbiko,

hamonga ne mhama ye zimitugo zenyu zono zijinile.

Nakondya kuyikatala sunga ne sakami ye zing'ombe,

hamba ye zingholo, hamba ye zimhene.

12 Mwene nayo yo nani,

mono yakamutyenyi vyono mweze kundetela vivyo vyose,

lono mukuza kuhemba 'ne?

Hamba mu Nyumba yangu?

13 Holeka yo nghani ya bwete du,

yo kujendelela kundavizya vilavywa vyenyu.

Hambi ane nha si nghwendezwa kanyhi,

ne mhunjile yo wuvumba wono mukufusa.

Hamba ne zi sikukuu zenyu ze Ndawo yo Mwezi,

ne zi Sabato, hamba no wihangulize wenyu wo wuhembi,

ane hamba si nozivumila yaye,

hambi nha zakondya kuwifywa no wubi wenyu.

14 Nane hamba si nghuzisacila lukulu,

zi sikukuu zenyu ze Ndawo yo Mwezi,

na ze vilo vyono vyelile.

Hambi nha zeza nha mutegulo du kuli'ne,

wono si nosuma kuwudinghula yaye.

15 “Na lono mowa kunyasula miwoko yenyu kucanyha mu wulombe,

hamba si nomutondowozenyi yaye.

Hamba mulombe muhidimbaye,

si nomuhulicizenyi ng'o,

hambi miwoko yenyu,

nha yimemile wuwulaji wose.

16 Holeka alu, mwizuguze, mwiyeze,

muwusize lukulu hali'ne,

u wubi wono mukwituma,

mulece kwituma vibi,

17 mwiyijize ku wituma ziswanu.

Mwitume zo wiling'ano,

muwataze wono wakwiwonelwa,

muwapele ze ling'ani i wana wono wasina walezi,

muwataze i wacekulu wafwile mu zinghani zawo.”

18 Alu Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ale, muze cilongane cizimale.

Vyono hamba masutya genyu gawe madunghu ninga

ane nomukwemenyi muwe wazelu swee nha seluji.

Hamba gawe madunghu kasu,

gowa mazelu swee, nho wukunzeje we ngholo nzelu.

19 Na hono muhulice 'ne,

nanye molya vinhu viswanu vyono vikulawa mu'si.

20 Ninga hono muleme no kufyula,

mbeka mofwa wose mukongojele.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, mono nizilonjile zizi.”

I likaya lyo wubi

21 Ati, ta, mulanje!

Likaya lyono lyali yihuwilwa,

alu lyeza yikaya lyo wusenyha!

Na kumbe mu mapango ganji,

ho lyali limemile wanhu we ling'ani.

Ninga lu mulanje, lyekala ne wanhu wawulaji du.

22 Gwe Yelusalemi, mwene wumanyile,

ha mwamulizo vyono wejelaga winha fweza,

ninga aluno, woyi, yeza nghani ya bwete du.

Wali winha divayi swanu muno,

ninga aluno weza winha malenga du.

23 Ne walongola wako weza wagaluci,

nalu wakondya kwiyenda ne wahizi,

alu weza wabocelaga vipegwa hamonga ne mafupo.

Wakondya kuleka no kuwataza,

i wana wono wasina walezi, mu zinghani zawo,

hamba kuwahuliciza bwete i wacekulu wafwile mu zinghani zawo.

24 Nalu muhulicize zono yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yakutya,

“Alu, nowalipiciza walugu wangu,

nawo si wongaza kanyhi.

25 Nomunozezenyi anye nghani swanu,

nomwezenyi mu wavu wenyu wose,

nomuwusizenyi zimbi zose.

26 Nane no mupelenyi walongola,

hamonga ne wanyamhala wenyu,

nha vyono mwali mwina nawo baho ha mwamulizo.

Na hanyuma nhondo, Yelusalemi ho yocemwa,

likaya lyo wiling'ano, likaya lihuwilwa.”

27 Alu, vyono Muwaha Mulungu mwihuwilwa,

holeka koliponya likaya lya Yelusalemi,

hamonga na wose wono wali mumo wono wonena wubi wawo.

28 Ninga kowongomeza i wasutyi wose,

hamonga ne wabezi, wono wakumubeza mwene.

Kanyhi kolowawulaga wose,

wono wakumulema mwene.

29 Nanye molokalala muno,

vyono mwahembaga mabici,

hamonga ne zi busitani zisiwanulwe.

30 Nanye molonyala nho mubici wo munghola wono wunyalile,

kanyhi kotya nha busitani yono si yimwajilwaga malenga.

31 Nawo i wanhu we vilungo,

wolokongomezwa no wubi wawo weneco,

nha vyono mabeleje gakuwa kwaka mu moto.

Na hasina munhu mono kolowemila mu wongomele wuwo.


Isaya 2

Mulamu we cibitilila

(Mik 4:1-3)

1 Azi zo nghani zono Mulungu yamulonjela Isaya u mwana wa Amozi, za kuli Yuda na Yelusalemi.

2 Mu vilo vyono vikuza litunda lyono lizenjigwe

Nyumba yo Mulungu,

lyolosikamya mucanyha ye matunda

lyolokwinulwa kuciza vigongo vyose,

ne makungugo gose golokuza kuli lyene,

3 nawo wanhu wolowa kutya,

“Alenyi cibite kwi'tunda ku Nyumba yo Mulungu,

ku Nyumba yo Mulungu we Waisilayeli.

Mwene nayo kocijiza zono yakuhila ase cizitume.

Nase cojendela mu zinzila zakwe zono kohagula mwene.

Hambi wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu,

nha wulawaga ku Yelusalemi

kalongaga ne wanhu wakwe ku wulawa ku Sayuni.”

4 Kololamula zo wiwanganile we makungugo mawaha.

Nawo wolotyana mimaje yawo yiwe masili,

ne migoha yawo yiwe mimaje yo kududulila.

Ne makungugo hamba si golokwihoma yaye,

hamba kwiwikaniza so wihomi kanyhi.

5 Holeka anye nyo wana wa Yakobo,

kenhenyi cijendele mu wuzelu wono Muwaha Mulungu yakucipela.

Widunhumuzi wose wolokogomezwa

6 Gwe go Mulungu, hongo wakondya kuwaleka wanhu wako du,

i wana wana Yakobo!

Vyono yisi yawo luno,

yakondya kumema myoto yono yilawile kucilima,

hamonga no wutowa visangu nhe Wafilisiti.

Wanhu wako watyatya myoto ye cijenzi.

7 Ne yisi yawo kumbe ho yimemile fweza na zahabu,

ne viwutwa vyawo si vimalikaga yaye.

Ne yisi yawo yimemile fwalasi,

ne matuka gawo go wulugu menji muno hamba si gakuwala yaye.

8 Ninga kanyhi yisi yawo yimemile vihwani vye milungu,

nawo wakuvihemba vinhu vyono wenozeza weneco kwe miwoko yawo.

9 Ninga 'gwe walekaje!

Hambi wose nha wolonyetwa, no kukozwa cevu.

Holeka go Muwaha Mulungu, wulece kuwanenela!

10 Ninga lizuwa lyolokuza,

lono wolowa kwinjila mu mapango,

na mu mapalika ge mabwe mawaha ga mu matunda,

wanji wolowa kuhimba mina wenjile,

ho weponye ne likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

wakujela kwihundika ha vilungo vyakwe no lukumyo lwakwe!

11 Kanyhi lizuwa likuza,

lono wiwoni wo munhu wolomalika,

no widunhumuzi wo munhu wolokongomezwa.

No Muwaha Mulungu du, yo kolopegwa lukumyo yali wene.

12 Na mwizuwa lilyo Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulngu we Mihanga,

kolowanyetya wose we vilungo,

na wose i wewoni, hamonga ne wevuni wose.

13 Koloyibenanjiza ne mibici yose mitali ye mihagata yono yili ku Lebanoni,

sunga ne mibici yose ye minghola, yono yili mu yisi ya Bashani.

14 Ne matunda gose matali, ne vigongo vyose,

mwene kolo gakumulanga hawe wuhihi,

15 hamonga ne minala yose mitali ne magomawakali gayo.

16 Kolozidodomeza mu malenga zingalawa zimbaha kanyhi swanu.

17 No wiwoni wo munhu,

baho langa ho wulamalicila,

no widunhumuzi nawo,

baho ho wolokogomezezwa lukulu.

18 Avi navyo i vihwani, nhondo ho vyolokwajililila lukulu.

No Muwaha Mulungu du, u Mudaha vyose,

yo kolopegwa lukumyo yali wene.

19 Wanhu wolotiza,

wawe kwinjila mu mapango na mu mabwe mawaha ga mu matunda,

na wanji wolohimba mina hasi,

wenjile ho weponye ne likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

wakujela kwihundika ha vilungo vyakwe no lukumyo lwakwe,

lono mwene yeza kuza kuyisinjisa yinze.

20 Ne lizuwa lilyo lilawa kuza,

i wanhu wologagumilila mafuto ne mabudibudi,

vihwani vyawo vye zahabu na vye zifweza,

vyono wali wenozeze, ho wavihembe.

21 No Muwaha Mulungu kolo yakuza,

kuloyicicimya yinze, i wanhu wolotiza,

wawe kwinjila mu mapango na mu mapalika ge mabwe mawaha ga mu matunda,

na wanji wolowa kuhimba mina hasi wenjile,

ho weponye ne likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

wakujela kwihundika ha vilungo vyakwe,

no lukumyo lwakwe.

22 Mulecele lukulu kumwitumbila munhu mono wumi wakwe wudumaga nha wehele.

Hambi nha si yakwiliye cinji?


Isaya 3

Wutilibulwe wa mu Yelusalemi

1 Lanje ta! Muwaha Mulungu,

u Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulungu we mihanga,

yakuwawusiza vinhu vyose vyono wetumbiye Yelusalemi na Yuda.

Kowawusiza zindigwa ne malenga gawo,

2 hamonga ne wanhu we nghakamo,

ne wasilikale wawo vitung'ati,

ne mahakimu wawo, ne wawizi wawo,

ne watowa cisangu wawo,

ne walongola wawo we selikali,

3 ne wasilikale wo wulugu,

ne walongola wawo, ne wanyamhala wawo,

hamonga ne waganga wose we cihalahala.

4 Muwaha Mulungu kolowawika wadodo wo wawe watemi wawo heya,

i wana wagalika wo wolowatema.

5 Nawo wanhu wolowa kwigaza munhu no muyagwe.

I wadodo wolowabedegala wawaha,

nawo wono wabedegaywe,

hamba si wolowapela cogopo wono wena lilumbo.

6 Na mu vilo vivyo, wanhu we mhuga monga,

wolomuhagula yumonga wa muli wene,

wawe kumutya, “Agwe muyetu wina mwenda wo kuvwala,

holeka ase cakuwika wuwe mulongola wetu mu vilo vivi vye magazo.”

7 Ninga nayo kolowapitula yawe kutya,

“Nye, ane nalema!

Hambi'ne nha si nosuma kumutazenyi cinji.

Ku kaya yangu, nha kusina ndigwa yo yose,

hamba vivwalo.

Holeka mulece kumbika niwe mulongola wenyu!”

8 Heya, mbeka, Yelusalemi yongomezwa!

Nayo Yuda yikuvulumuka!

Ku soko vyono mu zinghani zawo na mu metumo gawo,

wamubedegalaga Muwaha Mulungu,

no kumutovula calinze.

9 No wuhijila-mbejele wawo,

wukuwa kuwahomelela weneco.

Sunga no wubi wawo, nha wuli calinze,

nha vyono wanhu wa Sodoma wetuma.

Holeka wokongomezwa,

no wongomezwe wuwo, weletela weneco.

10 Ninga wanhu we ling'ani, wolowa wasangalaye,

ne zinghani zose, zolowa swanu du kuli wene.

Nawo, wolosangalalila kuli ze metumo gawo.

11 Ninga woyi he, kuli wanhu wabi,

hambi nha wolopegwa wulipicizwe,

nha vyono wene nawo wawatendelaga vibi wayawo.

12 Nawo wanhu wangu wokwigalilwa ne wana,

ne wacekulu wo wolowatema.

Nye nyo wanhu wangu,

i walongola wenyu wakumwajilizenyi,

ho mulece kuyiwona nzila yono munojeye muyikole.

Muwaha Mulungu yakuwaling'aniza wanhu wakwe

13 Muwaha Mulungu, yewikanize ho yalonje calo,

yewikanize ho yawaling'anize wanhu wakwe.

14 Muwaha Mulungu,

yakuwacema wanyamhala we wanhu wakwe,

hamonga ne walongola we wanhu wakwe,

ho yawaling'anize.

Nayo yakuwasitaka yakutya,

“Yo nyenye mono mwapumha migunda ye mizabibu,

ne zikaya zenyu,

zimemile vinhu vyono mwawaboka waciwa.

15 “Ninga hamba si yili ling'ani kuli'nye,

ho muwabanghanize wanhu wangu ko kuwewonela waciwa.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulungu we mihanga yo nizilonjile zizi.”

Suguzi kuli Wacekulu wa mu Yelusalemi

16 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Lanje ta, i wacekulu wa mu Yelusalemi vyono wewoni!

Wajendaga wagalamice mhula zawo mucanyha.

Nhondo zose wekalaga wakukonyeza,

ne meso gawo go wulembule.

Wajendaga wakunyata, ne zimbilili zawo zikulila.

17 Ninga 'gwe, wugole du, nane nowatonhya.

Nowaceta zimvwili mu mitwe yawo,

niwalece wena mikulungu.”

18 Lizuwa likuza, lono Muwaha Mulungu,

kolowawusiza i wacekulu wose wa Yelusalemi,

i vinhu vyose vyono vikuwapela wiwoni.

Kolowawusiza wihambo wono wakuvwala mu migulu yawo,

na mu mitwe yawo, na mu zi singo zawo,

19 hamonga na mu miwoko yawo.

Kolowawusiza sunga ne vitambala vyawo vyono wegubicizaga ku wusu,

20 hamonga ne zi kofiya zawo,

ne zimhiji zawo zono wavwalaga mu zi mhukumhuku ze miwoko yawo,

na mu viwino vyawo.

21 Hamonga ne zi nyamwidole zawo,

zono wavwalaga mu vidole vyawo,

na zono wavwalaga mu mhula zawo.

22 Nowawusiza hamonga ne vivwalo vyawo viswanu,

i myenda ye mawala ne mitote,

ne magawoni, ne mikoba midodo ya mu mawoko,

23 hamonga ne vilole vyawo vyo kwilanjila,

ne vitambala vye bafuta vye mafuwa,

ne mikumbo miwaha,

hamonga ne vitambala vitali vyono wegubicizaga ku wusu.

24 No munungho mubi,

wolohomela ha wibaka vye mhunjile swanu.

Woleka wuvwala misipi miswanu,

langa wovwala milejehe.

No wuwe wo kuwa na mvwili swanu mu mitwe yawo,

alu wowa na mikulungu.

Nawo mu wuvwala myenda yo wulenzi,

alu wovwala masambi-sambi du.

No wuswanu wawo wose ku wulangwe, wogaluka cevu!

25 Nawo wanhu wa mwi'kaya,

sunga ne wanhu we vilungo,

wose wowulagwa ku wulugu.

26 Nayo milango ye likaya, yokalala no kulila.

Ne likaya lyokwihwana no mucekulu mulekwa

mono yekaye hasi mu manghundi.


Isaya 4

1 Ne vilo vivyo kolo vikuza, wacekulu mupunghati, wolomwisoka mulume monga, wakutya, “Ase cokwilisa no kwivwika co weneco du, ninga cono cikusaka du kuli'gwe, cikusaka cijele vyono cili wacekulu zako, ho ciwusizwe cevu co wigane.”

Yelusalemi yolonozelezwa

2 Vilo vikuza, lono Muwaha Mulungu kolovinoza vilimwa vyose vya mu'si, hamonga ne mibici yose, viwiluce viwe kwendeza. Nawo i wanhu wa Isilayeli wono wopona, wolosangalala no kwilumbila mu vilimwa vya mu'si.
3 Na muli wose mono kololekwa mu Yelusalemi, mono Mulungu yamulecile mumi, yuyo kolocemwa yelile.
4 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, kwe vilungo vyakwe, kololing'aniza no kulyeza likungugo, no kuwukwema wubi wa Yelusalemi, hamonga ne sakami yono yetika mumo.
5 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kololeta livunde ligubice mu misi, mwi'tunda lya Sayuni, hamonga na mucanyha ya wose wono wowa wehangulize he 'tunda, hamonga ne lyosi, no wung'alale we malambi go moto ku cilo. Nawo u lukumyo lo Mulungu, wolomema no kulidima likaya.
6 Nalo lukumyo, wololipela likaya mubeho so lizuwa lya mu misi, no kulinoza hawe hanhu hono hapolile, hono howa hacitiywe mvula ne mbeho yo mukukunzo.


Isaya 5

Lwimbo lo mugunda we mizabibu

1 Ale, muhulicize ta, nimwimbilenyi lwimbo aluno,

u lwimbo lo mwendece wangu, no mugunda wakwe we mizabibu.

Mwendece wangu, yali yalimile mugunda we mizabibu,

mucanyha ye cigongo cono cina moso mwinji.

2 Yawutipanga muhangha, yakawusa mabwe gose,

yakahadika mizabibu miswanu muno.

Yakazenga ne cilingo co kwamilila,

yakahimba ne likombo lyo kukobonjela zi zabibu.

Yakalindila sunga mu wupilo we zi zabibu.

Ninga zabibu zose zikawa nghali.

3 Alu mwendece wangu yakutya,

“Nye nyo wanhu wono mwikaye mu Yelusalemi, na Yuda,

ta, mucilamulaje nene no mugunda wangu we mizabibu.

4 Wulenyi, ho yili nghani yinji yono yisigaye,

yono si nayinoza mu mugunda wangu we mizabibu?

Alu nhawule si wulelile zabibu ziswanu,

zono nali nanjiliye,

langa wukulela zabibu nghali, zono si nali nanjiliye?”

5 Alu, lecenyi ta nimulonjelenyi, nane vyono nowunoza,

“Nowupambula mululu wayo,

nigamwagule ne majinji gono gakuwa kuwucitila,

nigalecele malimu ga mu mbago,

gaze gawulye no kuwusambanjilanga.

6 Nowuleka wutite ne masukuzi, hamonga ne maselegwa gayo.

Hamba si nowupanyulila yaye ne mizabibu yayo,

hamba si nowulimila.

Langa nowuleka du vititane ne vikungugu hamonga ne mabijili.

Kanyhi nolajiliza mavunde,

galece kuwutonyezeza mvula.”

7 Ale, wanhu wa Isilayeli, wo mugunda we mizabibu

wo Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulungu we mihanga.

Nawo i wanhu wa Yuda, zo zabibu zono yazihadika.

Nayo yali yetumbiye, nha vyono wokwituma zo wuhile wakwe.

Ninga wene wakawa wawulaji.

Yali yetumbiye nha vyono wokwituma ze ling'ani,

ninga wanhu wakawa wakulila

vyono si wakutendelwa ze ling'ani.

I zimbi zono wanhu wakwituma

8 Woyi he kuli'nye!

Anye mono mugulaga zikaya ne migunda,

muwa kwiyonjelezeza muli vyono mwina navyo.

Sunga nhondo hawa hasina hamba cilejeho

co kwikala munhu yunji, nanye muwa kwikala muli wene du mu'si.

9 Ninga ane hulika Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu Mudaha vyose yakutya,

“Makaya gaga maswanu mawaha,

gose golowa matongo gali wene gasina wekazi.

10 Ne mizabibu, yono mihadice mu wugunda wa eka yikumi,

wololavya lita munana du za divayi.

Na mono kohadika kilo yigana limonga za mbeyu,

yuyo kolodudula kilo yikumi du za wuhemba.”

11 Woyi he, kuli nye!

Mono mwendile kwilawa hakali mitondo,

muwa kutyatya zo wugalo 'jimbi,

mono mugonaga cilo cose, muli mu wugalo 'jimbi.

12 Na mu zi sikukuu zenyu, mwendile kutowa masumbi,

ne zindonondo, ne misesele, ne fuluti,

hamonga no kung'wa divayi.

Ninga hamba si mwendile kwihwesa ze metumo go Muwaha Mulungu

hamba kuyimanya i milimo ye miwoko yakwe.

13 Holeka ku soko yiyo,

mosolwa mutegulwe ku yisi yo kutali so wuleka kumanya 'ne.

Ne walongola wenyu wolofwa so nzala,

ne wanhu wanji wose, wolofwa so nyhilu ye malenga.

14 Hambi i yinze ye wafu, nha yiwadabuciye wene,

nayo yahame mulomo wayo.

Nayo yowamela i wanhu we lilumbo wa mu Yelusalemi,

hamonga ne lipuga liwaha lye wanhu wayo,

wono wayisangalalilaga Yelusalemi.

15 Ne wanhu wose wolokozwa cevu,

ne wewoni wose wolonyetywa.

16 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu, u Mudaha vyose;

u Mulungu we mihanga kokumywa

hambi nha kawulajilaga wuwaha wakwe,

muli ze ling'ani zono ketumaga,

kanyhi kegubulaga zo wele wakwe,

mu nzila yo kuwaling'aniza wanhu wakwe.

17 Nazo zinyana ze zingholo, hamonga ne zinyhinda,

hamonga ne zinyehe, zolotuga wudimilo we mahanze

mumo mu matongo ge makaya gago.

18 Woyi hee, kuli'nye!

Mono si mukusuma kwileka no wubi wenyu.

19 Hambi nha mutezaga,

“Ale, Muwaha Mulungu,

na yetume mbela zono katezaga kokwituma,

nase ho ciziwone.

Lecenyi Mulungu mono yelile we Waisilayeli,

yetume za mu mahweso gakwe.

Mulece ciziwone zono zili mu mutima wakwe.”

20 Woyi hee, kuli'nye!

Hambi kuli'nye ziswanu nha zo 'mbi,

nazo zimbi nha zo swanu kuli 'nye.

Muwunozaga wutitu, wuwa yo misi kuli'nye,

nayo misi, ciwa co cilo kuli'nye.

Na kanyhi munozaga yono nghali,

kuli'nye yiwa yo yili muzinzi,

na yono yili muzinzi,

kuli'nye yiwa yo nghali,

21 Woyi hee, kuli'nye!

Hambi nha mwihwesaga vyono muli wanhu we mahala,

kanyhi muli wasugu muno.

22 Woyi hee, kuli'nye!

Mo wang'wela wo wujimbi!

Anye mo wanghula,

mono musina hamba woga bwete,

wo kuhanza-hanza ving'wegwa!

23 Na kanyhi so wulya mafupo wenyu,

mukuwa kuwawopolela wanhu we masutya,

no kuwemila ling'ani yawo i wanhu wono wasina masutya.

24 Na ku soko yiyo, nha vyono lilambi lyo moto,

ligatakamizaga mahanze,

na kanyhi, nha vyono mahanze gono ganyalile,

gatakamilaga mu malambi go moto,

ale, nhavivyo, ne lisina lyenyu nalyo lyowola,

ne maluwa gayo nago gonyala,

gapemheluswe ne mbeho,

hambi nha mwazilema zo wulajilize wo

Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha vyose,

u Mulungu we mihanga,

u Mulungu mono yelile we Waisilayeli,

zono yacijiza 'se.

25 Alu u Muwaha Mulungu,

yakondya kuwakolelwa likuwo wanhu,

wakwe, nalu yagoloye muwoko wakwe ho yawalipicize.

Matunda golosinjisika, ne miwili ya wono wafwiye,

yololekwa yipinile mu zinzila nha takataka.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo, i likuwo lyo

Muwaha Mulungu, hamba si lyolomalika yaye,

u muwoko wakwe vyo wowa wugoloywe du,

ho yajendelele kulavya wulipicizwe.

26 Muwaha Mulungu kolozicemeleza ne zinghungugo zo kutali.

Kolowacema walawe malanga gose ge'nze,

nawo wolokuza musunho kuli mwene.

27 Na holowa hasina hamba monga mono kolokatala,

hamba kwikobala.

Hamba hasina mono kolosinzila hamba kugona nhongo.

Hamba mono musipi wakwe wolohogoha,

hamba cilatu cakwe kutaguka.

28 Ne zisonga zawo nghali muno, no wupinde wawo,

wolowa wutawiciywe lukulu,

wawuwikanize so wugume du.

Ne magaga ge zifwalasi zawo,

makavu gehwanile libwe lye ng'anga.

Ne matuka gawo go wulugu, matayili gayo,

gowa kupilima nhe civulavumbi.

29 Ne wasilikale wawo,

wowa kukoloma nha simba zono ziwulaje mhelembo,

alu ziyidinghuye, zikulajila nha vyono,

hasina munhu wo wose,

mono kosuma kuziboka yaye.

30 Ne lizuwa lilyo, kolo lyeza kuza,

wolowakolomela Waisilayeli,

nhe malenga ge lilenga vyono gabulaga.

Na yunji hono yayilanje yisi, na langata,

kolowona citi ne magayo!

Wuzelu wakondya kumelwa no wutitu.


Isaya 6

Mulungu yakumucema Isaya yawe Muwizi

1 Mu mwaka, lono yafwila mutemi Uziya, ane nghamuwona Muwaha Mulungu, yali yekaye mwi'goda lyakwe lyo Wuwaha lya mucanyha, yali yali mwi'lumbo no lukumyo. Ne vivwalo vyakwe vyo wutemi, vyali, vitandaye hose mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
2 Kanyhi hali howali Wamalayika wono wali wemile wamupilime. Ne Wamalayika wawo, wose wali wena mabawa mutandatu-mutandatu. Mabawa mejete wegubikaga wusu, ne mejete wegubikaga i miwili yawo, na ganji mejete go wagulucilaga.
3 Ne Wamalayika wawo wakawa wakwimba wakwiboceza, wakutya,

“Welile, welile, welile!

Go Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.

Ne yinze yose nha yimemile nhojo yako.”

4 No wimbe we mazi gawo, wukawusinjisa no musinji we Nyumba yo Mulungu. Ne Nyumba yo Mulungu yikamema lyosi.
5 Nane hobaho nghawa kutya, “Woyi hee! Nha najilila! Hambi nghani zose zono nongaga mu mulomo wangu, nha ziwezaga 'mbi du, nane cikalane hamonga ne wanhu wono wekalaga wakulonga makani mabi du. Ninga nane luno ho nakondya kumuwona Mutemi, u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga!”
6 Hobaho yumonga wa muli wamalayika wa walya, yakaguluka, yakawa kunyiziza, akuno yebate no mwibato mu muwoko wakwe, likala lyono lyakaga moto, lyono yalisola mu cikumbicilo.
7 Yakamhamiza mu mulomo wangu ne likala lilyo, yakatya, “Alu vyono likala lili lyakupamiza mu mulomo wako, hodu wakondya kuwusizwa masutya gako, kanyhi wanenelwa no wubi wako.”
8 Hanyuma ya zizo, nghahulika Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Alu, nomutuma nani? Hamba nani mono kobita ku soko yetu?” Nane nghamupitula nghatya, “Nili baha! Wunhumaje nene.”

9 Nayo yakanyitya,

“Ale wubite, wukawalonjele wanhu wutye,

‘Hamba muhulicize muno, ninga si mokwizuka yaye.

Na hamba mulanje muno,

ninga si mosuma kuzimanya zono zikwijela yaye.’ ”

10 Hanyuma kanyhi yakanyitya,

“Wuyinozaje mitima ye wanhu wawa yiwe mizito,

ne makutu gawo gawe ciziwi,

ne meso gawo gawe cibofu, ho walece kuwona,

hamba kuhulika, hamba kwizuka kose.

Hambi hono wawone, wahulice,

no kwizuka, nha wombuyila niwaponye.”

11 Nane nghawuza nghatya,

“Gwe Muwaha Mulungu, alu zizi zokwijela nili?”

Nayo yakapitula yakanyitya,

“Zizino zokuza kolo makaya gose gekala matongo gasina wanhu,

sunga kolo zikaya zekala bwete,

ne yisi yalekwa wuciwa wuwaha.

12 Nane nowahamya wanhu wose niwategule kutali muno,

niyilece yisi yose nyhiwa yisina wanhu.

13 Hamba yiwe mu wanhu yikumi, yikumi,

yasigale munhu monga monga, mu yisi,

yuyo nayo kokongomezwa du.

Kokwihwana lisici lyo mubici wo munghola lyono lyakondya kutemangwa.”

(Lisici likuwa nha wo mwamulizo mupya we wanhu wo Mulungu.)


Isaya 7

Mulungu yakumusindamika Mutemi Ahazi

1 Kukawa mu vilo vya Ahazi u mwana wa Yotamu, kanyhi u mwizukulu wa Uziya, lono yali yatemile mu Yuda, wulugu wukaza. Lezini u mutemi wa Siliya, na Peka u mwana wa Lemaliya u mutemi wa Isilayeli, watemi wawa, wakayihoma Yelusalemi, ninga si wasuma kuyiteka yaye.
2 Nayo Ahazi mutemi wa Yuda, yali yeza kuhulika nha vyono walugu walawa Siliya, nawo wakondya kwinjila mu yisi ya Isilayeli, hodu mwene Ahazi, hamonga ne wanhu wakwe, wakawa kutuga woga wuwaha muno, wakawa kwanza kucicima kotya mibici yono yikuputwa ne libeho.

3 Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kumulonjela Isaya, yakamutya, “Alu wumusolaje Sheyali-yashubu u mwanago mubitane nayo, kuli mutemi Ahazi. Momwagana Ahazi ha nzila hono ho wali i wahona myenda, ha ndumilizo ye likololozi lyono liletaga malenga ku wulawa ku cilamba ca ha ka canyha.
4 Wukamulonjele vyono yetuze du, hamba yalece kuwa na woga, hamba kuzilika mu mutima wakwe. Hambi likuwo lyo mutemi Lezini ne wanhu wakwe wa Siliya, hamonga na wo mutemi Peka, nha gasina mbali yo yose, hambi nha wenha vizinga vyejete vyono vikufungha lyosi du.
5 Hambi Siliya hamonga na Isilayeli no mutemi wawo, nha wanoza maheho.
6 Hambi nha wakuhila wayisole Yuda, wawogofye wanhu, ho wamutemye u mwana wa Tabeli.

7 “Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nghumulonjelenyi nha vyono zizi si zokwijelela lukulu.

8 Ku sokoci?

Yili vilungo yicize Damesiki,

na vyono likaya lyayo liwaha hamonga ne Damesiki yeneco,

nha yisina vilungo yicize mutemi Lezini.

Nayo Isilayeli mu myaka makumi mutandatu ne 'hano,

yowa yakondya kupondangwa yilece kuwa yikungugo.

9 Hamba nhavivyo,

nayo Isilayeli nha si yili vilungo yicize Samaliya,

ne likaya liwaha lya Samaliya,

hamonga ne Samaliya yeneco,

nayo si yili vilungo yicize mutemi Peka.

“Holeka hono wulece kwihuwila,

mbeka hamba si kosuma kusikamywa.”

Citanjilo ca Imanuweli

10 Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela kanyhi Ahazi yakatya,
11 “Ale, wumulombaje Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wako, yakulajile citanjilo Ne citanjilo cico, cosuma kwijela ku wulawa kusi muno, ku yisi ye wafu, hamba kucanyha muno ku wulanga.”
12 Nayo mutemi Ahazi yakapitula yakatya, “Aa, ng'o! Ane si nosaka wundajile citanjilo, hambi nha si nghwenda nimujele Muwaha Mulungu.”
13 Ku soko yiyo, nayo Isaya yakapitula yakatya, “Ale, hulicizenyi ta anye mo mhuga yo mutemi Dawudi. Yo nghani 'mbi muno kuli'nye lono mukuwuhodanga wukangalizi we wanhu, alu mukwihwesa mosuma kuwuwusa no wukangalizi wo Mulungu hongo?
14 Ale, du, ninga Muwaha Mulungu mweneco komupelenyi citanjilo. U muhinza kodinghula 'nda, nayo kolela mwana wa cilume, ne litagwa lyakwe kocemwa ‘Imanuweli.’ ”
15 Na mu vilo vyono mwana yuyo kolowa yawahapa, no kumanya kuhagula zimbi ne ziswanu, i wanhu lulo wolowa kung'wa mele, no kulya wuci.
16 Ninga hamba na lono vilo vivyo vikali nafika, i zi'si ze watemi wawo wejete Lezini na Peka, wono wakukogofya, zolowa zakondya kulekwa zili bwete.
17 Ale, alu Muwaha Mulungu kokuletela gwegwe ne wanhu wako, hamonga ne we mhuga yo wutemi vilo vye magazo mawaha muno, kuciza magazo gose gono gamuzizenyi, ku wulawa lono wutemi wa Isilayeli wetemanulila mu wutemi wa Yuda. Ne magazo gago, kologaleta Lezini mutemi wa Ashuli.

18 “Ne vilo vivyo kolo vikuza, Muwaha Mulungu kolowacemeleza wanhu wa Misili, waze wenji muno nha mahazi, ku wulawa kutali mu zimhelo ze likolongo lya Nile, hamonga ne wanhu wa Ashuli, nawo waze wenji muno nhe zinzuci.
19 Wolomata mu malolo, na mu zimhango na mu mabwe, nawo wolomema hanhu hose mu vititika vye mamivya, sunga na mu wudimilo we zi mitugo.”
20 “Ne vilo vivyo kolo vikuza, Muwaha Mulungu, kolomutuma muceti we zimvwili ku wulawa ku nhumbi ye likolongo lya Efulata, u mutemi wa Ashuli! Nayo kolomucetenyi zindevu zenyu, ne zimvwili za mu mitwe yenyu, hamonga no wembe wa mu miwili yenyu.
21 Na mu vilo vivyo kolo vikuza, hamba no mulimi, mono kowa yena ng'ombe ndodo monga, hamonga na mhene nyejete du,
22 kolowa kutuga mele menji du muli zizo, munghango nha vyono kowa kusaka. Na mbeka, wawo wono wolosigala womi mu'si, wolowa kutuga mele menji du, hamonga no kulya wuci winji muno.”

23 “Na mu vilo vivyo kolo vikuza, migunda miswanu ye mizabibu, yono yina mizabibu elufu monga mugati yayo, yono yina cifo ca fweza elufu monga, yolowa yisopile masukuzi gose hamonga ne mabijili.
24 Ne wanhu wolowa kubita wena ne mapinde gawo ne zisonga, wawa kupelemba mumo. Na mbeka yisi yose yolomema mabijili du, hamonga ne mbago ye mamivya.
25 Na mu vigongo mose mono mwalimwagwa zindigwa, molocindika mabici ge mamivya, gono munhu si kosuma kwinjila. Langa wolowa wudimo wa ng'ombe du ne zingholo.”


Isaya 8

U mwana wa Isaya, yakuwa co citanjilo kuli wanhu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Wusole mbawo ngazi wandice mumo helufi mbaha zono zikutya, ‘WUTECE MBELA MBELA, WUPUMHE MUSUNHO.’
2 Ninga wusole wanhu wehuwilwa wejete, u mukohani Uliya, hamonga na Zekaliya mwana wa Yebelekiya, wawe wahomelezi.”
3 Ninga hanyuma mugolece wangu yakatuga 'nda. No mwana wetu we cilume yali yeza kulewa, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Wumucemelaje mwana litagwa lya ‘Wutece Mbela mbela, Wupumhe Musunho.’
4 No mwana yuyo lono kowa yakali nawiluka no kumanya kucema ‘Yaya’ na ‘Baba’ I sawo yose ya Damesiki, hamonga ne mateka ga Samaliya, vyosolwa no kutegulwa kuli mutemi wa Ashuli.”

Mutemi wa Ashuli yakuza

5 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi.
6 Yakatya, “Alu i wanhu wawa vyono wagalema malenga ga Shilo gono gavwilaga mhola mhola, langa wamucicimilaga mutemi Lezini, no mutemi Peka.
7 Holeka nane no Muwaha Mulungu, aluno nomuleka mutemi wa Ashuli yaze hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe wo wulugu, wayihome Yuda. Nawo wohuluma nhe lihwazi lye malenga ge likolongo lya Efulata, nha vyono galapukaga.
8 Nayo kohuluma no kufika sunga ko ku Yuda, kokola mose, koyigubika yisi yako yose 'gwe go Imanuweli. Ninga Mulungu yali hamonga nase! Mwene kogatandakula mabawa gakwe yayicitile yisi.”

U wiwikanize we bwete we wanhu

9 Holeka mwikongholelaje hanhu hamonga ko woga,

anye nyo makungugo!

Anye nanye mono muli kutali ku zimhelo ze yinze, muhulicize.

Alu, mwiwikanizaje so wihomi,

ninga mweze no woga!

Heya mwiwikanizaje, ninga mweze no woga!

10 Mwihehaje!

Ninga hamba si mokwambulila cinji yaye.

Anye mulonje du mu nghango yenyu!

Ninga yowa nghani ya bwete du.

Hambi ase, Mulungu nha yali hamonga nase.

Muwaha Mulungu yakumusugula Muwizi

11 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakasugula kwe vilungo vyakwe viwaha, nha vyono ndece kutyatya nzila yono wanhu wayikolile. Yakanyitya,
12 “Wulece kwihanza mu mahweso ge wanhu, hamba wulece kuvyogopa vinhu vyono wene wavyogopile.
13 Weze kukumbucila, vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu we mihanga, nelile, ane yo nene nili wene du, mono wunojeye wumhele cogopo.
14 Na ku soko ye cogopo co wele wangu, ane nowa i hanhu ho wutizizo, kanyhi libwe lyono wanhu wokwikobala, kanyhi nowa litalawe lyono lyowagwiza i wanhu wa mu wutemi wa Yuda na Isilayeli, kanyhi nowa mutego wo kuwagwiza ne wanhu wa Yelusalemi.
15 Na wenji wokwikobala, wogwa no kubenanjika. Wokwibatwa mu mutego.”

Suguzi kuli zo wuwuza wafu

16 Nye nyo wejizwa wangu, mulanjilile, muzikamise muno zinghani zono mwejizwa, zono Mulungu yandajiliza.
17 Ninga hamba yiwe, Muwaha Mulungu yehundice ho yalece kuwonwa ne wanhu wakwe, ninga ane nomwitumbila du, no kuwika witumbilo wangu muli mwene.
18 Nane 'lu, nili bahano hamonga ne wana wono Muwaha Mulungu yamhela, Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga, mono ligoda lyakwe lyo wutemi, lili mwi'tunda lya Sayuni. Nayo kacituma ase ciwa nha vitanjilo ne mezino kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli.
19 Ninga hono wanhu watye, mubite mu waganga muketoweleze visangu, na kuli wahawi wono wapwepwaga, wakulonga ne lizi yidodo. Wamutyeni “I wanhu yiwanojeye wawuze zinghani zawo kuli wafu ku soko ye womi.”
20 Nanye muwapitule muwe kutya, “Ciba muzihulice zono Muwaha Mulungu yakumwijizenyi! Mulece kuwahulika i waganga wo wulambi. Hambi zizo zono wakumulonjelenyi, nha sizo-mutazenyi cinji yaye.”

Lipango lye Magazo

21 Nawo wanhu wolowa kuyumba-yumba mu yisi, wakugaya, kanyhi wadabuce. Na mumo mu wudabuce wawo, likuwo lyowakola, hanyuma wawe kumulijita mutemi wawo, hamonga no Mulungu wawo. Wosuma kugalamila ku wulanga,
22 hamba woyilanga yisi yose, ninga hamba si wowona cinji, langa wowona wugaye, no wutitu. No wutitu wuwo wukogofya muno, nawo mumo mo wogumilwa.


Isaya 9

1 Nawo hamba si wolotuga nzila yo yose yono yolowataza ho weponye mu magazo gago.

Mutemi mono kolotema hanyuma

Mwamulizo yisi ya Zabuloni, hamonga ne yisi ya Nafutali, Mulungu yazikoza cevu. Ninga hanyuma, kolo ziletela lilumbo zisi zizo, ku wulawilila kwi'lenga lya Meditelaneya ku lubavu lwa kucilima, sunga ko ku yisi yono yili ku nhumbi ye likolongo lya Yoludani, sunga ko ku Galilaya kono wekalaga i wanhu wono si Wayahudi.

2 I wanhu wono wajendelaga mu wutitu wawona wuzelu wuwaha,

wali wekaye mu yisi ye citi ciwaha ninga lu,

wuzelu wuwabulilila.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kuwonjeleza nyemo yawo,

kuwapela wusangalale wuwaha.

Wakusangalalila muli zono wuwatendeye,

nha vyono wanhu wasangalalaga,

lono wali mu nhwanga ye ndudula yo wuhemba wawo,

hamba lono wakugawana sawo yono wayitecile.

4 Hambi nha wawubenanga lubano lo mutegulo wawo,

hamonga ne nghome yono yawatowaga mu mawega gawo.

Hambi nha walisuma likungugo lyono lyawewonelaga

no kuwatendela kwe vilungo wanhu wako,

nha vyono walisuma katali lipuga lye wasilikale

we Wamidiyani wo wulugu.

5 Hambi i malatu ge wasilikale vitung'ati mu milewo

hamonga ne myenda yawo yono milowece mu sakami,

nha yotakamizwa yose mu moto.

6 Hambi nha calelelwa mwana!

Capegwa mwana wa cilume!

Nayo yo kowa yo mutemi wetu.

Ne litagwa lyakwe kocemwa,

“Mwijizi wo Wizine, Mulungu mono yali Vilungo.”

“U sogo yetu we Cibitilila, Mutemi wo Mulamu.”

7 No wuwaha wo wutemi wakwe,

wojendelela kuwiluka no wutemi wakwe,

wowa kwikala wa mulamu nhondo zose.

Mwene kolohala u wutemi wose wo mutemi Dawudi,

ho no kutema mu wutemi wakwe,

kuwusikamya no kuwukamisa mu wiling'ano

ne ling'ani ku wulawa lulu, sunga ne cibitilila.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

kohidimbala sunga yazitume zose zizi.

Muwaha Mulungu, koyilipiciza Isilayeli

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakondya kulonga

nha vyono kowuling'aniza wutemi wa Isilayeli,

mu lulelo lwa Yakobo.

9 Ne wanhu wose wa Isilayeli,

wono wekaye mu Samaliya,

womanya vyono Muwaha Mulungu yo yetumile zizi.

Naluno wakwilumba no kwidunhumula, akuno wakutya,

10 “I matofali gose ge mazengo,

gakondya kugwa hasi,

ninga nase cogazenga kanyhi majinji ge mabwe!

I micici ye mibici ye mikuyu nha yatemangwa,

ninga nase cozenga ne mibici ye mihagata.”

11 Holeka Muwaha Mulungu kowakulujiza walugu wawo wawahome.

12 Nawo Waashuli kono lubavu lwa kucilima,

ne Wafilisiti kumwezi,

wose wahame milomo yawo,

wawamele Waisilayeli.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

si linadumuka yaye,

nayo vyo yakali yagoloye du muwoko wakwe ho yalipicize.

13 Nawo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

wakali du sinanena,

hamba yiwe Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakondya kuwalipiciza,

ninga hamba si wakumuwuyila ng'o.

14 Ninga mwi'zuwa linji,

u Muwaha Mulungu kolowalipiciza i walongola we Waisilayeli

hamonga ne wanhu wakwe.

Kolowadumula mutwe no mucila.

15 I wakombi ne wanhu watambule,

wawo wo mutwe.

Nawo mucila, wo wawizi,

wono wafundaga zo wulambi!

16 Nawo wono wakuwatonga i wanhu wawa,

wakuwajiliza nzila,

nawo wono wakulongozwa nawo nhavivyo wakwajiliza.

17 Holeka nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

hamba si kowaleka wazelelo wapone yaye.

Kanyhi hamba si kowawonela lusungu i wacekulu wafwile,

hamonga ne wana wono wasina walezi wawo.

Hambi i wanhu wose nha weza wahodanji kanyhi wabi.

Sunga na zose zono walongaga, vyo 'mbi du.

Hamba nhavivyo likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu si lyoduma yaye,

ninga muwoko wakwe wowa wugoloywe du ho yalipicize.

18 No wubi we wanhu, wunyanyaga nha moto,

wono wutakamizaga masanzu ne mamivya.

Wutakamizaga nha mbago yono yikolelezwe moto,

nawo wukwaka,

ne malambi gayo gapunaga kucanyha gepalinjile nhe 'sumbili lya lyosi.

19 Alu, na vyono Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakoligwe likuwo,

holeka no wulipicize wakwe wukutakamiza nha moto mu yisi yose,

no kuwongomeza wanhu.

No munhu wo wose kokwimanyilila mweneco.

20 Ne wanhu wose wa mu yisi yose munhu wo wose,

kosuma kukwepula no kulya cinhu co cose cono yakuwona,

soko du hono ciwe cikuwa kuligwa.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

si wakwiguta yaye.

Sunga nhondo wowa kulya sunga ne wana wawo.

21 Nawo i wanhu wa Manase na wa Efulayimu,

wokwihoma weneco wose.

Ninga kanyhi hanyuma,

wokwilunza wayihome Yuda.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu si lyoduma,

yakali yawugoloye muwoko wakwe du ho yalipicize.


Isaya 10

1 Woyi hee kuli'nye!

Mono si mukunoza ling'ani langa mukuwewonela wanhu wangu.

2 Nayo yo yikuleka mukuwewajiza waciwa,

mu zinghani zawo ze ling'ani,

ho walece kutuga ling'ani yawo.

Kanyhi na yiyi yo yikuleka mukuwasolela zisawo,

i wacekulu wono wafwiliywe ne walume zawo,

na ye wana wono wasina walezi wawo.

3 Hamba monozaci kolo Mulungu yakumulipicizenyi?

Hamba monoza nhawule,

kolo yakumuletelenyi magazo ku wulawa ku yisi yo kutali?

Hamba motiziza hayi hene mukalondole wutazi?

Hamba hayi hene hono mozihundika zisawo zenyu?

4 Hambi wose nha mowulajilwa ku wulugu,

hamba mosolwa nhe wafungwa.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

si lyoduma ng'o,

langa kojendelela kuwugolola muwoko wakwe ho yalipicize.

Mulungu yakumwitumamila Mutemi we Ashuli

5 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya,

“Gwe Ashuli!

Ashuli go nghome yangu yono nokwitumamila

ho niwalipicize wawo wono niwakoleywe likuwo.

6 Nowategula Waashuli wakalihome likungugo lyono lisina cihuliko,

wono wanghoza likuwo.

Nowategula wakasole vinhu mateka,

kanyhi wakahize no kuwakanyanjiza wanhu kotya nhope mu zinzila.”

7 Ninga mwene mutemi wa Ashuli,

nha yekondelezeze lukulu kuli zo wuwulaji mu mutima wakwe.

Holeka yewikanize ho yongomeze makungugo menji.

8 Kanyhi yakwilumba yakutya,

“I wawaha wose we wasilikale wangu wo wulugu, wose watemi!

9 Ane nagasuma makaya ga Kalino,

na Kalekemishi,

hamonga ne makaya ga Hamati, na Alipadi.

Kanyhi nghazisuma Samaliya na Damesiki.

10 Nghawugolola muwoko wangu

no kuwalipiciza wa mu wutemi wono wahembaga vihwani,

kanyhi vihwani vinji muno kuciza vya ku Yelusalemi na Samaliya.

11 Nghayongomeza Samaliya hamonga ne vihwani vyayo vyose.

Holeka na Yelusalemi nayo,

noyitendela nhavivyo,

hamonga ne vihwani vyono wanhu wakuvihemba kuko.”

12 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ninga nane kolo nakondya kuzinoza mwi'tunda lya Sayuni na mu Yelusalemi, nolomulipiciza u mutemi wa Ashuli ku soko yo widunhumuzi wakwe no wiwoni wakwe wose.”

13 Nayo mutemi wa Ashuli,

yakwilumba yakutya,

“Ane nha nazituma zizo zose

kwe vilungo vyangu nene no mweneco.

Ane nili vilungo,

kanyhi nina mahala, kanyhi nili musugu.

Hambi nha nayiwusiza lukulu mipaka haciligati ye makungugo,

nghasola sunga ne vinhu vyawo

vyono wali wewutiye mu viwutwa vyawo.

Nane nghawa nha nghambaku,

nghawakanyanjiza i wanhu wono wekalaga mumo.

14 Ne makungugo gose ga mono'nze,

gakawa nha manhuli ga ndeje,

nghakongholeza zisawo zose mu nzila mhelu du,

kotya munhu mono yakutondola magangha.

Hamba hasina mono yagolola libawa,

hamba mono yanyhenjela lwanji!”

15 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Mukwihwesa nha vyono hongo nhemo,

yosuma kumulonjela munya nhemo,

yiwe kutya nha vyono yene yo yina lilumbo,

yicizize munya nhemo?

Hamba musumeno ma,

wo wina lilumbo wumucizize mono yakuwitumamila?

Sunga ne nhumbi yo si yikuwa kumugumila munhu,

langa munhu yo kayigumilaga nhumbi.”

16 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

koleta nhamwa yawalipicize wose wono wajinile.

Nawo wotuga wusungu wukali muno mu miwili yawo.

17 U Mulungu mono wo wuzelu we Waisilayeli, kolowa moto,

u Mulungu mono yelile we Waisilayeli,

kolowa malambi ga moto,

mono kolovitakamiza vyose mwi'zuwa limonga,

hamonga ne mamivya ne mabijili.

18 Nayo mbago ye nhojo,

hamonga ne migunda miswanu,

vyose vyolohodanjilwa lukulu,

nha vyono litamwa libi, limuhodangaga munhu.

19 Ne mibici yayo ya mu mbago yono yolosigala,

yolowa micefu muno muno,

yono sunga no mwana mudodo kosuma kuyiwala.

Wuwuye wa wono wowa wasigaye

20 Ne vilo vikuza, vyono i wanhu wa Isilayeli wono wolopona, si wololyegamila kanyhi likungugo lyono lyamulipicizenyi ng'o. Langa wolomwitumbila Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli.
21 Ne wanhu wacefu wa Isilayeli, wolomuwuyila Mulungu we vilungo.
22 Hamba yiwe lulo i wanhu wa Isilayeli wolowa wonjezeka wawa wenji muno nho musawawa wa mwi'lenga, ninga wacefu wolowuya. No wongomezwe wuwikanizwe so wanhu, nawo wuli baho du.
23 Na mbeka u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu we mihanga kolo wuleta wongomezwe du nha vyono yatemya mweneco.

Muwaha Mulungu koyilipiciza Ashuli

24 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakuwalonjela i wanhu wono wekaye ku Sayuni, yakutya, “Ale, mulece kuwogopa Waashuli, hamba wawe wamwiwonelenyi 'nye, nha vyono Wamisili wamwiwonelenyi.
25 Hambi nha cikali cipango cidodo du ane noleka kumulipicizenyi, hanyuma niwapitucile nawo niwongomeze.
26 Ane no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, nowakunanga no mukotya wangu, nha vyono nawakunanga i wanhu wa ku Midiyani he'bwe lya Olebu. Nowalipiciza Waashuli, nha vyono nayilipiciza Misili.
27 Ne vilo vivyo kolo vyaza, nomuwusenyi mu vilungo vye Waashuli. No lubano lwawo, si wolowa mutegulo kanyhi mu mawega genyu.”

Walugu wakwinjila

28 I walugu wali wakondya kulisola likaya lya Ai!

Wakawa wakolela mu Migiloni!

Wakavileka vinhu vyawo ha Mikimashi!

29 Wakalowoka cilowoko,

wakagona ha Geba!

I wanhu we cikaya ca Lama wenjilwa no woga wuwaha.

Nawo wanhu wono wekalaga mu Gibeya cikaya co mutemi Sawuli,

wakondya kutiza wose.

30 Ale muzoze anye nyo wanhu wa Galimu!

Nanye nyo wanhu wa Layisha, muhulicize!

Nanye nyo wanhu wa Anatoti, mupitule!

31 Ale, mulanje i wanhu wa ku Madimena

na wa ku Gebimu weza kutiza, ho weponye.

32 Ne zuwali, walugu wakondya kwinjila mu cikaya ca Nobu,

na kuko wakwikamanga zingumi zawo mwi'tunda lya Sayuni,

mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi.

33 U Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

kowaleka webamilize hasi nhe manhambi gono gakudumulwa mwibici.

U wiwoni no widunhumuzi wawo,

wowuswa, nawo wonyetywa.

34 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

kowadumula wagwe nhe mibici,

yono yili haciligati ye mbago,

vyono yikuwa kutemangwa ne nhemo yiwa kugwa hasi,

nha vyono ne mibici miswanu ya Lebanoni yose yogwa!


Isaya 11

Wutemi wo Mulamu

1 U wityatye we ndelo ye citemi mu mhuga ya Dawudi,

wihwanile no mubici wono wadumulwa,

ninga lisina lyayo, likutula kanyhi manhambi ganji.

Holeka mutemi mupya kololelwa muli wawo i wana wa Dawudi.

2 Nayo muhe wo Muwaha Mulungu kolomupela mahala,

hamonga no wizuce no wunghula

wo kuwatemela wanhu wakwe.

Nayo kolowumanya wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu

kanyhi kowa ne cogopo kuli mwene.

3 Nayo kowona wendezwe muno mu cihuliko ca muli mwene.

Hamba no wuling'anizi wakwe,

sikoling'aniza so zinghani zono kolowona ne meso,

hamba zono kolohulika kwe makutu gakwe.

4 Langa kolowaling'aniza wasocele ko wiling'ano,

no kulamula nghani yo muciwa kwe ling'ani.

Nawo wanhu wolowa kulipicizizwa mu wulajilize wakwe,

ne wanhu wetuma zimbi wolowulagwa.

5 No wiling'ano wolowa musipi wa mu ciwino cakwe,

no wihuwilwa wo musipi wo kwiwopela.

6 Nago maminzi ne zingholo,

vyolokwikalana hamonga ko mulamu,

nago masuwi, golowa gagonile hamonga ne zinyana ze zimhene,

nazo zindama ne mana ge simba, zolodima hamonga.

Ne wana wadodo, wo wolowa wakuzidima.

7 Zing'ombe nazo, ne madubu, zolodima hamonga,

nazo zinyehe ne mana ge dubu,

zolowa zikugona hamonga ko mulamu.

Nazo simba zololya mahanze nha ng'ombe.

8 Hamba mwana mugalika,

yedawale behi ne lizoka, lyono lina sumu nghali,

ninga si lyolomuluma yaye.

9 Na mwi'tunda lyo Mulungu lyono lyelile lya Sayuni,

molowa musina citamisi co cose hamba cibi co cose.

Hambi yisi nha yolomema wumanye wo Muwaha Mulungu

nha vyono lilenga limemile malenga kose.

I wanhu wono wahamywa wolowuya

10 Ne lizuwa lyolokuza, lono mutemi mupya, mono kololawa mu wityatye we mhuga ye citemi ya Dawudi, kolowa citanjilo kuli makungugo. Makungugo golokwihanguliziza hamonga kwi'kaya lyakwe lyo wutemi, no kumupela lilumbo.
11 Na yolowa mwizuwa lilyo, U Muwaha Mulungu kolokwitumamila kanyhi vilungo vyakwe, yawagomole wanhu wakwe wono wowa wakali wasigaye ku Ashuli, na ku Misili, na ku Patilosi, na ku Kushi, na ku Elamu, na ku Sinali, na ku Hamati, ne zi'si zose za ku zimhwani.

12 Muwaha Mulungu kolowenulila bendela makungugo,

ho yagalajile nha vyono, aluno nhondo,

yeza kuwakongholeza kanyhi hamonga,

i wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda,

wono wali weyagaye ako na ako,

yawagomole ku wulawa ku zimhelo ze yinze zose vyono yine.

13 No wutemi we Waisilayeli si wolowuwonela wivu

kanyhi u wutemi wa Yuda.

No wutemi wa Yuda nawo si wolowa mulugu

kanyhi wo wutemi wa Isilayeli.

14 Langa wene wolokwilunza hamonga,

wabite wakayihome yisi ye Wafilisiti wa ku lubavu lwa kumwezi,

wawatece ne wanhu wono wekaye ku cilima.

Kanyhi wolowasuma i wanhu wa ku Edomu, na Mowabu.

Nawo i wanhu wono wekaye mu yisi ya Amoni,

wolowa wena cihuliko kuli wene.

15 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

kololinyaza lilenga lya Shamu,

kololeta mbeho yono yili moto,

yalinyaze likolongo lya Efulata,

ninga kololeka vikolongo vidodo-dodo vili mupunghati,

munhu wo wose ho yasume kulowoka hakavu.

16 Kanyhi holowa na nzila mbaha yono yilawiliye ku Ashuli,

yono wolowa kukola i wanhu wakwe Waisilayeli

wono wolowa wakali wasigaye kuko ku Misili.

Nha vyono yali yinha, lono i walezi wawo wawucila ku Misili.


Isaya 12

Lwimbo lo Wulumbe

1 Ne lizuwa lyolokuza lono wanhu wolokwimba wawe kutya,

“Nghukulumba 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Hamba wuwe wali wungholeywe likuwo,

ninga aluno wusindamice,

wulece kungholelwa kanyhi likuwo.

2 Mulungu yo mhonelo yangu nomwitumbila mwene,

hamba si nokogopa cinji yaye.

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo kamhelaga 'ne vilungo no wusume,

kanyhi yo muponyi wangu.

3 Ne wanhu wo Mulungu wasangalalaga lono yawaponya,

nha vyono malenga ge mbeho gamusangalazaga

mono yena nyhilu mwizuwa likali.”

4 Ne lizuwa lyolokuza lono wanhu wolokwimba wawe kutya,

“Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

Mucemelezenyi yamutazenyi!

Mugalonjele makungugo gose ze metumo gakwe!

Muwalonjele zo wuwaha wakwe!

5 Mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu

so metumo gakwe mawaha gono yetumile.

Ne wanhu wose wazihulice zo lukumyo lwakwe.

6 Muwalekaje wose wono wekaye mu Sayuni,

wembe ne mazi mawaha wawe kutya,

Mulungu mono yelile we Waisilayeli, yo muwaha,

kanyhi yekalane ne wanhu wakwe.”


Isaya 13

Mulungu koloyilipiciza Babiloni

1 Nazo azino, zo nghani za Babiloni,

zono Isaya yazibocela kulawa kuli Mulungu.

2 Ale, mutumbice bendela yo wulugu,

mucanyha ye litunda lyono si licindice mibici!

Muwaceme wasilikale mwinuye muno mazi genyu,

akuno mukuwayunziza miwoko yenyu,

ciwe co citanjilo cenyu,

co kuwalonjela nha vyono wagahome malango ge likaya lidunhumuzi.

3 Muwaha Mulungu kawalajiliza wanhu

wono wayisangalalilaga nhojo yakwe,

i wanhu we vilungo, ho wawahome,

no kuwalipiciza wawo wono yawakoleywe likuwo.

4 Ale, hulicizenyi ta i mazozo ge lipuga liwaha lye wanhu,

wono wehangulize mu matunda,

go mazi ge makungugo,

hamonga ne matemi gawo.

Muwaha Mulungu u muwaha we milewo,

yakuwikaniza mapuga gakwe ge wasilikale so milewo.

5 Wakulawilila mu ma'si go kutali ku mhelo ye yinze.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kokuza yayongomezeze yisi yose mwi'kuwo lyakwe.

6 Mulile muno!

Hambi lizuwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu nha lyasejelela,

lizuwa lyono u Mulungu Mudaha vyose,

koleta wongomezwe.

7 Holeka miwoko yose yotimba,

ne mitima ye wanhu wose yozizima.

8 Ne wanhu wose wolokogofywa,

no kwibatwa no wusungu,

nha wo mucekulu lono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

Kanyhi wowa kwilanga weneco wose ko woga wuwaha muno.

No wusu wawo wolowa wukununghaye so cevu.

9 Lizuwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu likuza,

lizuwa lyo wukali we likuwo lyakwe.

Mwi'zuwa lilyo, yinze yolonozwa yiwe mbago,

no kuwongomeza wanyo wubi wose.

10 Nazo zinyelezi hamonga ne vibuto vye zinyelezi,

si zolong'alala yaye.

Nalyo lizuwa lyolotukuwala kolo lyeza kusosomoka,

no mwezi nawo hamba si wolosana ng'o.

11 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Ane nololeta wongomele mono yinze,

nolowalipiciza ne wanhu wabi wose,

ku soko yo wubi wawo.

Nolowanyetya wose i wewoni,

nolowalipiciza wose i wedunhumuzi, ne wakali.

12 Nawo wono walasigala wolowa wacefu si nhe zahabu.

13 Nane mwi'zuwa lilyo nolowucicimya wulanga ne yinze,

nayo yolosinjisika yiwuce hanhu hayo,

lono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghulilajila likuwo lyangu.

14 “I wajenzi wono wekaye ku Babiloni,

wolomangha watizize ku zi'si zawo,

hambi nha wolowa weyagaye nhe zinhemanyika

zono zikutiza ho ziponye mu miwoko ye wapelembi.

Kanyhi nha kotya ngholo zono zisina mudimi.

15 No munhu wo wose mono kolokwibatwa, kolowulagwa.

16 Na kolo wakwijela vyono wasina wutazi wo wose,

hodu i wana wawo wagalika,

wolowa kutowezwa-towezwa sunga wafwe,

zikaya zawo zolosolwa,

ne wagolece zawo wolowa kwibatwa kwe vilungo.”

17 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Nawasagahiza likuwo Wamedi,

wabite wakayihome Babiloni.

Kanyhi wene hamba si wolosaka fweza hamba zahabu yaye.

18 Nawo wolowawulaga i wazelelo wawo ko wupinde ne zisonga zawo.

Hamba si wolowawonela lusungu yaye i wagalika hamba wana wadodo.

19 Heya, Babiloni, mbeka wo wutemi wuswanu muno,

kanyhi wulawiliciye mu matemi gose.

Nawo kanyhi wilumbilaga muli wanhu wayo.

Ninga ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nowongomeza nha vyono nayongomeza Sodoma na Gomola!

20 Kanyhi holowa hasina munhu hamba monga,

mono kolokwikala mumo.

Hamba ne Walabu i wayeji-yeji,

si wolokoma mahema gawo mumo yaye.

Hamba ne wadimi si wolodimila mumo kanyhi mitugo zawo.

21 Holowa heza hanhu hono malimu ga mu nyika golowa kwikala mumo.

Holowa hanhu hono matuwi gowa kuzenga manhuli gago mumo.

Ne nyacihema zolowa kwikala mumo,

ne zimhene za mu mbago nazo zolowa kwidawala mu matongo.

22 Nayo minala, hamonga ne makaya go wutemi,

golowa kubocelela mazi ge vililo vye zimbisi hamonga ne macewe.”

“Holeka cidaha co kuyongomeza Babiloni cakondya kuza!

Ne vilo vyawo vyeza malika.”


Isaya 14

Wuwuye ku wulawa ku wuhamywe

1 Ninga kanyhi Muwaha Mulungu, kolowawonela lusungu wanhu wakwe Waisilayeli, kolowahagula kanyhi wawe wanhu wakwe. Kolowagomola kanyhi wekale mu yisi yawo, sunga ne wajenzi wolokwinjila vikalane nawo.
2 Sunga ne makungugo menji, golowataziliza i wanhu wa Isilayeli, ho wawuye ku yisi yono Muwaha Mulungu yawapela, na hanyuma kanyhi i makungugo gago, golowa nha wawanda, golowetumamila i Waisilayeli. Na wawo wono wawateka Waisilayeli, alu nawo woletekwa ne Waisilayeli no kuwatema wene wono wali wawewoneye.

Mutemi wa Babiloni yali mu yisi ye wafu

3 Muwaha Mulungu kolowaceheza wanhu wa Isilayeli mu wusungu wawo hamonga no wugazwe, na mu wuhinyizwe wo wituma milimo mizito yono wakudindimizwa wetume.
4 Nayo kolo yakondya kuwanozeza zizo, ale nawo womusekanga no kumuzwajiza wawe kutya,

“Lanjenyi ta u mutemi mono yawakunhulangaga wanhu, alu yali hayi?

Yakondya kugwa!

Nalu, hamba si kolomukunhulanga yunji kanyhi!

5 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu

nha yakondya kuvidumya i vilungo vye watemi wabi,

6 wono wawakunhulangaga wanhu kwa nzizila,

no kujendelela kuwapela magayo i makungugo

gono wali wakondya kugasuma.

7 Ninga nhondo lu,

yinze yose yikusangalala vyono yatuga wutule,

hamonga no mulamu.

Ne wanhu wose wasangalala, wakwimba so nyemo.

8 Lanje, sunga ne mibici ye misonobali hamonga ne mibici ye mihagata,

yose yikuwusangalalila wugwe wo mutemi mono yakondya kugwa.

Vyono aluno nhondo,

hasina kanyhi munhu mono kowa kuyitemanga yaye!

9 “Nayo yisi ye wafu,

yikwiwikaniza yimubocele mutemi wa Babiloni.

Yikusinhuswa ne myuhe ya wose wono wali wena vilungo mono'nze.

I myuhe ye watemi yikuwuka mu magoda gayo go wutemi.

10 Ne myuhe yose yomulonjela yiwe kutya,

‘Alu nagwe wasocela cinya sese!

Wehwana na sese!

11 Wekalaga wukutogozwa ne mazi ge masumbi,

ninga 'lu lanje, wuli mu yisi ye wafu.

Ne zi semembe zolowa yo nyhingo yako,

nazo zolowa funicizwe no mutote we manyongololo.’

12 “Gwe go mutemi wa Babiloni,

go nyelezi yono ying'alalaga hakali mitondo,

alu hodu wagwiza ku wulawa ku wulanga!

Ninga katali ho wali wugasumize makungugo,

alu ninga wakondya kugwiswa sunga ho hasi.

13 Wukawa wina wuhile wo kubita kucanyha ku wulanga,

wukaliwice ligoda lyako lyo wutemi mucanyha ye zinyelezi

zono zili kucanyha muno muno.

Nagwe wehwesaga nha vyono kolowa wutemile na mu matunda

gono gali kusumuka kono milungu yikongholelaga.

14 Kanyhi wehwesaga nha vyono kobita

ko kucanyha ye mavunde wihwane na Mono Yalawiliciye Muli Vyose.

15 Ninga 'lu kumbe ho wategulwa ku'si muno muno ku yisi ye wafu.

16 “Nawo wono wokulanga,

wokukomalila ne meso, no kukulonga wakutya,

‘Mwene nye!

Mbeka yuyu yo munhu mono yayisinjisa yinze,

no kugacicimya ne matemi?

17 Mwene mbeka du,

yuyu yo munhu mono yagongomeza makaya,

no kuyinoza yinze yiwe nyika?

Yo yuyu mbeka mono si yasaka

hamba no kuwalecela wafungwa wakwe,

yawakombole wawuye ku zikwawo?’

18 Lanje!

Sunga ne watemi wose we yinze wazicigwe mu vitenjelo vyawo vye lilumbo,

19 ninga agwe, hamba citenjelo bwete wusina,

no muvimba wako wotagwa du wuwolele kunze.

Wowa wuli hamonga ne mivimba ye wasilikale

wono wawulajilwa ku wulugu,

nagwe kogumilwa hamonga nawo mwi'vwenjela lye mabwe,

no kukanyanjilwa.

20 Hambi agwe, nha wayihodanga yisi,

wukawawulaga ne wanhu wako.

Holeka nagwe hamba si kolozikwa nha

vyono watemi wanji wazikwaga ng'o.

Kanyhi na muli we mhuga yako wose i wabi,

hasina hamba monga mono kolopona.

21 Holeka luno, mwiwikanizaje, mongomeze!

Hambi i wana we cilume wo mutemi yuyu,

nha wanojeye wawulagwe,

ku soko yo wubi we walezi wawo.

Kanyhi howa hasina hamba monga wa muli wene,

mono kolotema mono'nze no kuzenga makaya.”

Mulungu koloyongomeza Babiloni

22 Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakutya, “Noyihoma Babiloni niyongomeze. Noyileka yili bwete, yisina hamba wana, hamba mono kowa mumi. Yo nene Muwaha mulungu vyo nghutya.
23 Nane noyinoza Babiloni yiwe malamba ga malenga, ne matuwi gawe kwikala mumo. Kanyhi noyifwagula Babiloni ne liteyu lyono lyovifwaguliza vinhu vyose no kuvyongomeza. Yo nene Muwaha Mulungu vyo nghutya.”

Mulungu koloyongomeza Ashuli

24 Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yelahiza yakutya,

“Mbeka, nha vyono nehwesa, na vyo yowa du.

Na zono nali mbikanize kuzituma, zokwitumwa du.

25 Nowongomeza Waashuli mu yisi yangu, ya Isilayeli,

kanyhi nowakanyanjila mu matunda gangu.

Nane nawawusiza wanhu wangu lubano lwe wanhu wa Ashuli,

hamonga no mutegulo muwaha wono wadinghuye.

26 Na gaga go mahweso gono nghuyihweseza yinze,

ngoloye no muwoko wangu ho niwaling'anize makungugo gose.”

27 Muwaha Mulungu yakondya kudumula nghani

nha vyono zizi zose zofisilizwa.

Mwene yawugoloye muwoko wakwe ho yalipicize.

Na hasina munhu wo wose mono kosuma kumwimila.

Mulungu koyongomeza yisi ye Wafilisiti

28 Azi nazo zo nghani zono zalongwa mu mwaka wono yafwila mutemi Ahazi.

29 Nye nyo Wafilisiti, mulece kusangalala,

mukutya hambi nghome yono mukatowelwa nha yabeneka,

musina soko yo kusangalala nhavivyo.

Mumanye nha vyono, lono nzoka monga yikufwa,

yokuza kanyhi yono 'mbi kuciza yiyo yono yifwiye.

Kanyhi mumanye nha vyono,

ligangha lye lizoka,

liganghasaga lizoka likali lyono ligulukaga.

30 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kowadima i wasocele wa muli wanhu wakwe,

kanyhi kowaleka wekale wapolile.

Ninga anye nyo Wafilisiti,

Muwaha Mulungu kolomuletelenyi nzala mbaha muno,

nayo yolomuwulajenyi nyenye wose,

na holowa hasina hamba monga mono yalasigala mumi.

31 Holeka anye nyo Wafilisiti,

mulile mu makaya genyu gose mulondole wutazi!

Wose mogope!

Hambi livunde lye manghundi,

nha likuza likulawilila kusumuka,

lipuga lye wasilikale wo wulugu,

wakali, kanyhi wasina woga.

32 Alu hamba ciwapitule ciwe kutyaci i wanhu

wono wacizize wakulawa ku Wafilisiti?

Ninga ase cowalonjela du nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu

yakondya kuyisimya Sayuni,

i wanhu wakwe wono wakugazwa

ho wasume kutiziza mumo no kuwa wapolile.


Isaya 15

Mulungu koyongomeza yisi ya Mowabu

1 Ayi yo nghani yono yalongwa kuli Mowabu.

Makaya ga Ali na Kili,

gakondya kongomezwa mu cilo cimonga,

ne yisi ya Mowabu yakondya kugwilwa ne cinyamazi ciwaha muno.

2 Nawo wanhu wa Diboni wakubita kulilila mu cigongo mucanyha.

Wanhu wa Mowabu nawo wakulila

ko wukalale ku soko ye makaya ga Nebo na Medeba,

waceta ne mitwe yawo ne zindevu zawo ko wusungu wuwaha.

3 Nawo wanhu wono wali mu zinzila,

wose wavwalile magunila.

Wose wakulila muno muno mucanyha ye matembe na mu mabalangu.

4 Wanhu wa Heshiboni, hamonga ne wanhu wa Eleyale,

nawo wakulila, ne cililo cawo cikuhulikwa sunga ko ku Yahazi.

Sunga ne wasilikale wose wakucicima,

no witumbilo wawo wawuka.

5 Nane nhumbula yangu yikuyikalalila muno Mowabu!

Hambi wanhu wayo nha wakondya kutiziza ku cikaya ca Zowali,

na ku Egilati-shelishiya.

Wanji watiziza ku Luhiti,

akuno wakujenda wakulila.

Wanji watiziza ku Holonayimu wakulila wazozize.

6 Ne cikolongo ca Nimulimu, naco canyala,

ne mahanze gono gacindice mu nhumbi yayo,

nago gakondya kunyala,

hasina cinji kanyhi cono cidedewaye.

7 Nawo wanhu hono wakulowoka likolongo lye mibici ye miyelezi,

wavilowose vinhu vyawo kuko, waviponye.

8 Ne cililo cikuhulikwa mu mipaka yose ya Mowabu.

Cililo cikuhulikwa mu vikaya vya Egilayimu na Beyelelimu.

9 Ne likolongo lya Diboni, lidunghuwaye sakami.

Nayo Mulungu yakali yawawiciye wongomele wuwaha

muno wanhu wawo wono wekaye kuko.

Ne wanhu wose wono wakali wasigaye mumo mu Mowabu,

wose wolowulagwa.


Isaya 16

Mowabu yisina kanyhi witumbilo

1 Anye nyo wanhu wa Mowabu,

ku wulawa mu cikaya ca Sela mu nyika,

ale mutegulilenyi ngholo,

mukamupele cipegwa mutemi mono yatemile lulu mu Yelusalemi.

2 Nawo walindiye ha nhumbi ye likolongo lya Alinoni,

wakupilima-pilima wenha zindeje zono wazikumuliye manhuli gazo.

3 Nawo wakuwalonjela wanhu wa Yuda wakutya,

“Ale mucilonjele zono citume.

Mucicitile nho mubici wono wukung'unhice mahamha gayo mu misi mugati,

mucilece cicehe mu mubeho wenyu.

Hambi ase nha cili wahamizi,

holeka muciwice i hanhu hono munhu yunji si kosuma kuciwona.

4 Mucilece cikale mono yisi yenyu.

Kanyhi mucicitile kuli wose wono wosaka kucongomeza.”

Wukunhulangwe hamonga no wongomezwe,

vyose vyolopuluta,

na wawo nawo wono wakuyihodanga yisi,

nawo nhavivilvya wolopuluta.

5 Hanyuma ya zizo, yunji wa muli wana wa Dawudi,

yo kolowa mutemi.

Nayo mutemi yuyo,

kolowatema wanhu ko wihuwilwa na ko wendo.

Nayo kanyhi kolowa mupelu ku wituma zo wiling'ano,

kanyhi kolosaka ling'ani du yo yitumwe mu wutemi wakwe.

6 Nawo i wanhu wa Yuda wolotya,

“Nase cihulice nha vyono wanhu wa Mowabu wewoni muno.

Kanyhi cimanyile, nha vyono wedunhumuzi muno,

kanyhi wevuni, na kumbe no widunhumuzi wawo ho wa bwete du.”

7 Ninga 'gwe walekaje!

Wololila ku soko ye magayo gono wakugaya.

Na wose wololila kolo wakukumbucila ndigwa swanu

yono walizaga mwi'kaya lya Kili-helesi,

no witumbilo wawo wowuka.

8 Ne migunda yono yili behi na Heshiboni,

hamonga ne migunda ye mizabibu,

yono yili ku Sibuma, yono yalavyaga divayi,

yono wetumamilaga watemi we zinghungugo zose,

alu yakondya kuhodanjika.

Mu mapango ganji,

mizabibu yiyo yikatandala sunga ko kwi'kaya lya Yezeli,

sunga ku lubavu lwa kucilima mu nyika,

na ku lubavu lwa kumwezi,

ku nhumbi nzagwe ye Lilenga lyo Mwino.

9 Alu nane nghuyililila Yezeli.

Ne mahozi gangu gakusulula so Heshiboni na Eleyale.

Hambi nha kusina ndudula yono yowasangalaza wanhu.

10 Alu hasina hamba munhu monga,

mono yayisangalaliye migunda yono yifisize muno.

Hasina hamba munhu monga mono yakwimba

hamba mono yena nyemo ku soko ye migunda yiyo ye mizabibu.

Hasina hamba munhu monga

mono yakukobonga zabibu yatuje divayi,

ne lizozo lye nyemo, lyaduma.

11 Holeka nane nghuzizidika muno ko wukalale ku soko ya Mowabu,

kanyhi nghuwona wusungu ku soko ya Kili-helesi.

12 Nawo wanhu wa Mowabu kolo wakwijela wakubita kwi'tunda lyawo wakahembe, wenjile mu wuhembelo wawo walombe, no wulombe wawo hamba si wohulikwa yaye.

13 Ayi yo nghani yono Muwaha Mulungu yalonga kuli Mowabu katali.
14 Ninga kanyhi aluno Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Na mu myaka midatu du, sawo yose ya Mowabu, yowa yamalika. Ne wanhu wakwe wose wofwa, langa wosigala wanhu wacefu du, ninga nawo wawo wono wosigala, wowa wasoceye muno.”


Isaya 17

Mulungu koloyilipiciza Siliya na Isilayeli

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Alu ku wulawa lulu,

Damesiki si lyolowa yikaya liwaha kanyhi,

langa lyowa yilundo du lye matongo.

2 Nago makaya ga Siliya, gowa nyika du cibitilila.

Howa wudimilo wa ngholo na ng'ombe,

hamba hasina mono koloziwinga mumo kanyhi.

3 Nawo Isilayeli wolowa wasina mudimi,

nayo Damesiki yolowusizwa wutemi wayo.

Nawo wanhu wa Siliya wono wolowa wasigaye,

wolowa mu cevu ciwaha nhe wanhu wa Isilayeli.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga, vyo nghutya.”

4 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Lizuwa likuza lyono Isilayeli yolowusizwa wuwaha wayo.

No wugoli wayo wologaluka wuwe wuciwa.

5 Nayo Isilayeli, kolowa yehwanile mugunda wono wakondya kududulwa,

walekwa wusina cinji,

nho mugunda wono wuli mwi'lolo lya Lefayimu

wono wakondya kuhalalizwa mahalalwa,

alu wekala wusina cinji.

6 Nawo Waisilayeli wolosigala wacefu muno muno,

wolowa nho mubici wo muzeyituni

wono wakondya kuhepwa mana gose,

alu gasigala mejete hamba madatu du ku cinang'anang'a co mubici,

hamba mana macefu du gono gasigaye

mu manhambi ga mu sika-sika du yo mubici.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu we Waisilayeli, vyo nghutya.”

7 Ne lizuwa lilyo kolo likuza, i wanhu wolomuwuyila Muwumbi wawo u Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli wakulondola wutazi.
8 Hamba si wolowika kanyhi witumbilo wawo mu vikumbicilo vyono wavizenga weneco ne miwoko yawo, hamba kwitumbila mu vihwani vyawo vyono wavisesa weneco, nha cinji vihwani vyo mulungu we cicekulu Ashela, hamonga ne vikumbicilo vyawo vyo kufusiza wuvumba.
9 Ne lizuwa lilyo kolo likuza, i makaya gose gono gena magomawakali, gololekwa matongo, nhe makaya ge Wahivi na ge Waamoli, gono wagaleka lono wawatizaga i wanhu wa Isilayeli.

10 Gwe Isilayeli!

Mbeka wamusemwa Mulungu mono kakuponyaga,

kanyhi mono kakucitilaga nhe litalawe lyono lili vilungo.

Alu wahadika mibici mu busitani siwanulwe,

ho wuwe kuyihemba milungu mijenzi.

11 Ninga wukumbucile nha vyono,

mibici yiyo, hamba yiwe yikutula,

no kulavya wuluwa wayo hakali mitondo muno,

ninga agwe hamba si kodudula cinji yaye muli yene.

Langa gowa magaziko no wusungu wuwaha wono si wulamalika yaye.

I makungugo malugu gakusumwa

12 I makungugo ge vilungo gali mu wugazi,

gakukoloma nhe lizozo lye lilenga vyono lizozaga,

gehwanile nho witoweze we mawingo mawaha ge lilenga.

13 Makungugo gozoza nhe lizozo lye mawingo mawaha ge lilenga,

ninga Mulungu kogatacila, nago gotiziza kutali.

Gopemheluswa nhe manghundi mucanyha ye matunda,

kotya mawaje mu civulavumbi.

14 Na mwi'pango lye cinyamihe galetaga wogofye.

Ninga kolo wukwela, hodu nago gawa kuwuka.

Nalyo lilyo lyo lifungu lyo munhu wo wose mono yakusaka kuyiteka yisi yetu.


Isaya 18

Mulungu koyilipiciza yisi ya Kushi

1 Ale, kuko ku nhumbi ye makolongo ga Kushi,

ko yili yisi yono kosuma kuhulika mulilo we mabawa.

2 Kushi yono yikutuma watumwa wakuziza mwi'kolongo lya Nile,

wapandile mu zingalawa zono nozwe ne madete-lulenga.

Ale muwuye kwenyu mbela,

anye nyo watumwa wapelu-pelu ku lumangho!

Mubite ku yisi yenyu yono yili haciligati ye makolongo,

kuli likungugo lyenyu lye nghakamo ne vilungo.

Kuli wanhu wenyu watali,

we miwili yono yitelemeye,

wono yinze yose yikuwogopa muno.

3 Ale, ta, muhulicize anye wose mono mwikaye mono'nze!

Hono muwone bendela yenulwa mucanyha mu vizuli vye matunda,

ale mulanje!

Kanyhi mweze muyihulicize ne ndulele kolo yeza kutowa!

4 Muwaha Mulungu yanonjela yakatya,

“Ane nolanga hasi mhola mhola ku wulawa ku wulanga,

nha vyono wubuhe wugwizaga ku cilo mu madaha ge nhwanga,

nzelu nhe lizuwa vyono ling'alalaga mu mapango ge misi.

5 Na lono zabibu zikali nadudulwa,

lono wuluwa wayo wakung'unhika, ne zabibu zapya,

walugu wolokuza wawongomeze Wakushi musunho du,

kotya munhu mono yakutema vinghonyi vye vivunda vye zabibu no mumaje.

6 Ne mivimba ye wasilikale wawo,

yolekwa yiligwe ne zindeje hamonga ne malimu ga mu mbago.

Mwi'pango lye cibahu, zindeje zowalya,

na mu cipango ce cifuku woligwa ne malimu.”

7 Ninga vilo vyokuza, vyono Muwaha Mulungu kolowa kubocela kanyhi vilavywa ku wulawa mu yisi yiyi yono yili haciligati ye makolongo, yisi ye nghungugo ye nghakamo, kanyhi yili vilungo, ye wanhu watali, we miwili yono yitelemeye, wono yinze yose yikuwogopa wene. Nawo wolokuza kwi'tunda lya Sayuni, i hanhu hono Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga, yakuwa kuhembwa.


Isaya 19

Mulungu koyilipiciza yisi ya Misili

1 Ayi yo nghani ya kuli yisi ya Misili.

Muwaha Mulungu kokuza ku Misili,

yapandile mwi'vunde lyono likumangha.

Ne vihwani vya Misili,

vikucicima hali mwene,

ne wanhu wa Misili vilungo vyawo vyodejepa.

2 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Nosagahiza wulugu mu yisi ya Misili,

wehome Wamisili wose weneco kwa weneco,

nawo wokwihoma nduguye no nduguye,

munhu no mwikalani muyagwe.

Nago makaya, gokwihoma yikaya ne likaya liyagwe,

wutemi no wutemi wuyagwe.

3 Nowudumuciza wuhile we mahweso ge Wamisili,

nolihodanga ne lihala lyawo.

Nawo wolondola wutazi kuli vihwani vyawo,

wokwitoweleza visangu mu waganga,

wowuza mahweso kuli myuhe ye wafu.

4 Nane nowakombola Wamisili mu miwoko yo munhu mono yali jila,

kuli mutemi mukali mono kowatema.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, vyo nghutya.”

5 Ne likolongo lye Nile, malenga golocepuka,

ne likolongo lyolojendelela kunyala mhola mhola.

6 Ne vikololozi vidodo dodo vye likolongo,

navyo vyowa kunyala mhola mhola.

Nago madete-lulenga hamonga ne madulwe, gowomba.

7 Navyo vilimwa vyose vyono vihadikwe mu nyenje ye likolongo lya Nile,

vyonyala no kupemheluswa ne mbeho.

8 Ne wanhu wose wono wetumbiye wanimula somba mumo mwi'kolongo lya Nile,

wowona wusungu no kulila.

Zindowano zawo hamonga ne zinyavu zawo,

zowa nghani ya bwete.

9 Nawo wono wahonaga myenda ye bafuta wotukuwikwa mitima yawo.

10 Nawo i wahoni we myenda,

hamonga na wono wali mbali ku milimo,

vilungo vyawo vyodejepa kanyhi wokalazwa.

11 Nawo i walongola wa mwi'kaya lya Sayuni, mbeka walele!

Hambi nha wakubocela wulanjizwe

wo wulele we wanhu we mahala wa Misili!

Ku sokoci wakwijelela kumulonjela mutemi,

nha vyono wene wo wahazi we walimu we katali,

hamonga ne watemi?

12 Nagwe Falawo u mutemi wa Misili,

wali hayi lu i wanyamhala wako wasugu?

Na lunji wali wasume kukulonjela ze mahweso

go Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga,

zono yakuyihweseza Misili.

13 I walongola wa Zowani na Mempisi wose walele.

Hambi nha wo wali wetumbiywe nha vyono wo wolitonga likungugo,

ninga alu walyajiliza.

14 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu,

yo yawanoza wawe no wulanjizi wo wajilizi.

Holeka nayo Misili, zose zono yikwituma,

yikwituma kotya munhu mono yagalile wujimbi,

mono yakupebesuka no kutelela mu madeko gakwe mweneco.

15 Na mumo mu Misili,

hasina munhu mugoli hamba muciwa,

munhu we lilumbo hamba mono siyamanyice,

mono kosuma kulavya wutazi.

Misili yolomuhemba Muwaha Mulungu

16 Ninga vilo vyokuza, vyono i wanhu wa Misili, wolowa wali cevu, wokwihwana we cicekulu. Hambi nha wocicima ko woga kolo wakuwona Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga yakuwugolosa muwoko wakwe yawalipicize.
17 Nawo wanhu wa Misili, Yuda yowa kuwogofya muno muno mu mapango gose, kolo wakukumbucizwa zo wulipicizwe, wono Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga yawawikanizize.
18 Ne vilo vivyo kolo vikuza, nonga ye Cihebelaniya yowa kulongwa mu makaya mahano ge Wamisili. Ne wanhu wolokwilahiziza mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga. Ne likaya limonga lya mu makaya gago, lyocemwa “Likaya lye Lizuwa.”

19 Ne vilo vivyo kolo vyafika, i cikumbicilo co Muwaha Mulungu, colozengwa mu yisi ya Misili, hamonga ne lisumbili lye libwe ilisiwanulile nene mu mipaka ya Misili.
20 Naco cowa co citanjilo co Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga kuko ku Misili. Na kolo wanhu wakwiwonelwa kuko, ho wamucemeleze Muwaha Mulungu ho watuje wutazi, nayo Muwaha Mulungu komutegula yunji yawaponye.
21 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kokwilajila kuli Wamisili. Nawo womumanya wawe kumuhemba mwene. Ho wasume kumulavizya vilavywa ne makumbiko. Wolomwihuwiza, wetume nha vyono wehuwize.
22 Alu Muwaha Mulungu kowalipiciza Wamisili. Ninga kanyhi kowaponya. Nawo womuwuyila mwene, nayo kowuhulika wolombe wawo no kuwaponya.

23 Na kolo vilo vivyo vyakondya kufika, holowa na nzila mbaha nyejete haciligati ya Misili na Ashuli. Ne wanhu wono wali mu zisi zizo zinyejete, wowa kujenda wakuyumba-yumba mu zinzila zizo zinyejete, ne makungugo mejete, gohemba hamonga.
24 Na kolo vilo vivyo vikuza, Isilayeli kokwilunza na Misili na Ashuli. Ne makungugo gaga madatu, zolowa wulugutwe wono wolowaziza yinze yose.
25 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga kolowalugutiza, yakutya, “Nomulugutizenyi anye nyo Wamisili nyo wanhu wangu, nanye nyo Waashuli, mono namuwumbenyi, hamonga nanye Isilayeli, nyo wanhu wangu wahagulwa.”


Isaya 20

I citanjilo co Muwizi Isaya, lono yali Cipwi

1 Saligoni u mutemi wa Ashuli, yakamulajiliza musilikale muwaha we wasilikale we Waashuli wo wulugu, wabite wakayihome Ashidodi likaya lye Wafilisiti.
2 Na mu mapango gago, Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Isaya u mwana wa Amozi, yavule vilatu vyakwe ne myenda yakwe ye ligunila, vyono yali yavwalile. Nayo Isaya yakahulika, yakawa kuvula nha vyono yalonjelwa, yakawa kujenda cipwi kanyhi yasina vilatu.
3 Nalyo likaya lya Ashidodi lyaza kutekwa, Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “U mwitumami wangu Isaya mu myaka midatu yali jenda cipwi kanyhi yasina vilatu. Holeka cicino co citanjilo cono cikulajila zono zowejelela we zi'si za Misili na Kushi.
4 Hambi u mutemi wa Ashuli nha vyo kowategula wali cipwi, i wafungwa wono yawateka ku wulawa mu zi'si zizi zi nyejete Kushi na Misili. Nawo i wazelelo hamonga ne wakombi, wose wojenda wali cipwi, kanyhi wasina malatu, akuno ne madako gawo gali cipwi, ho wawakoze soni Wamisili.
5 Na wose wono wayitumbiye Kushi, no kwilumbila Misili, woloyilajila mbeka yawo kono ko yili, hambi witumbilo wawo nha wakondya kuhodanjika.
6 Ne lizuwa lilyo kolo likuza, i wanhu wono wekalaga mu mhwani ye yisi ye Wafilisiti, wololonga watye, ‘Alu mulanje ta, zono ziwejeleye i wanhu wono cali ciwetumbiye ho wacitaze mu miwoko yo mutemi Waashuli alu mwene mbeka ho cosuma wule kupona?’ ”


Isaya 21

Wugubulilwe wo wugwe wa Babiloni.

1 Ayi yo nghani ya kuli Babiloni.

Ale, wongomele wokuza ku wulawa mu yisi yo kogofya.

Nawo wokuza nha civulavumbi cono cikufwagula nyika cikulowoka.

2 Ane niwuwona wugubulilwe we metumo go wukali,

wugubulilwe wo wugaluci no wongomezwe.

Lipuga lye wasilikale wa Elamu, likuhoma!

Lipuga lye wasilikale wa Mediya,

nalyo ligapilime makaya!

Alu langa Mulungu kogawusiza lukulu magazo gono galetwa na Babiloni.

3 Nane, zizo zono nghaziwona no kuzihulika mu wugubulilwe,

zikamhela woga hamonga no wusungu,

wusungu wono wihwanile

no wusungu wo mucekulu hono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

4 Mutwe wangu wukutama,

nane nghucicima so woga.

Nali nindiye cizuwa cigwile,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

si nambulila cinji, langa woga du.

5 Na mumo mu wugubulilwe,

ndigwa yiwa kutengwa,

ne masambi-sambi gakutandikwa hasi,

i wajenzi ho wekalile.

Alu wakulya akuno wakung'wa.

Na hanyuma hadodo,

musilikale muwaha we wasilikale,

kawa kucema nhinicizo yakutya,

“Nye walume!

Ale, muwikanize luno zingula zenyu!”

6 Hanyuma nayo Muwaha Mulungu kawa kunonjela yakutya,

“Ale, wubite wukawice mulindilizi,

wumulonjele yazilonje zono yakuziwona.

7 Hono yawe yakuwona wanhu wakuza wapandile fwalasi,

wakujenda wejete wejete,

na hone wanhu wanji nawo wapandile mu ndogowe na mu zingamiya,

ale, yimunojeye yasuguce muno.”

8 Nayo mulindilizi yakulonga yehindile yakutya,

“Ane nali nimile bahano mu wudumi, misi ne cilo.”

9 Ninga kwa nhinicizo,

niwa kuwawona wakuza!

Wanhu wapandile fwalasi wakujenda wepanhame wejete wejete.

Mulindilizi yakulonga zono yawene, yakutya,

“Babiloni yakondya kugwa!

Sunga ne vihwani vyose vyono wavihembaga,

vyakondya kugwa hasi vyabenanjika.”

10 Nye nyo Waisilayeli, mo wanhu wangu,

mwali mwikaye mukukuzulwa nho 'hemba.

Ninga aluno nakondya kumulonjelenyi nghani swanu,

yono niyihulice kuli Muwaha Mulungu

we mihanga u Mulungu wa Isilayeli.

Nghani kuli Edomu

11 Ayi nayo yo nghani kuli Edomu.

Ko yuli munhu yunji mono yakunyhemeleza ku wulawa Seyili,

yakutya, “Gwe mulindilizi,

alu wusiku ho wokwela nili lo?

Ta wunonjele, ho wokwela nili?”

12 Nane nghamupitula nghatya,

“Wusiku weza behi no kwela,

ninga hamba nhavivyo, i cilo cokuza kanyhi.

Holeka nagwe, hono wuwe wukusaka kuwuza,

ale, nhondo wuze du wuwuze kanyhi.”

Nghani kuli Alabiya

13 Ayi nayo yo nghani kuli Alabiya.

Alu anye nyo wanhu wa Dedani,

alo wanani lo wono wasitile nghambi yawo

mu nyika nyhiwa, ya Alabiya,

14 ale muwapelaje malenga wang'we wanhu wono wakaluce,

wono wakumuzizenyi.

Nanye nyo wanhu we yisi ya Tema,

nanye muwapele ndigwa walye wawo watizizi.

15 Hambi wanhu nha wakutiza ho weponye no mumaje

wono wakondya kuwikanizwa ho wuwawulaje,

no wupinde wono wuwikanizwe wugume,

hamonga ne zimbali zose zo wulugu.

16 Hanyuma Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Lono mwaka wumonga du wukali namalika, i nhojo ya Kedali yomalika.
17 Hamba yiwe i wanhu waguma wupinde wa Kedali, wo wanhu wono vitung'ati muno, ninga hamba nhavivyo, wacefu muno muno wa muli wene wo wosigala. Ane yo nene no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu we Waisilayeli vyo nghutya.”


Isaya 22

Nghani yo Mulungu kuli Yelusalemi

1 Ayi nayo yo nghani yo Mulungu kuli lilolo lyo wugubulilwe.

Ta munonjelaje,

alu mwina nghanici yono yikuleka wose

mukunozeza wuhembi wenyu mucanyha ye matembe ge zikaya?

2 Hambi nha nghuwona likaya lyose

limemile sasamo ya nyhenze ne nyemo mbaha.

I wanhu wako wono wafwa mu cipango co wulugu,

si wawulagwa no wulugu yaye.

3 Langa walongola wako wose watiza,

ninga kanyhi wose wakatekwa

hamba si wasuma kuguma songa monga yaye.

4 Alu ta, mundekaje niwalilile ko wusungu wanhu wangu wose

wono wakondya kufwa,

hamba mulece kusindamika yaye.

5 Hambi ali nha lyo lizuwa lye mahwele,

no wusumwe, hamonga no woga wuwaha,

mwi'lolo lyo wugubulilwe.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

nha yo yacileteye.

Alu lanje, sunga ne majinji ge likaya lyetu,

gose gakondya kumwaguka,

ne mazi ge cililo cetu co kulondola wutazi,

ne matunda nhondo gakubocelela.

6 Wakawa kuza wasilikale wakulawa ku yisi ya Elamu,

wapandile mu zifwalasi,

wahabatize ne mapinde gawo,

hamonga ne zisonga zayo.

Nawo wasilikale wanya ku yisi ya Kili,

nhavivyo, nawo wena ne zingula zawo.

7 Ne lilolo liswanu lya Yuda,

lyono lina moso mwinji,

likamema matuka menji muno go wulugu.

Ne wasilikale wono wali wali mucanyha ye zifwalasi,

wakawa wemile ha mwande ye milango ye likaya lya Yelusalemi.

8 Ne vilungo vyose vya Yuda, vikawa vyakondya kumalika. Na zizo zali zeza kwijela, nanye mukavisola vitwalo vye milewo vya mwi'kaya lya mu mbago.
9 Mukagawona ne majinji ga Yelusalemi, mono gasakwaga gazengwe kanyhi.
10 Mukazisunjilila zikaya zose za mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi, na zinji za muli zikaya zizo, mukazimwagula ho musole mabwe gayo muzenjele majinji mono gali gamwaguce.
11 Na hanyuma kanyhi, mukanoza lilamba haciligati ye likaya, ho lyambe malenga gono gahulumaga gakulawa mwi'lamba lye katali. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, anye si mwamuhulika Mulungu yaye, mono yaziwikaniza zose zizo ku wulawa katali, no kuzileka zijele.

12 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

yamucemenyi ho mulile ko wukalale,

mucete mitwe yenyu, muvwale magunila.

13 Ninga anye, mukaseka no kusangalala.

Mukawulaga ngholo, hamonga ne zing'ombe ho mulye,

mukang'wa muno muno divayi sunga mukagala.

Hanyuma mukawa kutya, “Kaa!

Lekata cilye, cing'we!

Hambi mitondo nha cofwa.”

14 Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

yakanonjela yakatya,

“Hamba si wolonenelelwa lukulu u wubi wuwu,

sunga kono walafwila.

Ane yo nene no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, vyo nghutya.”

Nghani yo Mulungu kuli Shebina

15 Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, yakanonjela nha vyono mbite kuli Shebina, u mulanjilizi muwaha we viya vye kaya yo wutemi, nghamulonjele nitye,
16 “Alu mwene wukwihwesa gwegwe wuli nani? Na cono coleka wisesese citenjelo mu mabwe mu matunda, gwegwe go mweneco, cicici?
17 Heya, wali wuwe mbeka wuli munhu we'lumbo, na we vilungo ninga 'lu, Muwaha Mulungu kokusola yakugumile kutali muno.
18 Mbeka kokutondola nha mupila wo kubana, yakutaje ku yisi ngazi muno. Nagwe kolofwila kuko du, behi ne matuka gako go wulugu gono wukwidunhumulila. Hambi nha wukuyikoza cevu kaya yo muwaha wako.
19 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kolokuwusiza wuwaha wako wo wulanjilizi we kaya yakwe, yakuwinje na mu mulimo wako.

20 “Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Shebina yakamutya, Na zizi kolo zikwijela, ane nolomutuma mwitumami wangu Eliyakimu u mwana wa Hilikiya.
21 Nomuvwika yuyo Eliyakimu i vivwalo vyako vyo witumami, hamonga no musipi wayo, niwe kumupela wulajilize wo mulimo wono agwe wali kwituma. Nayo kokwihwana sogo yo munhu muli wanhu wa Yelusalemi na Yuda.
22 Nomupela wulajilize wose yawe hasi yo mutemi, u mwana wa Dawudi. Mwene yo kokamisa zifunguwo zose ze nyumba ya Dawudi. Nayo hono yadigula, hasina munhu mono kodinda, na hone yadinda, hasina munhu mono kodigula.
23 Nane nomusikamya nha musumali mutowelwe hanhu, nayo kowa wo mwamulizo we lilumbo kuli we mhuga yakwe wose.

24 “Ninga kanyhi i wanduguze, hamonga na wose wono wamwitumbiye ku visakwa, awo wose wowa mutegulo wakwe. Wokwisomeka muli mwene, wawe kuninyina nhe zimheyo, ne mahali, ne zinhambiko vyono vininyinaga mu visomeko!
25 Ninga kolo zizi zikwijela, u musumali wono wali wuwambile muno, wolohogoha, wuwe kugwa. Na wuwo wo wolowa wo mudumo wa vyose vyono vyali vitumbice mumo.” Yo nene Muwaha Mulungu vyo nghutya.


Isaya 23

Nghani yo Mulungu kuli Tulo na Sidoni

1 Ayi yo nghani yo Mulungu kuli Tulo.

Ale, mulile ko wusungu,

anye nyo wajendela mu zingalawa mwi'lenga!

Hambi i hanhu ha wuficilo we zingalawa zenyu ku Tulo,

nha hakondya kongomezwa.

I makaya gayo, hamonga ne hanhu ho wuficilo we zingalawa,

mose gawa matongo du.

Nagwe zingalawa zako kolo zakondya kuwuya ku wulawa Kupulo,

ale, komanyizwa.

2 Alu mulile'nye nyo wasuluza wa ku Sidoni!

Hambi nha mwali muzoweye kutuma,

3 wanhu wakulowoka lilenga wakagule

no kuguza wuhemba wono wukulimwa ku Misili,

no kunoza wusuluza ne wanhu we zinghungugo zose.

4 Gwegwe 'gwe 'kaya lya Sidoni, alu wakozwa cevu!

Hambi lilenga, ne ligomawakali lye lilenga liwaha,

nha lyo lilonjile likutya, “Ane nha nghali natuga,

hamba kuwilusa i wana we cilume hamba we cicekulu.”

5 Sunga ne Wamisili wose wotinuka,

kanyhi wokogofywa muno muno,

kolo wakuhulika vyono Tulo yakondya kongomezwa.

6 Holeka nanye nyo wanhu wa Foyinike,

mulile ko wusungu wuwaha!

Mutizize ku Sipaniya!

7 Alu mbeka lili lyowa lyo likaya lya Tulo lyono lyali lisangalaye,

lyono lyazengwa katali muno?

Alu mwene, lilino mbeka lyo likaya

lyono lyawategulaga wahamizi ku nhumbi ye lilenga ho wakebate zisi?

8 Alu mwene yo munhuci mono yazihwesa

ho yazilete zono zinha zizi mwi'kaya lya Tulo,

mwi'kaya lili liswanu muno.

Tulo, lyono ne wakwawi walyo,

wali wo watemi wono wali wo wanhu

wono wena lilumbo liwaha muno mono'nze yiyi?

9 Ninga wumanye, vyono yo Muwaha Mulungu du we mihanga,

yo yaziwika zizi.

Yaziwika ho yawawusize lukulu cibede cawo wanhu wa Tulo,

cibede cono cali ciwatumvizye,

kanyhi ho yawanyetye ne wanhu wawo we lilumbo.

10 Ale, mubite mukayilime yisi,

anye nyo wanhu wose

mono ma'si genyu gali hasi yo wutemi wa Sipaniya!

Kanyhi howa hasina

hamba munhu monga mono kosuma kumucitilenyi yaye.

11 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

yawugoloye muwoko wakwe mwi'lenga,

ho yagongomezeze mwi'lenga matemi gose.

Kanyhi yakondya kulajiliza nha vyono,

magomawakli gose ga mu Kanani, gongomezezwe lukulu.

12 Alu 'gwe, 'gwe 'kaya lya Sidoni,

i nyemo yako nhondo yakondya kuduma,

ne wanhu wako wakwiwonelwa.

Hamba watizize ku Kupulo, ninga si wopona yaye.

13 (Nawo wanhu wa Babiloni wo wagenjiza malimu ga mu mbago ho gayihodanje yisi ya Tulo, ninga si wali wanhu wa Ashuli yaye, wali wawo wanhu wa Babiloni wo walipilima likaya lya Tulo, wakagamwagulanga ne magomawakali gayo, wakalileka likaya lyawa yitongo.)

14 Nanye nyo wajendela mwi'lenga,

mulile ko wusungu wuwaha!

Hambi likaya lyono mwali mulitumbiye,

nha lyakondya kongomezwa.

15 Ne vilo vyokuza, vyono Tulo yolowa yisemigwe, sunga yipulute myaka makumi mupunghati ye vilo vyo wutemi wo mutemi monga. Na kolo myaka yiyo yapuluta, nayo Tulo, nhondo ho yolowa nho lwimbo lo musenyha.

16 Ale, lu, wusole lisumbi, wupilime ku zikaya,

agwe go muciwa mono wusemigwe!
