Wuvine, no kwimba ne zinyimbo zako,

wuwagomole kanyhi wanhu kamonga.

17 Nayo myaka yiyo makumi mupunghati kolo yapuluta, nayo Muwaha Mulungu ho koloyileka kanyhi Tulo, yijendelele no wusuluza wayo we katali. Nayo yolokwiyedya yeneco mu matemi gose ga mono'nze yose.
18 Ne sawo yose yono kowa kuyituga mumo mu wusuluza wakwe, yowa kulavywa kuli Muwaha Mulungu. Ninga yene si yolokwiwicila ciwutwa yaye. Langa sawo yiyo, yolokwitumamilwa ne wanhu wono wolowa kumuhemba du Muwaha Mulungu, wolowa kugula ndigwa hamonga ne vivwalo vyono wanjiye.


Isaya 24

Muwaha Mulungu koloyilipiciza yinze

1 Muwaha Mulungu koloyihodanga yinze no kuyileka yili bwete.

Koloyipitula yinze no kuweyagaza wanhu wayo.

2 Ne wanhu wose wolokwiyagazwa,

i wakohani hamonga ne wanhu.

Watumwa, hamonga ne wawaha wawo.

Wono wakugula, hamonga na wono wakuguza.

Wono wakwazima, hamonga na wono wakuwazima.

Iwagoli, hamonga ne waciwa.

3 Yinze yolokwiyagazwa no kongomezwa.

Muwaha Mulungu yo yalonjile, na vyo zowa zinha.

4 Yinze yikukalala no kunyala,

ne yinze yose yikutimba,

yinze hamonga no wulanga, vyose vikusakala.

5 Ne wanhu wakondya kuyavufya yinze,

so wubena malajilizo go Mulungu,

no kudelengula cilagane cono yacinoza, ho cikale cibitilila.

6 Holeka nayo Mulungu yayilijita yinze.

Ne wanhu nhondo wakulipicizizwa mu metumo gawo weneco.

Na muli wawo, wacefu du wono wasigala womi.

7 Ne migunda ye mizabibu yeza kunyala,

ne divayi nayo yikuwa nyhefu.

Ne wanhu wose wono wali wasangalaye alu wasina nyemo kanyhi.

8 Ne lizi lye nyemo lyanyamala no wuvina we zindonondo wawuka,

nawo wutowe we zing'oma, alu waduma.

9 Nalu, hawa hasina kanyhi wimbe wo wusangalale ku soko ye divayi,

hasina hamba munhu monga

mono yakuyisangalalila divayi muzinzi wayo.

10 Na mwi'kaya namo, mweza mumemile wuwulaji,

sunga ne wanhu weza kwidindila mu zikaya zawo ho weponye.

11 Ne wanhu nawo wakulila mu zinzila,

vyono heza hasina kanyhi divayi.

Ne nyemo yawo yawucila lukulu,

no wusangalale wakondya kuwuswa mu'si.

12 Ne likaya lyasigala matongo du,

ne milango yayo yakondya kubenanjizwa.

13 Na zizi zo zoyijelela yinze yose mu zinghungugo zose.

Yokwihwana no mudumo we nhwanga,

lono mizeyituni yakondya kukong'onhwa mu mibici yayo,

no kuhalaliza zabibu zose mu mizabibu yayo.

14 Nawo wono walasigala,

wolowa kwimba ko wusangalale.

Na wono wolowa wekaye ku lubavu lwa kumwezi,

wologanula zo wuwaha wo Muwaha Mulungu vyono winha,

15 nawo wono wekaye ku lubavu lwa kucilima,

wolomukumya mwene.

Nawo i wanhu wono wekaye mu mwalambalo we lilenga,

wolomukumya Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli.

16 Nawo we ma'si go kutali mono'nze,

wolozihulika zinyimbo zo kumukumya Isilayeli,

nghungugo ye ling'ani.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo, ane nha sina witumbilo!

Nghuganda!

Nawo i wagaluci vyo wakucila kungaluka du,

no wukonji wawo wukucila kuwa wubi muno muno.

17 Alu muhulicizaje ta anye wose!

Howuli wogofywe ne makweme,

hamonga ne mitego vikumulindilenyi nyenye.

18 No munhu wo wose mono kolojela kwiponya mu wogofywe,

kologwila mwi'kweme.

No munhu wo wose mono kolojela kwiponya mwi'kweme,

kolokwibatwa no mutego.

Ne malenga menji ge mvula,

golotonya ku wulawa ku wulanga.

Ne misinji ye yinze yolosinjisika.

19 Yinze yolopanduka no kubenanjika hamonga no kudemuka yiwe mwazi.

20 Ne yinze yoloyumba-yumba nho mugalo 'jimbi

no kupemheluswa nha cibanda mu mbeho yo mukukunzo.

Nayo yinze yikuvunwa no wubi wayo,

na mwi'zuwa linji yologwa hasi,

hamba si yolokwinucila lukulu kanyhi.

21 Nalyo lizuwa likuza,

lono Muwaha Mulungu kolowalipiciza mapuga ge wawaha,

gono gali kucanyha,

hamonga ne watemi we yinze wono wali mono'nze.

22 Mulungu kolowakonghola hamonga watemi wose

nha wafungwa wono wali mwi'kweme.

Kolowadindila mu jela wekale mumo,

sunga lizuwa lyawo lyo wulipicizwe lize lifice.

23 Nawo mwezi wolotukuwala,

ne lizuwa nalyo hamba si lyolowalika yaye.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

nha kolowa yo mutemi.

Nayo kolotema mu Yelusalemi,

mwi'tunda lya Sayuni,

ne walongola wose we wanhu,

wololuwona lukumyo lwakwe.


Isaya 25

Lwimbo lo Lukumyo

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

yo gwegwe go Mulungu wangu.

Nane nokupela cogopo, no kulikumya litagwa lyako.

Hambi nha wetuma metumo go wizine

kugafisiliza ko wihuwilwa i mahweso gako

gono wagawikaniza ku wulawa katali na katali.

2 Kugakumulanga makaya kugaleka matongo,

kugongomeza ne magomawakali gawo

sunga ne makaya go wutemi gose,

gono walugu wagazenga,

gose gamwagucila lukulu cibitilila.

3 Ne wanhu we makungugo ge vilungo

wokukumya gwegwe

ne makaya ge makungugo gose magazi,

gokogopa gwegwe.

4 I waciwa hamonga na wono wasina wutazi

wose wakutiziza kuli'gwe

nawo wakuwa wapolile mu vilo vyo wugaye.

Kuwacitilaga mu mubeho yo mukukunzo,

kanyhi kuwacehezaga mu mbeho mwi'zuwa likali.

Ne wanhu wagazi, wahungucilaga,

wenha libeho likali lyo mukukunzo.

5 Wenha i hanhu hakavu,

hono mvula yikali si natonya vilo vinji.

Ninga 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wakondya kuwanyamaza i walugu wetu,

waganyamaza mazozo ge wanhu wagazi,

nha vyono mavunde gapozaga livuce lya mwi'zuwa likali.

Mulungu yakuwikaniza Ndigwa

6 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, kolowikaniza ndigwa mwi'tunda lili lya Sayuni, so makungugo gose ga mono'nze. Ndigwa yono yolowa ndigwa swanu muno, hamonga na divayi swanu muno.
7 Nayo baho kololiwusa kamonga likukuwi lyo wukalale lyono lyaninyinaga mucanyha ye makungugo gose.
8 Muwaha Mulungu kololiwusiza lukulu lifwa cibitilila! Kanyhi kolopatila lukulu mahozi mu meso ge wanhu wose, no kuwawusiza cevu wanhu wakwe, cono wali wakondya kukozwa mu yinze yose. Muwaha Mulungu mweneco yo yazilonga zizi!
9 Nazo zizi kolo zejela, wanhu wose wololonga watye, “Mbeka, heya! Ayu yo Mulungu wetu! Nase comwitumbila mwene, nayo nha yaciponya. Mwene yo Muwaha Mulungu! Nase cakondya kumwitumbila. Nase luno cisangalaye muno, kanyhi cina nyemo mbaha, vyono mwene yakondya kuciponya.”

Mulungu koyilipiciza Mowabu

10 Muwaha Mulungu kolicitila litunda lya Sayuni, ninga i wanhu wa Mowabu, wokanyanjizwa ho hasi, nha vyono wagakanyanjilaga mabeleje gawa suji.
11 Nawo woloyigolola miwoko yawo nha wanhu wono wakuhila kwitiba mwi'lamba. Ninga Mulungu kolowanyetya, ne miwoko yawo yiwe yisina vilungo kanyhi.
12 Mulungu kologongomeza magomawakali ga Mowabu, hamonga ne majinji gawo matali, yagagwise sunga ho hasi, gawe manghundi.


Isaya 26

Mulungu kowapela wanhu wakwe wusume

1 Lizuwa lyolokuza,

lono wanhu wololwimba lwimbo aluno mu yisi ya Yuda;

Likaya lyetu, lye 'kaya lye vilungo!

2 Holeka 'lu digulenyi milango,

ho linjile likungugo lye ling'ani,

likungugo lyono wanhu walyo wetumaga ze mbeka.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kuwapelaga mulamu wono wukwilaniciye,

i wanhu wono wuhile wawo wose wuli kuli'gwe

kanyhi wakwitumbiye gwegwe.

4 Mumwitumbile Muwaha Mulungu cibitilila

hambi nha yo mukamisi wetu vilo vyose.

5 Nha yakondya kuwanyetya i wedunhumuzi,

ne makaya gawo gono wekalaga,

Mulungu yakondya kugongomeza,

ne majinji gayo kakondya kugatulanjiza gawa manghundi.

6 Na wa walya wono wewonelwaga,

alu wakugakanyanjila mucanyha yago

wakugasambanjiza hasi ye magulu gawo.

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kuyitelemezaga nzila ye wanhu we ling'ani,

wawa kukola mu nzila yo wuhihi.

8 Ase cikutyatya zo wuhile wako,

kanyhi cikwitumbiye gwegwe,

agwe yo gwegwe mono kucikwilanicizaga

vyose vyono cikwilowa.

9 Nane nikalaga nghukwihwesa gwegwe du

cilo cose kwe nhumbula yangu yose,

nagwe kolo wukuyiling'aniza yinze

hamonga ne wanhu wayo,

baho ho wolomanyila ze ling'ani yako vyono yinha.

10 Hamba yiwe agwe wuli lusungu kuli wanhu wabi,

ninga wene hamba si wamanyaga yaye ku wituma ze ling'ani.

Hamba wawe luno,

wali mu yisi ye wanhu we ling'ani,

ninga wene vyo wakali kwituma zo wubi,

hamba si wakusacila lukulu kuzimanya zo wuwaha wako.

11 Ne walugu wako nawo,

hamba si wakumanya yaye nha vyono kowalipiciza.

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuwakozaje cevu,

wuwalece wenjile mu magayo go

wulipicizwe wono wuwawikanizize.

Ninga 'gwe, wuwalajile zo wendo wako kuli wanhu wako.

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kocimotya mu milimo yetu yose,

hambi nha yo soko ye metumo gako.

13 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wetu,

nha cali citemigwe ne wanhu wanji,

ninga agwe yo gwegwe du wuli wene,

yo wuli Muwaha Mulungu wetu.

14 Ninga 'lu wawo wose wakondya kufwa,

hamba si wowumipa kanyhi

myuhe yawo hamba si yozuka yaye,

hambi agwe nha wakondya kuwalipiciza

no kuwongomezeza lukulu.

Hasina kanyhi hamba yunji mono kowakumbucila.

15 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, walinjifya likungugo lyetu,

wayigazimhya ne mipaka ye yisi yetu mbavu zose,

ne nghani yiyi yo yikupelile lilumbo yiwaha.

16 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wawalipiciza wanhu wako

nawo wakahidimbala kukulomba ko

wusungu wuwaha muno.

17 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wacilizya muno,

nha vyono kalilaga ko wusungu mucekulu mono yali

mu nghunga yo lusawo.

18 Nase cali cili mu wusungu,

cali cili mu nghunga yo lusawo,

ninga hamba kulela cinji co cose.

Cali cisina vilungo vyo kusumila mu yisi yetu

holeka hamba si ciketuma cinji co cose.

19 Ninga wanhu wa muli wanhu wetu wono wafwa,

awo langa wolozuka kanyhi!

Ne miwili yawo yilawumipa kanyhi.

Na wose wono wagonile mu vitenjelo,

wolokwinuka wawe kwimba na nyemo.

Hambi Muwaha Mulugu nha kolowazukula

wose wono wafwa katali,

wowa nha buhe yono yiyisangalazaga yisi.

Wuling'anizwe no wuzuce

20 Nye nyo wanhu wangu,

alu mwinjile mu zikaya zenyu, mwidindile mumo.

Mwihundice mumo cidaha, sunga likuwo lyo Mulungu lipulute.

21 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu

nha yakuza yakulawa ku wulanga ku wikalo wakwe,

ho yawalipicize wanhu we yinze ku soko yo wubi wawo.

No wuwulaji wose wono wetumwaga cinyele mono'nze,

wose wololajilwa cagalanze.

Ne yinze nayo,

hamba si yolowahundika yaye wose wono wawulagwa.


Isaya 27

1 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, Muwaha Mulungu kolokwitumamila mumaje we lifwa, kwe vilungo vyakwe, kololilipiciza lilimu lyono likucemwa Lewiyatani, lizoka lyono libinzulaga, liwa kwinyongola, no kuliwulaga lizoka lyono likalaga mwi'lenga.

2 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo,

u Muwaha Mulungu kololonga

zo mugunda wakwe muswanu we mizabibu, yawe kutya,

3 “Nene no Muwaha Mulungu mono niwukamisaga,

kanyhi niwumwajilaga ne malenga nhondo zose.

Yo nene mono niwulindilizaga ku cilo ne misi,

ho yunji yalece kuwuhodanga.

4 Hamba nghali si nawukolelwa likuwo yaye u mugunda wuwu.

Na hono gawe mogali mamivya ne mabijili,

gono gosaka gawutitilize,

ane nigalimaga niwa kuganyanya.

5 Ninga nawo i walugu we wanhu wangu,

hono wasace niwadime,

yiwanojeye wamule welamule nane.

Heya mbeka, yiwanojeye sunga welamule nane.”

6 Na mu vilo vivyo vyono vikuza,

i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wo lulelo lwa Yakobo,

wolocindika nha mubici,

wolosanula no kupasicila,

no kuyimemya yinze yose mikalo.

7 Hambi Waisilayeli nha si watuga wulipicizwe wuwaha

nha vyono walipicizwa i walugu wawo,

ne wanhu wa Isilayeli,

hamba si wafwa wenji,

nha vyono wafwa walugu wawo.

8 Langa Muwaha Mulungu,

yawalipiciziza wanhu wakwe,

mu nzila yo kuwahamya no kuwategula ku yisi yo wujenzi.

Lono yawahwajiliza no mukukunzo we mbeho nghali ya kucilima.

9 Ninga wubi we Waisilayeli, wolonenelwa du,

kolo wene wakondya kugatulanjiziza lukulu i mabwe

ge vikumbicilo vye wanhu wono si wamumanyile Muwaha Mulungu,

hawe hasina kanyhi vikumbicilo vyo kufusiza wuvumba,

hamba vihwani vyo mulungu we cicekulu Ashela,

vyono vikali visigaliye.

10 Woyi hee!

I magomawakali ge makaya, gaweza 'lu matongo.

Gakondya kulekwa gasina wanhu,

geza nha mbago yono yisina cinji.

Alu haweza wudimilo we zing'ombe,

zikudima no kuceha mumo.

11 Sunga ne manhambi ge mibici gakunyala,

gakubeneka, ne wacekulu nawo wakutenya mumo ne zingwagu.

Hambi i wanhu nha siwakutanga yaye,

holeka nayo Mulungu u Muwumbi wawo,

si kowawonela lusungu, hamba si kowanenela yaye.

12 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, u Muwaha Mulungu kolowakongholeza wanhu wakwe yumonga yumonga nha vyono munhu kelaga wuhemba mu mawaje ku wulawa Efulata, sunga ko ku mupaka we yisi ye Misili.
13 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, ndulele yololila, yiwacemeleze Waisilayeli wose wono wali ku wuhamywe, ku yisi ya Ashuli, na ku yisi ya Misili, wose wawuye. Nawo wolowuya, wamuhembe Muwaha Mulungu mwi'tunda lyakwe lyono lyelile lya mu Yelusalemi.


Isaya 28

Suguzi kuli Wutemi wo lubavu lwa kusumuka

1 Woyi hee, kuli wutemi wa Isilayeli,

hambi nha wongomela!

No lukumyo lwayo lukuwomba

nhe mikumbo ye maluwa yono wavwalile mu mitwe yawo,

iwalongola wang'wela wo wujimbi.

I mitwe yawo yo widunhumuzi, mhola,

webace mafuta ge mhunjile swanu ninga i wanhu weneco,

walabalaye hasi so cigalo co wujimbi.

2 Ninga 'gwe, walekaje, hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yena yunji mono yuli vilungo,

we citung'ati, mono kowahoma.

Yuyo, kokuza nho mukukunzo we mvula ye mabwe,

nho mukukunzo we malenga menji,

nago goyigubika yisi yose.

3 No widunhumuzi we walongola wang'wela,

wo 'jimbi, wokanyanjizwa hasi.

4 No lukumyo lono luwombile lwe walongola wedunhumuzi,

lokwajililila lukulu,

hambi nha lolagala nhe malimbuko ge mana go mukuyu,

gono wagalizaga na lono gakali napililicila.

5 Ninga lizuwa lyokuza,

lyono Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga kolowa

nho mukumbo we maluwa we nhojo kuli wanhu wakwe wono wasigaye.

6 Kolowapela mahala go wuling'anizi

kuli wose wono wena mulimo wo wuhakimu.

Kanyhi kolowapela vilungo,

wose wono wakwituma mulimo wo wulindilizi we milango ye likaya,

ho lilece kuhomwa ne walugu.

Isaya ne Wawizi wagalo 'jimbi wa ku Yuda

7 Sunga ne wawizi, hamonga ne wakohani,

wose wakupebesuka so ngholwe yo wujimbi.

Hambi nha wang'wa muno wujimbi,

nhondo sunga wakolwa,

weza kwikobala hamba kumanya yaye.

I wakohani wagalile muno muno,

sunga luno, weza si wakuwumanya no wugubulilwe,

wono Mulungu yakuwagubulila.

Nawo wakohani nhavivyo,

weza wang'wela muno wo wujimbi,

sunga nhondo weza kulemwa no kulamula vyalo vyono wakuletelwa.

8 Ne zimeza zono wakwitumamila

lono wakung'wa wujimbi wawo,

nha zimemile madeko du,

hasina hamba hanji haswanu hono hasigaye.

9 Sunga nalu, wakung'ung'ulicila wakutya,

“Alu, mwene nye, nani 'lo mono yamunozize munhu yuyu,

yehwese nha vyono mwene kosuma kwijiza munhu yunji?

Hamba nani mono yakuzisaka zinghani zakwe?

Hambi zinghani zakwe,

nha ziwanojeye i wana wagalika du,

wono wakali kongha!

10 Hambi nha yakujela kucijiza ase nha wana wadodo,

nghani kwa nghani, lucilili kwa lucilili,

lisomo kwe 'somo hanyuma aci na aci.”

11 Ninga mulanjilile!

Hono mulece kuhulika 'ne zono nghulonga,

u Mulungu kowetumamila i wajenzi,

walongane nanye mu nonga njenzi ho wamufundenyi.

12 Hambi Mulungu nha yamupelenyi anye wose wucehe,

hamonga no wumoteswe,

ninga anye mukalema kumuhulika.

13 Na yiyi yo yikuleka Muwaha Mulungu

yakumwijizenyi nghani kwa nghani,

lucilili kwa lucilili, lisomo kwe 'somo.

Nanye ho muwe kwikobazanga magulu lono mukujenda.

Na mu wikobaze wenyu,

motuga nghomele, mogwizwa,

na hanyuma musolwe nha muli wafungwa'

Libwe lye nhuzi so Sayuni

14 Ale ta, muhulicize zono Muwaha Mulungu yakumulonjelenyi,

anye nyo wanhu wedunhumuzi,

mono muwatemile wanhu wangu mu Yelusalemi.

15 Hambi 'nye nha mukwilumba mukutya

vyono mwakondya kwilumbaniza ne lifwa,

hamonga ne yisi ye wafu.

Na kanyhi mwitumbiliye lukulu

nha vyono wongomele si wolomuzizenyi yaye,

vyono anye mwitumbiye wulambi wenyu hamonga no wukoci wenyu,

mukutya wuwo wo womuponyenyi.

16 Ninga 'lu, muhulicize zono Muwaha Mulungu

yakumulonjelenyi yakutya,

“Alu nane nghuwika mu Sayuni musinji wono wuli vilungo,

kanyhi wukamile.

Kanyhi nghuwika ne libwe lye nhuzi lyo wuguzi wukamu,

lyono lyowa lyandicigwe zinghani azi,

‘Wihuwilo wono wukamile, wina no wukangalizi.’

17 “No wiling'ano, wowa wo mulejehe wo kujelela musinji wuwo,

nawo wihuwilwa cowa co cijelelo cayo co wima.”

Ne mvula ye mabwe yolowuwusiza lukulu wutizizo we walambi wose,

nalyo lihwazi lyolohuluma mucanyha yo wihundiko wawo liwuhodanje.

18 Ne cilagane cenyu cono mwali mwalagana ne lifwa,

hamonga ne yisi ye wafu, vyose vyowembulwa,

hamba si vyokwima yaye.

Ne lihuhuji kolo lyeza kuza, lyolomusumenyi.

19 Lyolomuzizenyi kali kenji nhondo zose hakali mitondo.

Nanye mowa munojeye muwe no wukangalizi misi ne cilo.

Ne nghani yo yose yono yolawa kuli Mulungu,

yowa no wogofye wuwaha!

20 Hambi nha mowa mwihwanile mbazi yo munhu

mono yakujela kugona mu citanda cifupi muno,

cono si yakusuma hamba kutambaliza ne migulu yakwe,

no kwifunika bulanjeti lisisili lyono si likukwila

hamba kumugubika muwili wakwe wose.

21 Ninga mumanye nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu kolewana,

nha vyono yetuma kwi'tunda lya Pelazimu,

na mwi'lolo lya Gibeyoni.

Ho yetume zono yakuhila mweneco.

Yetume metumo gakwe majenzi go wizine nha vyono gokwijela.

Kanyhi kofisiliza litumo lyakwe, lyo wizine.

22 Holeka mulece kuwubedegala

wusugule wangu wono nghumusugulenyi!

Na mbeka hono muwubedegale,

mumanye nha vyono kowa kukamu muno kuli'nye ku wupone.

Hambi 'ne nha nakondya kuzihulika nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu,

koyongomeza yisi yose.

Lihala lyo Mulungu

23 Nalu, muhulicize ta zono nghulonga,

nghutya nhavi, “Muzitejelezaje viswanu zono nghumulonjelenyi.”

24 Hambi nha hasina mulimi mono kalimaga cibitilila

no kuwikaniza mugunda wakwe ku wupanhe.

25 Langa kolo yawuwikaniza yakondya,

kahadikaga lulo baho du ne zimbeyu ze zimboga,

nha vyono nandala, ne binzali ne jila.

Kanyhi kahadikaga wuhemba ne shayili mu micilili.

Hanyuma kanyhi ho kawa kuhadika

vilimwa vinji vinji kawa kuvipilimya

mu mwalambalo wo mugunda wakwe.

26 Kanyhi mwene yakuwa yamanyile

nha vyono yimunojeye yetume mulimo wakwe,

hambi Mulungu nha yakondya kumwijiza.

27 Kanyhi hamba si kazikung'unhaga

na nhumbi nandala zakwe hamba jila,

ho ziwuswe mu mabada gayo,

langa ketumamilaga du nghome yono yikwilaniciye.

28 Hamba wuhemba nawo si kowupondanga caka caka du mu cuga,

langa kowutowa nha vyono yinojeye hamba si kowusajinhya yaye.

29 Ninga wumanye nha vyono,

mahala gaga gose,

galawaga kuli Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.

Ne mahweso gakwe go ge cihalahala muno muno,

kanyhi nhondo zose gena cambuzi ciwaha.


Isaya 29

Zimbali zono zoyagana Yelusalemi

1 Woyi hee, kuli likaya lya Yelusalemi,

lyono co cikumbicilo co Mulungu!

Woyi hee 'likaya lyono Dawudi yasita nghambi!

Ale, mulece mwaka monga,

hamba myejete yipulute,

nalyo liwe lijendeleye ne zisikukuu zalyo.

2 Ninga hanyuma Mulungu koliletela

wongomele likaya lilyo lyono lyacemwaga,

“I cikumbicilo co Mulungu.”

Na mu mapango gago, colowa cililo no wukalale,

ne likaya lyose lyolokwihwana cikumbicilo

cono cihiliwe sakami cose.

3 Nayo Mulungu kololihoma likaya,

yalipilime, yalizinjilize mbavu zose.

4 Nalyo likaya lya Yelusalemi,

lyolowa kutakanya hasi nho munhu mono yena mhepo,

no wulonje walyo,

wolowa wa wupwepwe wono wukulawilila baho hasi mu manghundi.

5 Ninga nago mapuga ge wajenzi i walugu wako,

golopemheluswa ne mbeho nhe manghundi.

Ne mapuga gawo ge wasilikale gono gakogofya,

golopemheluswa nhinicizo nhe mawaje.

6 Ninga u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

kolomuponyenyi anye mu mbali ze madidimo,

hamonga na ze matulo ge'si.

Mulungu kololeta libeho lyo mukukunzo,

hamonga no moto we malambi mawaha,

7 no moto wuwo wolowongomeza wasilikale

wose we makungugo gose

gono gakulihoma likaya lya Yelusalemi,

likaya lye cikumbicilo co Mulungu.

Wolokongomezwa hamonga ne vitwalo vyawo vyose vyo wulugu,

hamonga ne vinhu vyawo vyose vyono wena navyo.

Vyose vyolokwajilila lukulu nha ndoto,

vyowa nhe vinhu vyono vyali kwihweswa du ne cilo.

8 Ne makungugo gose gono golowa gekongholeye

hamonga ho galewane ne likaya lya Yelusalemi,

golokwihwana no munhu mono yadabuce,

alu yakulota yakulya ndigwa,

ninga kumbe kolo yakusinhuka mu zinhongo,

yakwagana nzala vyo yikutama du.

Hamba lunji nho munhu mono yena nyhilu muno muno ye malenga,

alu yakulota yakung'wa malenga,

ninga kumbe kolo yakusinhuka mu zinhongo,

yakwagana nyhilu ye malenga vyo yimwibate du.

Wu wula kucelelela kuli zo wusugulwe

9 Na hono mwipele ku wulele nyo weneco,

na mbeka mocila kuwa walele!

Na hono mwipele ku wubofu nyo weneco,

na mbeka mojendelela kuwa wabofu!

Na mokwijela mugalile,

ninga kumbe ho si muwung'weye no 'jimbi yaye.

Kanyhi mokwijela mukupebesuka-pebesuka,

ninga kumbe ho si muwujezize bwete no wujimbi.

10 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu

nha kamupelenyi nhongo ze lifwefwe,

ho mugone hawaha.

Kanyhi yali yiwanojeye wawizi wawe go meso ge wanhu,

ninga Mulungu yakondya kuwabofuza meso gawo.

11 Ne zinhunizo zo wugubulilwe wose wo wuwizi, wowa muhundikwe kuli'nye. Wowa nhe zinghani ze citabu cono cigubikwe kanyhi cinamatizwe ne cinamatizo.
12 Na hono mumupele munhu mono si yamanyile kusoma, mumutye yamusomelenyi, nayo ko mupitulenyi nha vyono mwene hamba si yamanyile yaye kusoma.

13 Ninga nayo Muwaha Mulungu yalonga yakatya,

“I wanhu wawa, wenozaga nha wakuhemba nene,

ninga kumbe ne zinghani zawo ho zisina nhunizo yo yose,

ne zinhumbula zawo zili kunji caka.

Ne cogopo cawo ca kuli'ne nghani ya bwete du,

hambi wene nha wakutyatya wulajilize we wanhu,

hamonga ne myoto yono wayizoweye

nha vyono wakondya kwiyijiza.

14 Ninga nane nowatowa nhinicizo kali kenji.

Na muli wanhu wawo we mahala,

wolelepa, nawo wasugu,

no wusugu wawo wowa wa bwete du.”

Witumbilo wa mu vilo vyono vikuza

15 Ninga woyi hee,

kuli wanhu wono wakujela kumuhundika Muwaha Mulungu mahweso gawo!

Wakuzinoza zinghani zawo cinyele akuno wakwihwesa

nha vyono hasina yunji mono yakuwawona yaye,

hamba kuzimanya zono wakwituma ng'o.

16 Wakuzipitulanya zinghani.

Alu mwene u muwumbi we nyungu ne lilongo,

ca hayi ciwaha?

Hamba cinhuci cono munhu yacinozize cono kanyhi comutya,

“Agwe yo si gwegwe mono nozize?”

Hamba lunji cimutye,

“Agwe hamba si wumanyile yaye na cono wukwituma?”

17 Na zizo lono zowa zikali zijendeleye kulongwa,

cidaha cidodo du, mbago mbaha yogaluka mugunda,

nawo mugunda kanyhi wowa kwijela nha mbago mbaha.

18 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo kolo lyaza,

i we ciziwi wolohulika kolo citabu cikusomwa kwe'zi yiwaha.

Nawo wono wali cibofu,

wono wekalaga mu wutitu,

meso gawo golonugula wasume kuwona.

19 Nawo i waciwa, hamonga ne wanhu wenenenyhezi,

wolotuga kamonga nyemo yono Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu mono yelile we Waisilayeli kalavyaga.

20 Na mwizuwa lilyo lyolowa lyo lizuwa

lyo mudumo kuli wanhu wono wendile kuwewonela wayawo,

no kumubedegala Mulungu.

Ne wanyo wubi wose, wolokongomezezwa lukulu.

21 Mulungu kolowongomeza wanhu wose

wono wendile kuwasonjeza-sonjeza wayawo.

Hamonga na wono wendile kuwahijila i wabeno lajilize,

na wono wendile kulonga zo wulambi

ho wawapamanjize iwanhu

we ling'ani walece kunozezwa i ling'ani yawo.

22 Holeka nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

mono yamuponya Abulahamu mu magayo,

yakutya, nhavi, “Alu anye nyo wanhu wangu,

hamba si molokozwa cevu kanyhi,

sunga no wusu wenyu si wolosininghalila lukulu kanyhi so cevu.

23 Nanye kolo mwawawona wana wenyu wono nolomupelenyi,

nhondo ho mulamanyila vyono,

ane yo nene Mulungu mono nelile,

u Mulungu wa Isilayeli.

Nanye baho ho mulamhelela lilumbo no kwikala mu cogopo cangu.

24 Ne wanhu walele nawo wolotuga wizuce.

Ne wanhu wono wekalaga wakung'ung'ulika nhondo zose,

nawo wolokwenda kulanjizwa.”


Isaya 30

Wilumbanize ne yisi ya Misili wono wusina cambuzi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“I walongola wono watemile mu yisi ya Yuda,

wose wali mu wongomezwe du,

hambi nha wambedegala'ne.

Wene 'mbi nha wakwituma

zono zili mu mahweso gawo du,

zono ane si niwakomboye wazitume.

Kanyhi wakwilumbaniza no wilumbanize

wono ane si niwakomboye wawunoze.

Wakucila konjeleza wubi mucanyha yo wubi wunji.

2 Wakubita kulondola wutazi ku Misili,

akuno si wamuye kumbuza 'ne.

Ati wakusaka yisi ya Misili yo yiwadime,

na ku soko yiyo holeka 'lu,

wamwitumbila muno u mutemi wa Misili.

3 Ninga yuyo mutemi wa Misili,

kowa yasina vilungo vyo kuwataziza yaye.

Hambi u wutazi wono wolawa kuko ku Misili,

nha womalicila du mu wongomele wuwaha muno.

4 Hamba wawe wawaha we yisi ya Misili,

wakondya kwinjila mu makaya ga Zowani na Hanesi,

gono gali mu wutemi wa Misili,

5 ninga i wanhu wa Yuda wolokalala

vyono wali wetumbiye likungugo

lyono si lyowaletela cambuzi,

langa likuwagwisa du,

hamba kuwapela wutazi nha vyono wali walanjiliye.”

6 Ayi nayo yo nghani yo Mulungu kuli vilimu

vyose vyono vikaye mu nyika

yo lubavu lwa kutakama, yakutya,

“I wawaha wa Yuda, wajendaga muhinzo mutali,

wakukola mu yisi yono yina mbali nyinji,

i hanhu hono gekalaga masimba,

mono mogali mazoka gono gena sumu nghali muno,

hamonga ne mazoka gono gagulukaga.

Wakukola mumo akuno ho wapaciye ne vinhu

vyo wuguzi wukamu muno

mu zindogowe na mu zingamiya zawo,

ho walitegulile vipegwa likungugo

lyono si lyosuma hamba kuwapela wutazi wo wose yaye.

7 Hambi Misili,

nha yisina wutazi wo wose hamba wudodo bwete.

Si holeka nane nghayicemela

yiyo Misili litagwa lya fumbo,

lyono likutya, ‘Nzoka yono yisina Sumu.’ ”

Wanhu wono wasina cihuliko

8 Mulungu yakanonjela nizandice

mu citabu ze wanhu vyono wenha,

ho hawe ne cikumbucizo ca nhondo zose

cono cikulajila zo wubi wawo vyono zinha.

9 Hambi wene nha wasina cihuliko kuli Mulungu nhondo zose.

Wekalaga wakulamba du,

hamba si wendile no kugebata mejizo go Muwaha Mulungu yaye.

10 Wakuwalonjela wawizi wanyamale twii,

wakutya, “Anye nyo wawizi,

mulece kucilonjela za hayi ze ling'ani.

Langa mweze kucilonjela

zono na sese cosaka cizihulice.

Mucilekaje 'se cijendelele no wukoci wetu.

11 Holeka muwuce mu nzila yetu,

hamba mulece kucimila mu wukolo wetu.

Nase hamba si cikusacila lukulu kuzihulika

zinghani zo Mulungu wenyu,

u Mulungu wa Isilayeli.”

12 Ninga 'lu, u Mulungu mono yelile,

u Mulungu wa Isilayeli yakutya, nhavi,

“Alu vyono mwakondya kuzibedegala zono nghumulonjelenyi,

nanye mwakondya kuwetumbila wawo wanhu wagazi kanyhi walambi.

13 Nane nghumutyenyi vyono muli wasutyi.

Alu mwehwana ne lijinji litali muno,

lyono lina lizatu, lyono lidemuce ku wulawa kucanyha sunga ho hasi.

Nanye mogwa hasi nhinicizo.

14 Nanye motulanjika mwiyagale

nhe nyungu yono yitulice vibi no kuzaganzika,

hawe hasina hamba cizaye cidodo cono cosigala,

cono cokwila hamba kupalila moto bwete,

hamba kukupila kunze malenga gono gali mwi'kandilo.”

15 Kanyhi Muwaha Mulungu we Mihanga,

mono yelile, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

yakuwalonjela wanhu yakutya,

“Ale mumbuyilaje wose, kanyhi munyitumbile,

nanye ho mowa ne vilungo,

kanyhi mowa mupolile.”

Ninga nye mukulema!

16 Ati langa mukusaka mwiponye

nyo weneco kuli walugu wenyu,

mu nzila yo kupanda zifwalasi zenyu mumanyhe muno, muwatize.

Heya vyo yinha, hambi nye nha mukusaka mwiponye du!

Mukwihwesa vyono zifwalasi zenyu zowa manyhi kuciza.

Ninga mumanye nha vyono,

wawo i walugu wenyu wono wakumutyatililenyi mu nyuma,

wowa wamanyhi muno muno kumucizenyi nyenye.

17 Kanyhi mumanye vyono muli nyenye,

wanhu elufu monga,

womutiza musilikale yumonga du we walugu wenyu,

kolo wakumuwona yakuza.

Holeka ku soko yiyo,

wasilikale wahano du we walugu,

womukwilenyi kumunozenyi anye wose mumanyhe no kutiza.

Kanyhi howa hasina cinji

co cose cono mosigazizwa mu wasilikale wenyu,

langa libici lyo kutumbicila bendela

lyo lyosigala lili wene du mwi'tunda mucanyha!

18 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

mwene Muwaha Mulungu wo lusungu kowa

vyo yakali yakumuwonelenyi lusungu du.

Kanyhi yewikanize ho yamuwonelenyi lusungu,

hambi mwene nhondo zose nha ketumaga ze ling'ani.

Holeka wamoti wono wamwitumbiye u Muwaha Mulungu.

Mulungu kowamotya wanhu wakwe

19 Nanye nyo wanhu mono mwikaye mu Yelusalemi, hamba si mololila kanyhi yaye. Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha komululenyi, na kolo mukumulilila mukusaka wutazi kuli mwene, nayo komutazenyi.
20 U Muwaha Mulungu komukozenyi mu magayo, ninga kanyhi mweneco kowa yali hamonga nanye ho yamwijizenyi, nanye hamba si momulondola yaye.
21 Nanye kolo mukupatuka mu nzila no kubita lubavu lo kulume hamba lo kucekulu, molihulika lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu mu nyuma yenyu likutya, “Ayi yo nzila. Muyityatyaje yiyo.”
22 Nanye movisola vihwani vyenyu vyose vyono vihiliwe fweza, hamonga na vyono vihiliwe zahabu, muwe kuvitaga kutali, nha cinhu ca cikomaza, akuno mukuzoza mukutya, “Wuce ha meso gangu!”
23 Nanye kolo mwahadika zimbeyu mu migunda yenyu, Muwaha Mulungu komutonyezezenyi mvula ho zicindice, no kumuletelenyi nhwanga mbaha muno. Ne mitugo zenyu zowa no wudimo winji muno.
24 Nazo zing'ombe hamonga ne zindogowe zono zikumulimilenyi mu migunda yenyu, zowa kulya mahanze makavu maswanu muno.
25 Na mwi'zuwa lyono golokumulwa magomawakali ge walugu wenyu, ne wanhu wawo wakuwulagwa, mwi'zuwa lilyo, vikolongo vye malenga vyolohuluma vikulawilila mucanyha ye matunda gose na mu vigongo vyose.
26 Nawo mwezi wolong'alala nhe lizuwa. Nalyo lizuwa lyolong'alala wung'alale wuwaha muno kali mupunghati kuciza wung'alale walyo we vilo vyose, lyolotowelela wung'alale wa vilo mupunghati mwi'zuwa lilyo limonga. Na zizi zolokwijela mwizuwa lyono Muwaha Mulungu yeza kuwawopa bandeji wanhu wakwe, mu zinghomele zono yawapela, hamonga no kuwaponya.

Mulungu koyilipiciza yisi ya Ashuli

27 Nalo lukumyo, hamonga ne vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu,

vijelelaga kutali.

Nawo moto hamonga ne lyosi,

vilajilaga likuwo lyakwe.

Mwene kalongaga,

ne zinghani zakwe zikuwa kunyanya nho moto.

28 Mwene kayilongozaga mbeho ku mwande yakwe,

yiwa nhe lihwazi lye malenga,

yiwa kuvikwanghwaniza vyose vyono yikwagana.

Nayo yigafwagulizaga makungugo,

yiwa kugongomeza, yiwa kugadumya ne mahweso gawo mabi.

29 Ninga anye nyo wanhu wo Mulungu, molosangalala muno no kwimba nha vyono munozaga mu cilo ce sikukuu yono yelile. Kanyhi molosangalala nha wono wakujenda no mulilo we zifuluti, wakubita ku Nyumba yo Mulungu, u Muwaha Mulungu, u mudimi wa Isilayeli.
30 No Muwaha Mulungu kolowanoza wanhu wose walihulice lizi lyakwe lye nhojo, waliwone ne likuwo lyakwe vyono linha. Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, kolowa lilambi lyo moto hamonga no mukukunzo we mvula ye mabwe.
31 Nawo Waashuli, wolokwinjilwa no woga wuwaha muno kolo wakulihulika lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu no kuliwona likuwo lyakwe lyo kulipiciza.
32 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu lono yakuwatowa kali kenji, i wanhu wakwe wolowa wajendeleye no wutowa ng'oma ne masumbi. Hambi nha Mulungu du yali wene mono kolowahoma Waashuli.
33 Ne likombo lyo moto muwaha, lyakondya kuwikanizwa ku wulawa katali, i hanhu hono kolotakamizizwa u mutemi wa Ashuli. Ne likombo lilyo yitali, kanyhi yigazi muno, nalyo limemile lilundo yiwaha lya ngwagu. Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kololyehelela, no mwehelo wakwe wolowa malambi ga moto, gono gololikoleleza moto likombo lilyo.


Isaya 31

Mulungu koloyidima Yelusalemi

1 Woyi hee, kuli wose wono wabitaga kulondola wutazi ku Misili.

Hambi nha wetumbiye vilungo

vyo winji we wasilikale wa Misili,

nha cinji, zifwalasi ne matuka go wulugu,

hamonga ne wasilikale.

Ninga hamba si wamwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli,

hamba si wakumulomba yawapele wutazi.

2 Ninga mwene Mulungu,

yamanyile zono yakwituma!

Mwene kaletaga wongomele,

kanyhi kawalajilaga cogofyo cakwe

mu nzila yo kuwalipiciza i wanhu wabi,

hamonga na wono wakuwacitila.

3 Ninga Wamisili nha wanhu du,

hamba si milungu yaye.

Sunga ne zifwalasi zawo nha nimu du,

hamba si muhe yaye.

Na lono Muwaha Mulungu yakwituma,

likungugo lye vilungo lyolotijinhwa,

nalyo likungugo lyono lisoceye lyono likutazwa, lyologwa.

Nago gose vyono mejete, wolokongomezwa.

4 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,

“Nawo wadimi hamba wazoze vyono wozoza,

yowa nghani ya bwete du,

hamba si wosumila lukulu kulyogofya lisimba,

ho liyilecele nimu yono liyiwulaje.

Holeka nane yo nhavivyo,

hasina cinhu co cose cono cosuma kunyimila ane

no Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

ho ndece kulidima litunda lya Sayuni.

5 Nha vyono ndeje yipapatikaga hacanyha ye linhuli

lyono lina mana gayo ho yigadime,

ale nane no Muwaha Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose,

vyo noyidima Yelusalemi kanyhi noyicitila no kuyikamisa.”

6 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, mwakondya kunhendela wubi, hamonga no kumbedegala. Ninga aluno muwuye, mumbuyile nene!
7 Ninga lizuwa lyokuza, lono anye wose molovitajila lukulu i vihwani vyose vye fweza na vye zahabu, u wubi wono mwenozeza nyenye nyo weneco kwe miwoko yenyu.

8 Nayo yisi ya Ashuli,

yolokongomezezwa lukulu mu wulugu,

ninga si kwe vilungo vyo munhu yaye.

Nawo Waashuli wolotiza ku wulugu wuwo.

Ne wazelelo wawo, wolonozwa wawanda.

9 Nayo mutemi wawo kolotiza so woga.

Nawo wetumami wakwe,

wolokwinjilwa no woga wuwaha,

wolotiza wazilece ne zibendela zawo zo wulugu.”

Muwaha Mulungu vyo yakutya,

u Muwaha Mulungu mono yakuwa kuhembwa mu Yelusalemi,

mono no moto wakwe wakaga mumo so makumbiko.


Isaya 32

U mutemi mwihuwilwa

1 Na mwizuwa linji kolotema

u mutemi mono kolowa mwihuwilwa,

ne wawaha wa mu'si,

nawo wolotema mu nzila yo wiling'ano.

2 Nawo wawo wose,

wolowa nho wihundiko so mbeho,

kanyhi wolowa nho wicito so mukukunzo.

Kanyhi wolowa nhe vikolongo vye malenga,

vyono vikuhuluma mu nyika.

Kanyhi wolowa nho mubeho muwaha we libwe liwaha,

lyono lili mu yisi yono yilecigwe.

3 Ne meso gawo golowa gawalanjile wanhu,

nago makutu gawo golowa gahulicize,

ho gahulice visakwa vye wanhu.

4 Hamba si wolowa wali musunho-musunho yaye,

langa wolowa kwituma no kulonga zono wezuce ne zinhunizo zayo.

5 Na mwizuwa lilyo, u munhu mulele,

si kolopegwa lilumbo yaye.

Hamba munhu musugu-sugu nayo,

si kotegwa mwihuwilwa ng'o.

6 Hambi u munhu mulele,

nha kalongaga zinghani zo wulele du,

kanyhi kekalaga yakwihwesa ku wituma zimbi du.

Na zose zono ketumaga,

na zono kalongaga,

zose zibitile ku wutovula Muwaha Mulungu du.

Kanyhi hamba si kawapelaga ndigwa wono wali nzala,

hamba kuwapela malenga wono wena nyhilu.

7 No munhu mono yasina mahala,

kawezaga munhu mubi,

kanyhi yendile kwituma zimbi du,

mwene yendile kupinya zo kuwongomeza

waciwa ne zinghani zakwe zo wulambi,

no kuwemila ho walece kunozezwa ling'ani.

8 Ninga u munhu we lilumbo,

ketumaga zo mugoloka,

kanyhi kawezaga yakamile mu mbeka.

Mejizo kuli Wacekulu wa mu Yelusalemi

9 Nanye nyo wacekulu mono mwikaye mwilejehile,

musina mahwele go gose,

ale muhulicize 'ne zono nghulonga.

10 Heya aluno mokwiguta,

ninga ku mwaka mu mapango gaga,

molowa mu mahwele,

soko ndudula ye zizabibu yolowa ndodo.

Hamba si molotuga zabibu zono zikwiliye.

11 Hambi nha mwali

mwizoweleze kwikala mwilejehile du,

musina mahwele.

Ninga luno, mucicime so woga!

Mugazice anye nyo wacekulu wetagalici!

Vulenyi myenda yenyu,

mwitunje magunila mu viwino vyenyu.

12 Mwitowe mu mhambaga zenyu ko wukalale,

ku soko vyono i migunda yenyu yo moso,

hamonga ne migunda yenyu ye zabibu,

yose yahodanjika,

13 alu vititika vye mamivya hamonga ne mabijili,

vyakondya kutita mu yisi ye wanhu wangu.

Heya, mulile ku soko ye zikaya zono

wekalaga wanhu wangu wena nyemo,

na so likaya lyono lyali limemile wusangalale.

14 Sunga ne kaya ye likulu, yololekwa,

hamonga ne likaya lyayo,

vyose vyololecelwa lukulu.

Nago makaya hamonga

ne magomawakali gayo gono gawacitilaga,

gose golokumulwangwa gawe matongo cibitilila.

Nago madogowe ga mu mbago,

gowa kupilima pilima mumo,

zingholo nazo zokwitujila wudimo mumo.

15 Ninga hanyuma kanyhi,

u Mulungu kolocijidila muhe wakwe.

Ne yisi yono yali yeza magungu,

nhondo yologaluka yiwe yisi yono yina moso muwaha.

Nayo migunda, nhondo yolofisa vilimwa viswanu muno.

16 Nawo wiling'ano, hamonga ne ling'ani,

nhondo vyolokwitumwa mu yisi yose.

17 Vyono mu mapango gago,

wanhu wose wolowa kwituma ze ling'ani du.

Kanyhi kolowa na mulamu no wutuvu hamonga no witumbilo cibitilila.

18 Nawo i wanhu wo Mulungu

wolowa kwikala wasina kanyhi mahwele.

Na mu zikaya zawo, wolowa wena mulamu kanyhi wapolile.

19 Ninga mvula ye mabwe yolotonya mu mbago,

ne likaya lyologwiswa ho hasi.

20 Mwene lulo!

Wanhu wolowa wena nyemo mbaha,

ku soko ye malenga menji ku so vilimwa vyawo,

hamonga no wudimilo wuswanu hanhu hose,

so zindogowe hamonga ne zing'ombe zawo.


Isaya 33

Kulomba wutazi

1 Woyi he kuli'nye nyo walugu wetu!

Hambi nye nha mwagaluka kanyhi mukahiza.

Ninga anye hasina mono

yamugalukajenyi hamba kumuhizizenyi yaye.

Ninga zinghani zenyu zizo zo wugaluci,

hamonga no wuhizi,

vyose vyoloduma lwa kamonga du.

Hambi nanye nha molowa kwihiziza no kwikonga nyenye wose.

2 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuciwonele lusungu.

Hambi ase, nha cikwitumbiye gwegwe du.

Weze kucikamisa vilo vyose,

kanyhi weze kuciponya mu vilo vye magayo.

3 Na lono wukucilewanila,

i makungugo gakutiza ku soko ye lizozo lyo wulugu.

4 Ne vinhu vyawo vikupumhwa no kusolwa mateka.

5 Lanjenyi ta u wuwaha wo Muwaha Mulungu vyono winha!

Hambi mwene nha yo mutemi we vinhu vyose.

Mwene koyimemya Yelusalemi wihuwilwa hamonga ne ling'ani.

6 Kanyhi koyipela wusikame mu nghungugo.

Kanyhi kowa kuwadima wanhu wakwe nhondo zose,

no kuwapela mahala hamonga no wizuce.

Ne cogopo co Muwaha Mulungu,

yolowa yo sawo yawo mbaha.

7 Lanjenyi ta,

i we citung'ati wo wakulila wakulondola wutazi.

Nawo wetumami wono wajelaga kuleta mulamu,

wakulila ko wusungu wuwaha muno.

8 Ne zinzila zimbaha, zose zeza zina mbali,

sunga ne wanhu weza si wakukola mu zinzila zizo kanyhi.

Wilumbanize, hamonga ne cilagane,

vyakondya kubenwa.

Heza hasina hamba munhu monga

kanyhi mono yakuvitonganiza yaye.

9 Alu yisi yakondya kulekwa, nayo yikusocela.

Ne mbago mbaha ya Lebanoni, yanyala.

Sunga ne lilolo lya Shaloni lyo moso mwinji,

nalyo lyeza nha nyika.

Namo mu Bashani na mwi'tunda lya Kalimeli,

nhondo ne mahamha geza kukung'unhika mu mabici gayo.

U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwasugula i walugu wakwe

10 Muwaha Mulungu yakugalonjela makungugo gose yakutya,

“Ale nokwituma, ho nilajile vyono ane nina vilungo.

11 Hambi anye,

nha mukwihwesa mahweso gono gasina cambuzi,

na zose zono mukwituma, mukugaya bwete du.

Ninga mumanye vyono,

u muhe wangu wihwanile nha moto wono womongomezenyi.

12 Nanye molofugunhika nhe mabwe gono

wakuwa kuganyanya i wanoza swakala,

mowa nhe mamivya gono ganyanywa gatakamila gawa mavu.

13 Holeka muwalekaje wanhu wose

wono wali behi na wono wali kutali,

wazihulice ze metumo gangu,

ho wavimanye vilungo vyangu vyono vinha.”

14 Ne wanhu wabi wa ku Sayuni,

wose wakucicima so woga, akuno wakutya,

“Mbeka ning'aniza yo Mulungu

yihwanile no moto wono wunyanyaga cibitilila.

Alu mukwihwesa,

yunji wa muli sese kosuma kupona mu moto wuwo?”

15 Ninga yene,

kosuma kupona hono wuwe wuli

mwituma ze ling'ani no kulonga ze ling'ani.

Holeka wulece kwitumamila wuwaha

wono wina nawo ho wuwakonje-konje waciwa,

hamba wulece kubocela mafupo.

Kanyhi wulecele lukulu kwilunza

ne wanhu wono wehwesaga zo wuwulaji,

hamba kwituma zimbi.

16 Na mbeka hono wulece kwituma zimbi zizo,

nagwe kokwikala wupolile.

Kanyhi kowa wuli mu wucitilwe wuwaha muno,

nha kotya wuli mugati ye magomawakali

gono gakamile muno muno.

Nagwe kowa ne ndigwa yo kulya,

hamonga ne malenga go kung'wa.

U lukumyo lwa mu vilo vyono vikuza

17 Hanyuma kanyhi molomuwona mutemi

mono kolowa yatemile mu nhojo mbaha,

mu yisi yono yitandaye mu zimbavu zose.

18 No woga wenyu wono mwali mwina baho katali

wo kutowezwa kodi ne wajenzi,

hamonga ne watandizi,

vivyo vyose movisemwela lukulu.

19 Kanyhi hamba si molowawonela

lukulu i wajenzi wedunhumuzi,

wono walongaga nonga yono si muyimanyile yaye.

20 Ale, mulanje ta ku Sayuni,

likaya lyono ho hanhu hono canozezaga zi sikukuu zetu.

Muyilangaje ta Yelusalemi!

Baho howa ho hanhu du hono cosuma kwikala ko mulamu!

Hambi nha howa hehwanile lihema

lyono si lilawuswa yaye,

lyono sunga ne zimambo zalyo,

si zilasumika hamba hadodo kuwukulwe.

Hamba ne milejehe yalyo si yiladumuka yaye.

21 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu

nha kolocilajila lukumyo lwakwe.

Nase colokwikala mu zinyenje ze

makolongo magazi hamonga ne vikolongo.

Na mumo si zolokola ne zingalawa ze walugu yaye.

22 Ne milejehe yose ya mu zingalawa zizo, yitimbile du, ne matote gayo hamba si gakugoloka yaye!

23 Holeka nase cosuma kuyisola

sawo yose ye wasilikale we walugu wetu.

Nase cowa cina sawo nyinji yono

cosuma kuwagawila sunga na wono wali citewe.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu mweneco

du yo kolowa mutemi wetu.

Yo kolocitema no kucidima.

24 Ne wanhu wose wono cowa cikalane

nawo mu yisi yetu,

hasina hamba monga mono kolo kwing'ulanza yawe kutya yatamwa,

ne wanhu wose wono wokwikala mumo, wolonenelwa wubi wawo.


Isaya 34

Mulungu kolowalipiciza i walugu wakwe

1 Alu muze nye nyo wanhu we makungugo gose!

Mwihangulize hamonga muhulicize.

Nagwe go yinze,

hamonga ne wanhu wose mono mwikaye mumo,

muze muhulicize.

2 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha yawakoleywe

likuwo makungugo gose,

hamonga ne wasilikale wawo wo wulugu.

Nayo yakondya kuwawalila wongomezwe.

3 Kanyhi ne mivimba yawo,

si yolozikwa yaye,

langa yololekwa yiwole no kulavya munungho mubi.

Ne matunda gose, golodunghuwala so sakami.

4 Nalyo lizuwa no mwezi,

hamonga ne zinyelezi,

vyolotulanjika vyose viwe manghundi.

Nawo wulanga woloweya no kupalinjika nha nyhingo mhalinje.

Nazo zinyelezi,

zololagala hasi nhe mahamha gono

gakulagala mu mubici wo muzabibu, hamba mu mukuyu.

5 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakondya

kuwuwikaniza mumaje wakwe kuko ku wulanga,

nawo woyitowa Edomu,

i wanhu wono wawiciywe wongomele.

6 No mumaje wuwo wolokwila sakami ne mhama ye zingholo

ne zimhene zono zikuwa kulavywa makumbiko.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kololavya

makumbiko gago mwi'kaya lya Bozila.

Kowunoza wuwulaji wuwu wuwaha mu yisi ya Edomu.

7 Nawo wanhu wolomalika nhe zimbogo

za mu mbago ne zinghambaku zindodo-ndodo.

Ne yinze yolodunghuwala so sakami,

kanyhi yolomema mhama yose.

8 Nalyo lyolowa lyo lipango lyono Muwaha Mulungu,

koloyiponezya Sayuni, no kwilipiciza kuli walugu wakwe.

9 Ne makolongo ga Edomu,

golohunduswa gawe lami.

Ne manghundi gayo,

gawe libwe lye cibiliti.

Ne yisi yose yologubikwa ne lami.

10 Nayo lami yolowa yigubice mu yisi yose misi ne cilo,

ne malyosi gayo gowa kudoma kucanyha cibitilila.

Nayo yisi yokwikala vyo yitongo du ndelo na ndelo.

Hamba hasina yunji kanyhi mono kolokola mu yisi yiyo.

11 Nago matuwi ne mahowe go golokwikala mu yisi yiyo.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu koloyinoza kanyhi yilece kulela yiwe

nha vyono yali lono yali yikali si nawumbwa.

12 Kanyhi mu vilo vivyo,

holowa hasina mutemi mono koloyitema yisi.

Hambi ne walongola wose, nha wolowa wakondya kuwuka.

13 Nago mamivya hamonga ne matundulu,

go golocindika mu makaya gawo go wutemi,

na mu makaya gono mazenjele magomawakali.

Nago macewe ne matuwi, wolowa wo wikalo wago.

14 Ne malimu ga mu mbago golowa kuyumba-yumba mumo.

Nago mamyuhe mabi, gowa kwicemeleza geneco.

Nalyo lizoka lye cilo,

lyowa kuza mumo likulondola hanhu ha wuceho.

15 Nago matuwi golozenga mumo manhuli gayo,

gataje ne magangha,

gaganghase ne mana gayo,

gagawilusize mumo sunga gaguluce.

Nazo zi'ndi, zolowa kuza monga monga zikwihanguliziza zose mumo.

16 Ale, mulondole-londole mu citabu co Muwaha Mulungu,

cono cikulolosa ze mikalile ye viwumbwa vyumi,

mucisome, muziwone zono zikulongwa mumo.

Hambi nha hasina ciwumbwa hamba cimonga cono colokwajilila.

Na kanyhi hasina hamba cimonga cono cisina muyagwe.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo yazilajiliza ziwe nhavivyo.

Kanyhi yo mweneco du mono kolovikongholela viwumbwa vyose hamonga.

17 Kanyhi yo mweneco du Muwaha Mulungu mono kolovigawila yisi,

no kuvipela hanhu ho kwikala, hanhu na havyo.

Navyo vyokwikala mumo mu yisi, ndelo na ndelo.

Navyo lyowa lye gawo lyavyo sunga cibitilila.


Isaya 35

Nzila yo Wusiwanulwe

1 Hambi nyika nha yosangalala.

Ne maluwa gobonhola mu mbago.

2 Nayo nyika yolokwimba no kusangalala so nyemo,

hambi nha yolowa yanoga yawa swanu

nhe matunda ga Lebanoni,

na kanyhi yowa yina moso nhe migunda ya

Kalimeli na Shaloni.

Ne wanhu wose woloyiwona nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu.

Kanyhi wolowuwona wuwaha ne vilungo vyakwe.

3 Muyipele vilungo miwoko yono yisoceye,

hamonga ne mafugamilo gono gakucicima so wusocele.

4 Muwalonjele wono wena nhumbula nyoga, mutye,

“Ale, mweze ne vilungo, mulece kuwa na woga!

Mulungu kokuza no kumuponyenyi

kokuza yawalipicize walugu wenyu.”

5 Ne wabofu wolonuguzwa meso, wawone,

nawo we ciziwi, wolosuma kuhulika.

6 Nawo we citewe, wolozumha-zumha no kuvina,

nawo wono wali cibwibwi, wolokwimba na nyemo.

Vikolongo vye malenga vyolohuluma mu nyika.

7 No musawawa wono wuli moto,

wolopituka wuwe yilamba,

ne yisi nghavu, yolomema mivwilo ye malenga.

Ne hanhu hono macewe gekalaga,

holocindika mahanze, hamonga ne madete-lulenga.

8 Kanyhi baho holowa na nzila mbaha,

ne nzila yiyo yolocemwa “Nzila yo Wele.”

No munhu mubi si koloyikola nzila yiyo

langa yowa ku soko ye wanhu wono wayityatizye

hamba wawe walele,

ninga si wokwajilila mu nzila yiyo.

9 Ne masimba si golowa mumo yaye

hamba malimu makali, si goloyikola nzila yiyo.

Langa wawo du wono Muwaha Mulungu

yakondya kuwaponya wawo du

wo woloyikola nzila yiyo wakuwuya ku kaya.

10 Nawo wolokwinjila mu Yelusalemi wasangalaye,

wakwimba ne mazi mawaha so nyemo,

wolowa wasangalaye cibitilila,

hambi wukalale no wusungu

nha vyolowucila lukulu muli wene.


Isaya 36

Waashuli wakuyogofya Yelusalemi

(2Wat 18:13-27; 2Zin 32:1-19)

1 Na mu mwaka we likumi na yine, lono Hezekiya yali mutemi wa Yuda, Senakelibu u mutemi wa Ashuli, yakagahoma makaya ga Yuda ge magomawakali, yakagasola.
2 Hanyuma yakamutuma mwitumami wakwe muwaha, hamonga ne lipuga liwaha lye wasilikale, ku wulawa ku Lakishi, wabite ku Yelusalemi, wakamulonjele mutemi Hezekiya, yelavye mweneco kuli Senakelibu. Nayo mwitumami yuyo, yakema behi ne likololozi lye lilamba lya kucanyha, mu nzila ngazi yono yakolelaga ha mugunda wo mukanza myenda.
3 Hobaho Wayuda wadatu wakawa kuza kumuhinjiliza, nawo wali awano. Eliyakimu u mwana wa Hilikiya u mwitumami muwaha wa mwi'kulu, na Shebina u mwandici, hamonga na Yowa u mwana wa Asafu, mono yandikaga zinghomo ze zinghani ze vilo vivilya.

4 Nayo u mwitumami we Ciashuli, yakawalonjela vyono, u mutemi wa Ashuli yakusaka kumanya, cicici cono mutemi Hezekiya yetumbiye.
5 Kanyhi yakutya, “Mwene Hezekiya wukwihwesa wulonje du wuli wene, wo wosuma kwituma mulimo we vilungo we wasilikale? Alo mwene, wukwihwesa nani lo mono kokutaziza, lono agwe weza kuyibedegala yisi ya Ashuli?
6 Ane manyile nha vyono, agwe witumbiye vyono yisi ya Misili yo yokutaza. Ninga wumanye, nha vyono yiyo, yowa nho munhu mono yakusola lidete-lulenga, kawa kujenda yakwisimatiziza, ninga wumanye vyono lidete lilyo lyobeneka likucifugule, mu muwoko wako. Ale, no mutemi wa Misili nayo vyo kowa yenha, hono munhu yunji yamwitumbile.”
7 Nayo yu yulya u mwitumami we Ciashuli yakajendelela kulonga yakatya, “Hamba lunji mononjela nha vyono anye mumwitumbiye Mulungu wenyu? Heya hamba yiwe nhavivyo, ninga si yo yuyu Hezekiya mono yahawusa hanhu hose hono helile ho Muwaha Mulungu, hamonga ne makumbicilo gayo, lono yawalonjelela wanhu wose wa Yuda na Yelusalemi, vyono wose wawe kubita kuhemba ha cikumbicilo cimonga du.
8 Holeka alu, nane nowika ha mwande yenyu wisumanize no mutemi wa Ashuli. Ane nomupelenyi fwalasi elufu nyejete, hono musume kutuga wanhu wenji wono wosuma kuzitumamila.
9 Ninga waco nye! Nha si mosuma kwisumaniza hamba no mwitumami mudodo du bwete, wo mutemi wa Ashuli, na 'kuno muwalanjiliye Wamisili wamuletelenyi matuka go wulugu hamonga ne wasilikale wapanda fwalasi.
10 Alo 'nye mukwihwesa nha vyono lono nayihomela yisi yenyu no kuyongomeza, mono yanhaza nani? Alu mumanye nha vyono, Muwaha Mulungu yo yamhela wutazi wakwe. Hamby nha yo mweneco mono yanonjela vyono nize niyihome no kuyongomeza.”

11 Eliyakimu na Shebina na Yowa, nawo wakamulonjela yuyo mwitumami wakatya, “Alu, wulongane nase mu nonga ye Cialameya. Hambi nase nha ciyimanyile nonga yiyo. Ninga wulece kulonga mu nonga ye Cihebelaniya, hambi wanhu wose wono wekaye mucanyha ye lijinji, nha wakuyihulika.”
12 Ninga mwene yakamupitula yakatya, “Alu, 'nye, mulece kwihwesa nha vyono mutemi wa Ashuli kanhuma ho nonje zizi na nyenye hamonga no mutemi wenyu du, aa ng'o. Langa nghulonga na kuli wanhu wose wono wekaye mwi'jinji mucanyha nawo wose wazihulice. Hambi wene nha wo wolya mavi gawo, kanyhi wang'we ne matunzi gawo weneco, hamonga na nyenye wose.”

13 Hodu nhondo mwitumami yuyulya, yakenuka yakema wima, yakawa kwanza kuzoza mu nonga ye Cihebelaniya, yakutya, “Ale, wose muhulicize zono mutemi wa Ashuli yakumulonjelenyi.
14 Yakumusugulenyi, nha vyono mulecele lukulu kuhulika wukonji wa Hezekiya. Hamby nha yakumukonjenyi du, hamba si kosuma kumuponyenyi yaye.
15 Kanyhi, mulece kumuleceleza yamupepelezenyi mumwitumbile Muwaha Mulungu. Mulece kwihwesa nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu komuponyenyi, no kugemila mapuga getu ge Waashuli ho galece kulisola likaya lyenyu.
16 Holeka mulece kuzihulika zono yakuzilonga Hezekiya ye! Hambi mutemi wa Ashuli, nha kalajiliza vyono muwuce mwi'kaya mwiyilavye nyo weneco kuli mwene. Na hono munoze nhavivyo, wose mokombolwa muwe kulya ne zabibu zenyu zono zili mu mibici yenyu ye mizabibu, hamonga ne zinghuyu zono zili mu mibici yenyu ye mikuyu, hamonga no kung'wa malenga gono gakulawa mu visima vyenyu nyo weneco,
17 sunga yaze yamusolenyi yamutegulenyi mukekale mu yisi yono yihwanile yisi yenyu, hono howa ho yili ne migunda ya zabibu, yono yosuma kumupelenyi divayi, kanyhi no wuhemba wono mowa kunoza mikate.
18 Holeka mulece kumuleceleza yuyo Hezekiya yamuputuzenyi, ho mwihwese nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu komuponyenyi. Mwihwesaje ta! Mwene ho yili milungu yo yose ye nghungugo yo yose, yono yasuma kuyiponya yisi yawo ku wulawa mu miwoko yo mutemi wa Ashuli?
19 Na hono yali yiwe vyo yinha, alu milungu ya Hamati na Alipadi yili hayi lo? Hamba milungu ya Sefalavayimu, nayo yili hayi? Wulenyi ho yuli mulungu hamba monga wa muli milungu yiyo, mono yayiponya Samaliya?
20 Mwene na nili lwene, lono milungu yi yino yose ye zi'si zizo, yono yasuma kuyiponya yisi yayo mu miwoko yo mutemi wa Ashuli? Alu hono zili nhavivyo, alu cicici cono coleka anye mwihwese nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu kosuma kuyiponya Yelusalemi?”

21 Ninga wanhu wose wakanyamala twii, nha vyono wali wakondya kulonjelwa no mutemi Hezekiya wanyamale. Holeka si wapitula hamba nghani bwete.
22 Ninga ho bahobaho Eliyakimu na Shebina, hamonga na Yowa, wakavidemulanga vivwalo vyawo ko wusungu wuwaha, wakawa kubita kuli mutemi Hezekiya kumumanyiza zizo zose zono mwitumami we Ciashuli yalonjile.


Isaya 37

Mutemi Hezekiya yakulomba wutazi kuli Isaya

(2Wat 19:1-7)

1 Nayo mutemi Hezekiya yali yeza kuzihulika zono wamulonjela, hodu yakawa kuvidemula vivwalo vyakwe ko wusungu wuwaha muno, yakavwala ligunila, yakawa kwinjila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
2 Hobaho yakawa kuwatuma Eliyakimu u mwitumami muwaha wa mwi'kulu, na Shebina u mwandici u mwana wa Amozi. Ninga nawo lulo wali wavwalile magunila.
3 Nayo yakawalajiliza wabite wakamulonjele Isaya watye nhavi, “Izuwali lye zuwa lyo wugaye, hambi nha calipicizwa, no kukozwa cevu, caweza nho mucekulu mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo, ninga yasoceye hamba si yakusuma no kulela.
4 Hambi u mutemi wa Ashuli, nha kamutuma mwitumami wakwe ho yaze yamutovule u Mulungu mumi. Holeka nghukulomba, u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wako yagahulice matovulo gaga, yawalipicize wawo wono wagalonjile. Na kanyhi nghukunena wuwalombele kuli Mulungu i wanhu wetu wono wasigaye.”

5 Nayo Isaya yali yeza kuzihulika zinghani zizo zo mutemi Hezekiya,
6 hodu nayo yakawa kumupitula mutemi, yakutya, “Gwe Hezekiya, Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, wulece kuwaleceleza Waashuli wakogofye, so wutya ati Mulungu si kokuponya yaye.
7 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu, nha komunoza mutemi wa Ashuli yahulice zikulongwa ne wanhu nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu komusola yamugomole ku yisi yakwe ya Ashuli, yamulece yawulajilwe kuko.”

Waashuli wakumogofya kanyhi Hezekiya

(2Wat 19:8-19)

8 Mwitumami we Ciashuli yakamanya nha vyono mutemi wa Ashuli yawuka ku Lakishi, nalu wakwihoma ne likaya lya Libina lye behi. Hodu nayo yakawa kumutyatya kuko ho vikalongane nayo.
9 Nazo zinghani zikawaficila Waashuli, nha vyono lipuga lye wasilikale we Cimisili, lyono likulongozwa na Tilihaka u mutemi wa Kushi, likuza kuwahoma. Nayo mutemi wa Ashuli yali yeza kuzihulika zizo, hodu yakawa kumutumila baluwa Hezekiya
10 mutemi wa Yuda yakumulonjela yakutya, “Ane manyile nha vyono, yuyo Mulungu wako mono witumbiye, kakulonjela nha vyono Yelusalemi si yosuma kutekwa no mutemi wa Ashuli. Ninga zizo zilece kukukonga.
11 Nane manyile nha vyono wakondya kuzihulika zono mutemi wa Ashuli yakuwa kuyinozeza yisi yo yose yono yakusaka kuyongomeza. Alu nagwe wukwihwesa nha vyono kopona hongo?
12 Ninga wumanye nha vyono walezi wangu wo wagongomeza makaya ga Gozani, na Halani, hamonga na Lezefu. Wakawawulaga ne wanhu wose wa Edeni, wono wekalaga ku Telesa, ne milungu yawo hamba si yawaponya yaye.
13 Ma, i watemi we makaya ga Hamati, na Alipadi, na Sefalavayimu, na Hena, hamonga na Iva, wali hayi?”

14 Nayo mutemi Hezekiya yakayibocela baluwa yiyo, mu wulawa mu miwoko ye wanhu wono watumwa wayilete, nayo yakayisoma. Yali yeza kukondya kuyisoma, yakenjila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, yakayiwika baluwa yiyo hali Muwaha Mulungu,
15 yakawa kulomba yakutya,
16 “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, go Mulungu wa Isilayeli, mono wikaye kucanyha ha ciligati ye Makelubi. Yo gwegwe du Mulungu wuli wene, mono wutemile mu matemi gose ge yinze.
17 Alu 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wucihulice, kanyhi wuzilangaje zono zeza kucijelela. Kanyhi wuzihulikaje zinghani zose zono Senakelibu yakukulonga agwe no kukutovula go Mulungu mumi.
18 Nase wose nha cimanyile, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nha vyono i watemi wa Ashuli wagongomeza makungugo menji, no kuzinoza zi'si zawo ziwe nyhiwa,
19 Wakayikongoga ne milungu yawo, yono na mbeka si yali milungu yaye, langa gali vihwani vye mabici masese na vye mabwe, yono minoze ne miwoko ye wanhu.
20 Alu, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, cikukulomba wuciponye mu miwoko ye Waashuli. Ne makungugo gose ga mono'nze ho gamanye vyono, agwe yo gwegwe du Mulungu wuli wene.”

I nghani ya Isaya kuli mutemi Hezekiya

(2Wat 19:20-37)

21 Nayo Isaya yakamutumila mutemi Hezekiya wanhu, wamutegulile lipitulo lyo wulombe wakwe kuli Mulungu,
22 na vyono Muwaha Mulungu yamupituye, yakutya,

“Agwe Senakelibu,

likaya lya Yelusalemi likukusekanga muno,

kanyhi likubedegaye muno.

23 Alu mwene, wukwihwesa nani lo,

mono wali kumutovulanga no kumwizwajiza?

Agwe mbeka wawula cogopo kuli'ne,

ane no Mulungu mono nelile wa Isilayeli.

24 Agwe wukatuma wetumami wako,

waze kono kwangu wanyidunhumulile

ku soko ye matuka gako gose go wulugu,

wukutya, vyono agwe wagasuma matunda matali ga Lebanoni.

Kanyhi wukwikumya

nha vyono wagatemanga mabici matali go muhagata,

hamonga ne misonobali yayo miswanu.

Kanyhi na vyono wenjila sunga mo muciligati muno muno ye mbago.

25 Na kanyhi wukwidunhumula vyono wahimba masima

mu zi'si zo wujenzi no kung'wa ne malenga gayo.

Na vyono ne magulu ge wasilikale wako,

galikanyanjila ne likolongo lya Nile, sunga likanyala.

26 “Alu 'gwe hongo hamba si wali wumanyile

vyono zizi zose yo nene mono naziwikaniza ku wulawa katali,

ho zize ziwe nhavivyo?

Sunga nalu, nakondya kuzifisiliza.

Hambi nha yo nene mono nakupela vilungo

ho wugongomeze makaya gono gena magomawakali,

gawe matongo.

27 Ne wanhu wono wekalaga mu makaya gago,

nghawawusiza vilungo vyawo,

wakawa no woga wuwaha, wakazilika.

Wakehwana nhe mahanze gono gali mwi'wimbi,

hamba mahanze gono gatimbile gono gacindice mwi'tembe mucanyha,

gono gosuma kuwomvywa ne mbeho ye civuce ya kucilima.

28 “Ninga ane, nizimanyile zako zose,

na zono kwitumaga, na kono kujendelaga.

Kanyhi nghumanyile na vyono wukuwa kungholelwa likuwo.

29 Ninga nane nakondya kuzihulika zizo ze likuwo lyako,

hamonga no widunhumuzi wako.

Ninga nane luno, nokutobola zimhula zako,

nghuvwice ndobogo, hamonga no luzali mu mulomo wako.

Hanyuma nghugomole mu nzila yi yilya kono walawilila.”

30 Hanyuma Isaya yakamulonjela mutemi Hezekiya, yakamutya, “Aci co citanjilo ca zizo zono si zoleka kwijela. Mwakawu, hamonga no mwaka wono wukuza, anye molya wuhemba wono wupasiciye weneco. Langa mu mwaka we kadatu, aho langa ho molohadika nyo weneco, no kududula nhwanga yenyu. Molohadika ne mizabibu no kulya zabibu zayo.
31 Ne wanhu wa Yuda wono wasigala, wolotula nhe vilimwa vyono mbeyu yayo yinjize milela yayo kusi muno yo muhangha, no kulela mana.
32 Namo mu Yelusalemi na mwi'tunda lya Sayuni, molowa mowali wanhu wono wasigaye. Hambi Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, nha yo yatemya zizi zijele.

33 “Na kanyhi azi zo nghani zono Muwaha Mulungu yakuzilonga kuli mutemi wa Ashuli, yakutya, U mutemi wa Ashuli, hamba si kosuma kwinjila mwi'kaya lili, hamba kuguma songa monga du. Hasina musilikale hamba monga mono yena ne ngula yakwe, mono kolisejelela likaya, hamba hasina mono kololipilima likaya, hamba kulizenjela yigagala.
34 Nayo mutemi wa Ashuli kowuya ne nyuma yatyatizye nzila yono yayizize, hamba kwinjila mwi'kaya lili yaye. Ane nene no Muwaha Mulungu, vyo nghutya.
35 Nane nolidima likaya lili no kulikamisa, ku soko ye lilumbo lyangu, na ku soko yo wihuwizo wono namwihuwiziza Dawudi u mwitumami wangu.”

36 Lolulolulo malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kubita ku nghambi ye Waashuli, yakawa kuwawulaga wasilikale we Waashuli waficile elufu yigana limonga na makumi munana na yihano. Na mwizuwa lye kejete, hakali mitondo muno muno, wasilikale wose we Waashuli wakawa wakondya kufwa!
37 Nayo Senakelibu u mutemi we Waashuli, yali yeza kuziwona zizo, hodu yakawa kutuluka yakawa kuwuya ku Ninawi.
38 Na mwi'zuwa linji lono yali yenjiye mu wuhembelo wo mulungu wakwe Nisiloki, yakuhemba, ale baho, i wanagwe wejete Adilameleki na Shalezeli, wakawa kumusepa, wakawa kumuwulajila mumo sogo yawo ne mimaje yawo. Wene wakawa kutiziza ku yisi ya Alalati. No mwanagwe yunji, litagwa lyakwe yali Esale-hadoni yuyo yo yakahala wutemi wo sogo yawo.


Isaya 38

Mhungo yo mutemi Hezekiya hamonga no wunoje wakwe

(2Wat 20:1-11; 2Zin 32:24-26)

1 Na mu vilo vivyo mutemi Hezekiya yakatamwa yakawa yali behi no kufwa. Nayo muwizi Isaya u mwana wa Amozi, yakabita kumulanga. Yali yeza kumwagana, yakamutya, “Muwaha Mulungu yakukutya vyono alu, wiwikanizaje ne nyumba yako, hambi nha si kokwikala mumi yaye, langa kofwa du.”

2 Hodu baho nayo Hezekiya yakawa kupitucila kwi 'jinji, yakawa kumulomba Mulungu yakutya,
3 “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nghukulomba wukumbucile nha vyono ane nakwitumamila ko wihuwilwa ne cogopo, na vyono nhondo zose kanyhi, nitumaga zo wuhile wako.” Hanyuma yakalila muno ko wusungu.

4 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu nhondo yakawa kumutuma kanyhi Isaya
5 yabite kanyhi kuli Hezekiya, yakamulonjele yatye, “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, no Mulungu wo mulezi wako Dawudi, niwuhulika wulombe wako, nigawona ne mahozi gako. Holeka nane lu, nokuleka wikale mumi, sunga myaka yikumi na mihano.
6 Nane nokuponya gwegwe, hamonga ne likaya lili lya Yelusalemi, mu miwoko yo mutemi wa Ashuli. Nane nojendelela du kulidima likaya.”

7 Nayo Isaya yakamupitula yakatya, “Muwaha Mulungu kokupela citanjilo cono cokulajila vyono mwene kokamisa wihuwizo wakwe.
8 Muwaha Mulungu, kowugomola ne nyuma nghazo yikumi u muzizi wa mu madalaja gono yazenga mutemi Ahazi.” Nawo muzizi wukawa kuwuya ne nyuma, nghanzo yikumi.

9 Nayo Hezekiya yali yeza kupona mu wutamwa wakwe, hodu yakawa kulwandika lwimbo alu lwe lilumbo.

10 Hamba niwe nili mu wuswanu wo wikale wangu.

Ninga vyo nobita du ku yisi ye wafu.

11 Kanyhi nghehwesa nha vyono si nolomuwona

Muwaha Mulungu, mu yinze yiyi ye womi.

Hamba kumuwona munhu wo wose kanyhi.

12 U wikalo wangu nha wali wakondya kuduma.

Nhe lihema lyono likumwagulwa.

Nho mwenda wono wukuwuswa mu citanda.

Nane nghehwesa vyono Mulungu yeza kuwudumya wumi wangu.

13 Nghawa nghulila cilo cose so wusungu

Nghawa kwiwona nha simba yikugabenanjiza mafupa gangu.

14 Ne lizi lyangu likasocela, likawa yidodo muno.

Nane nghawa kulila nha nghunda.

Ne meso gangu gakakatala so wugalamila kucanyha.

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wumhonye mu wugaye wuwu.

15 Hambi 'ne ho nolongaci,

hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha yo yetumile zizi.

Ne nhumbula yangu yiwene wusungu muno muno

hamba ne zinhongo zose zanyala.

16 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nowumipila muli'gwe

nane nokwikala muli gwegwe du.

Holeka wumhonye, wundece mumipe.

17 No wusungu wangu wopituka wuwe mulamu.

Hambi nha wamhonya mu zimbali zose.

Wakondya kunenela no wubi wangu wose.

18 Hambi ku yisi ye wafu, nha hasina yunji

mono kosuma kukulumba.

Ne wafu hamba si wowitumbila wihuwilwa wako.

19 Langa i womi wo wakulumbaga.

Nha vyono nane luno nghukulumba.

Nawo walezi, wawalonjelaga wana wawo

zo wihuwilwa wako.

20 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wakondya kumhonya.

Holeka nase luno, cotowa masumbi, no kwimba

ze lilumbo lyako.

Kanyhi cowa kukulumbila mu Nyumba yako

mu vilo vyose vyo wikale wetu.

21 Na Isaya yakamulonjela mutemi Hezekiya yakubule cibanda co mukuyu,

yabandice mu lipu lyakwe, nalyo lyopona.

22 Nayo mutemi Hezekiya yakamuwuza Isaya, yakamutya,

“Alu citanjiloci cono condajila vyono nosuma kanyhi kwinjila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu?”


Isaya 39

Wanhu wono watumwa ku wulawa ku Babiloni

(2Wat 20:12-19)

1 Na mu vilo vivyo, Melodaki Baladani, u mutemi wa Babiloni, u mwana wa Baladani. Nayo yakahulika vyono mutemi Hezekiya mutamwa. Hodu nayo yakamwandicila baluwa Hezekiya, yakamutegulila hamonga ne cipegwa.
2 Nayo Hezekiya yakawabocela viswanu wa walya wono watumwa yakawa kuwalajila nyumba yakwe ye viwutwa, fweza, na zahabu, hamonga na vye mhunjile swanu, ne vitwalo vyakwe vyose vyo wulugu. Hakawa hasina cinji co cose ca mu zikaya zakwe ze viwutwa vyakwe, hamonga ne hanhu hose mu wutemi wakwe vyono si yawalajila wawo wanhu wono watumwa.
3 Hanyuma muwizi Isaya yakamubitila mutemi Hezekiya yakamuwuza yakatya, “Alu wanhu wawa walawa hayi, na kanyhi wakulonjela cici?”

Nayo Hezekiya yakapitula yakatya, “Wanhu wawa, walawa yisi ya kutali muno muno, yisi ya Babiloni.”
4 Nayo Isaya yakatya, “Alu wawona cici mwi'kulu?” Hezekiya yakatya, “Waviwona vinhu vyose, sunga na vyose vyono vili mu nyumba ye viwutwa.”

5 Hodu nayo Isaya yakamulonjela Hezekiya yakatya, “Alu Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakukutya nhavi,
6 ‘Lizuwa lyokuza, lono vinhu vyose vyono vili mwi'kulu, hamonga ne viwutwa vyose vyono walezi wako waviwika, vyose vyolosolwa no kukunyhilwa ku Babiloni. Hasina hamba cinhu cimonga cono cilasigala.
7 Hamonga na wanji wa muli wanago wako, nawo wolosolwa wategulwe kutali, wakapaswe, wawe wetumami wa mwi'kulu lyo mutemi wa Babiloni.’ ”
8 Nayo mutemi Hezekiya yakamanya nha vyono, mu vilo vyakwe, wowa mulamu du. Holeka yakapitula yakatya, “Nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu yono wuyilonjile, swanu.”


Isaya 40

Zinghani zo witumbilo

1 Mulungu wetu yakutya,

“Muwasindamice 'nye wanhu wangu!

Muwasindamice!

2 Muwasikamye wanhu wa Yelusalemi.

Muwalonjele nha vyono wakondya

kugaya mu nghango yono yiwakwiliye,

no wubi wawo nhondo wanenelwa.

Hambi nha nakondya kuwalipiciza mu

nghango yo wubi wawo.”

3 Ne lizi likulonga kwe vilungo likutya,

“Ale, muwikanize nzila yo Muwaha Mulungu mu nyika!

Mumutelemezezaje Mulungu wetu nzila ya mu nyika!

4 Mufucilize malolo gose

mugatenjelemule ne matunda gose.

Na mu vigongo molowa mutelemeye,

na mono mogali masasala-sasala,

namo motelemezwa.

5 Ne nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu yologubulwa,

ne wanhu wose woloyiwona.

Muwaha Mulungu mweneco yo yehuwiza zizi.”

6 Ne lizi likulonga kwa vilungo likutya,

“Wuyanzaje nghani.”

Nane nghawuza nghatya,

“Alo nghanici yene yono niyanze?”

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya,

“Wanze nha vyono wanhu wose wehwanile mahanze,

hamba si wekalaga vilo vinji, wehwanile maluwa

ga mu mawimbi.

7 I mahanze ganyalaga, nago maluwa gawombaga,

lono Muwaha Mulungu yakuleta libeho

liwa kugaputa.

Na mbeka, wanhu siwekalaga

wehwanile mahanze du.

8 Na heya, mahanze ganyalaga,

nago maluwa gawombaga,

ninga nghani yo Mulungu wetu yikalaga cibitilila.”

9 Gwe Yelusalemi,

alu wubite mwi'tunda litali mucanyha,

wukalwanze luganuzi luswanu!

Wuzoze hawaha 'gwe Sayuni

wanze zo luganuzi luswanu!

Wulonje, wulece kogopa.

Wugalonjele makaya ga Yuda

nha vyono Mulungu wawo yakuza!

10 Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga kolokuza

yateme ne vilungo, kolowaleteleza

hamonga ne wanhu wono yawaponya.

11 Kololidima litewa lyakwe nho mudimi.

Kolozikongholela hamonga zinyana ze zingholo,

yazidinghule mu mawoko gakwe.

Kolozitonga mhola mhola zono zikosa.

U Mulungu we ciwene wa Isilayeli

12 Alo nani mono kosuma kulipima lilenga

ko wufumbate mwi'ganza,

hamba kuwupima wulanga kwe vidole vyakwe?

Hamba munhu kosuma kuwudinghulila muhangha

we yinze mu cikombe,

hamba kugapima matunda ne vigongo mu vipimo?

13 Hamba lunji munhu kosuma kumulonjeleza

Muwaha Mulungu cono yetume?

Hamba nani mono kusuma kumwijiza,

hamba kumulanjiza?

14 Alo mwene, Mulungu yalondola lihala kwa nani,

ho yamanye wizuce, no kwiyijiza nha vyono

zinghani zinojeye zitumwe?

15 Makungugo gose,

yo nghani ya bwete du hali Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha genha mabanhi du ge malenga,

ne vizingwa vyo kutali,

kaviwonaga vipelumhile muno nhe manghundi.

16 Ne vilimu vyose vya mu mbago ya Lebanoni,

hamba si vikondizye so makumbiko,

kuli Mulungu wetu sunga ne mabici gayo,

nha macefu ku wasiza moto.

17 Ne makungugo nha si cinji yaye hali mwene.

18 Alu mwene, kosuma kumwihwananiza Mulungu nha nani?

Hamba si kosuma yaye kumulolosa nha vyono yenha

hamba kumwihwananiza yaye.

19 Hambi mwene nha si yehwanile ne vihwani

vyono wanhu wavisesaga,

vyono i watyani nawo, wavihiliwaga zahabu,

hanyuma wawa kuvinozeza litende lya fweza.

20 Nayo munhu mono yakuwona

nha vyono si kosuma wuguzi we fweza,

hamba zahabu,

mwene kahagulaga mubici we nghulunje wono si wufukunywaga yaye.

Nayo kamulondolaga musesi munghula

ho yamunozeze cihwani ciseswe

cono si cosuma kung'anuka yaye no kugwa hasi.

21 Hongo lu agwe wukali namanya yaye?

Hamba baho katali si walonjelwa ng'o?

Hamba wukali si nahulika na vyono yinze

yamula yamula ng'o?

22 Yinze yiyi, yawumbwa na mono yekaye

mwi'goda lyakwe lyo wutemi,

kucanyha ye yinze kuko ku wulanga.

Ne wanhu wono wekaye mono'nze,

wejelaga nha vinhu vidodo du, wehwanile mumehe.

Mwene yakawukunzulula wulanga kotya mutote,

yakawugolola nhe lihema, ho yekale mumo.

23 Mwene kawawembulaga watemi

kanyhi kawa kuwapuvya.

24 Wakuwa wehwanile no mubici mudodo

wono wukuhadikwa no kucindicila mulungu-yeye.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kawa kuleta libeho,

nawo wuwa kunyala no kupemheluswa ne libeho

nhe mawaje.

25 Alu mwene, kosuma ku mwihwananiza Mulungu

mono yelile nha nani?

Hamba ho yuli yunji mono yehwanile mwene?

26 Ale ta, wugalamile wulanje ku wulanga!

Mono yaziwumba zinyelezi zizo zono

wukuziwona, nani lo?

Mono yaziwumba,

yo yuyo kanyhi mono yakuwa kuzitonga,

nhe wasilikale wo wulugu.

Kanyhi mwene yazimanyile ne liwalo lyazo zose,

kazicemaga nyelezi ne'tagwa lyayo!

Mwene yali vilungo muno,

holeka hasina nyelezi hamba monga yono yajiliye!

27 Alu mwene anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli

nhawulenyi 'lu mukung'ung'ulikayiwe

nha kotya Muwaha Mulungu si yawumanyile

wugaye wenyu, hamba kuyilanga ling'ani yenyu?

28 Alu mwene anye si mumanyile?

Hamba mukali nahulika?

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo Mulungu we cibitilila,

kanyhi yo yayiwumba yinze yose.

Mwene si kakatalaga, hamba kudobanya yaye.

Hasina munhu mono kosuma

kugamanya mahala gakwe.

29 Mwene kawapelaga vilungo wono watimbile

no kuwonjeleza vilungo wono wasoceye.

30 Sunga ne wazelelo wokatala no kuzilika.

31 “Ninga wono wamwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu

yawapele wutazi, wawo wolopegwa vilungo vipya.

Wologuluka kucanyha kotya 'ndi,

kanyhi wolomangha hamba kukatala

yaye wolowa kujenda hamba si wolozilika ng'o.”


Isaya 41

Mulungu wo witumbilo kuli Isilayeli

1 Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Anye nyo zi'si zo kutali,

munyamale du twii, muhulicize nene!

Anye mwiwikanizaje du,

mulete zinghani zenyu ku wuling'anizi,

hambi nha motuga cilejeho co kuzilolosa.

Cihangulizaje hamonga, ciduwane,

cimumanye mono yali ling'ani.

2 Mwene nani lo mono yaleta wusume

ku wulawa kucilima no kumupela vilungo vyo wusume

hanhu ho hose hono yakubita?

Hamba nani mono kamupelaga vilungo

vyo kuwasumila watemi, hamonga ne makungugo?

No mumaje wakwe,

wukuwatemanga wagwa ho hasi,

wawa nha manghundi.

Ne zisonga zakwe zikuweyagaza nhe mawaje,

gono gakupemheluswa ne mbeho.

3 Yakuwawinga no kukola yapolile.

Kanyhi yakuwa yamanyhile muno kuciza,

yakuwatyatya sunga mo mu nzila yono yali

yakali si nayikola kwe magulu gakwe!

4 Mwene nani lo mono yazinoza zizi zijele?

Hamba nani mono yawuwika mwamulizo we zinghomo?

Yo nene, ane no Muwaha Mulungu,

yo nene mono nali nili baho ku wulawa ha mwamulizo,

kanyhi yo nene du no Muwaha Mulungu,

mono nowa baho ha ndumilizo ya zose.

5 “Ne wanhu wose we zi'si zo kutali,

nha wakondya kuziwona zono netuma.

Alu nawo wenjilwa no woga, wakucicima so woga.

Holeka nawo luno, wekonghola no kuza.

6 Nawo i wanghula wo kusesa,

alu wakwiyitaza no kwisinyhimika weneco wose.

7 I waselemala nawo,

wakuwalonjela i watyana zahabu wakutya,

‘Ale mwetuma mulimo muswanu!’

Nayo mono yakucikulunjiza cihwani,

yakumusindamika mono yakucilunza-lunza

hamonga ne misumali, yakutya,

‘Ka! Mwene wacilunza viswanu si.’ ”

Na hanyuma, wawa kuvipanga vihwani hanhu

hamonga no kuvitowela ne misumali.

8 “Ninga anye nyo Isilayeli,

anye muli wanhu mono nene namuhagulenyi,

anye nyo wana wo mwendece wangu Abulahamu.

9 Nane nghamusolenyi ku wulawa ku mhelo ze yinze.

Nghamucemenyi ku wulawa ku zimhilimo zayo.

Nghamulonjelenyi nghatya,

Anye muli wetumami wangu.

Yo nene mono nimuhagulenyi,

hamba si nomutajenyi yaye.

10 Holeka mulece kogopa,

ane nha nili hamonga nanye!

Ane yo nene Mulungu wenyu,

holeka mulece kogofywa na cinji co cose!

Ane nomupenyi vilungo, kanyhi nomutazenyi.

Nomudimenyi, kanyhi nomuponyenyi.”

11 “Na wose wono wakumukolelwenyi likuwo,

wolosumwa no kukolwa cevu.

Na wose wono wakulewana nanye, wolofwa.

12 Wajililile lukulu mono yinze, hamba kwijela kanyhi.

13 Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu,

mono nghumusikamyenyi nghutya,

Mulece kogopa, ane nomutazenyi.”

14 Kanyhi Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Anye nyo Isilayeli, hamba muwe muli wacefu,

kanyhi muli wasocele nhe nyongololo,

ninga mulece kuwa no woga,

hambi ane nha nomutazenyi.

Ane mono nelile, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

yo nene du mono nomuponyenyi.

15 Ane nomunozenyi anye muwe nhe lituli

hamonga no mutwango wayo mupya,

nanye mogakobonga matunda no kugatenjelemula,

navyo vigongo vyotijinhika viwe manghundi.

16 Nanye mogesa mucanyha,

nayo mbeho yogapemhelusa,

nago gokwiyagazwa ne civulavumbi.

Nanye baho nhondo, ho mosangalala,

hambi ane nha yo nene mono nili Mulungu wenyu

nanye nhondo, ho momhela lilumbo

ane mono nelile, u Mulungu wa Isilayeli.

17 “Na kolo wanhu wangu wakulondola malenga,

lono malaka gawo ganyala so nyhilu ye malenga,

ane Muwaha Mulungu nowapitula wulombe wawo.

Ane no Muwaha Mulungu wa Isilayeli sinowaleka yaye.

18 Nane nogatula makolongo gahulume

mu vigongo mu hanhu hakavu,

ne mivwilo ye malenga yihulume mu malolo.

Noligalusa linyika hawe malamba ga malenga,

ne yisi nghavu hawe ne mivwilo ye malenga.

19 Noyicindya mibici ye mihagata mu nyika

hamonga ne mikungugu, ne mibici midodododo,

hamonga ne mizeyituni.

Ne mbago yotita mu yisi nghavu,

yowa mbago ye mibici ye minanasi ne minghulwa,

hamonga ne mibici yinji yinji.

20 Nawo wanhu wose woziwona zizi,

wamanye nha vyono nene Muwaha Mulungu

yo nitumile zizi.

Nawo ho wokwizuka nha vyono Mulungu

mono yelile wa Isilayeli, yo yazinozize zizi zijele.”

Muwaha Mulungu yakuyibedegala milungu yo wulambi

21 U Muwaha Mulungu, u mutemi wa Isilayeli yakutya,

“Nye nyo milungu ye makungugo,

ale mulete zinghani zenyu

Mulonje nghani zono mwina nazo!

22 Ta muze mucilonjele zono zokwijela

mu vilo vyono vikuza,

nase ho ciwe cimanyiye lukulu kolo zikwijela.

Ale mulolose ha mwande yo wuling'anizi zono zapuluta,

mucilonjele ne nhunizo yayo.

23 Kanyhi mucilonjele na zono zolokwijela hanyuma,

nase baho ho comanyila vyono kumbe

nanye muli milungu!

Kanyhi mosuma kucinozeza

nha vyono nyenye mwendile,

hamba swanu, hamba kuciletela matamwa,

mucimemye woga hamonga ne cogopo ca kuli'nye!

24 Ninga ase cimanyile

nha vyono zose zono mukwituma,

yo nghani ya bwete du na wose wono wakumuhembenyi,

wakwikomaza wali wene du!

25 “Ninga ane nakondya kumuhagula munhu yunji,

mono yekaye ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.

Nane nomutuma yayanzize milewo

yakwe ku lubavu lwa kucilima.

Nayo kowazumhila i watemi,

yawasambanjile nha malongo,

nho muwumba nyungu vyono kaluwaga lilongo lyakwe.

26 Mwene, nani wa muli nyenye mono yali yakondya

kuzimanya zizi nha vyono zolokuza,

nase ho cimumanye?

Ninga ase cimanyile nha vyono,

hasina hamba monga wa muli nyenye,

mono yazilonga zizi,

hasina hamba yunji mono yamuhulicenyi mukuzilonga!

27 Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

mono nali mulongozi kuyilonjela Sayuni zinghani zizi.

Kanyhi nghategula zinghani na ku Yelusalemi,

nghawalonjela nghatya,

‘I wanhu wenyu wakuza, wakuwuya kono kaya!’

28 Nane nali neza kuyilanga milungu yiyo,

nghawona vyono yose, yisina cono yopitula,

hakawa hasina hamba yono yapitula zono nayiwuzaga.

29 Holeka milungu yiyi yose, yo nghani ya bwete du,

siyosuma hamba kwituma cinji co cose

kanyhi yisina hamba vilungo bwete.”


Isaya 42

U mwitumami wo Muwaha Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Lanjenyi ta u mwitumami wangu

mono nghumusikamya,

yo yuyo mono namuhagula, kanyhi

mono nghwendezwa nayo.

Nakondya kumumemya muhe wangu,

nayo kowaletela makungugo gose wiling'ano.

2 Nayo si kozoza hamba kwinula lizi lyakwe,

hamba kulalika cinji co cose mu zinzila.

3 Hamba si kolibena yaye, lidete-lulenga,

hamba si kocizimya yaye cikoloboyi

cono cikufwifwiza.

Langa mwene kowaletela ling'ani wanhu wose.

4 Nayo hamba si kotukuwika mutima hamba kukatala yaye,

langa mwene koleta ling'ani kono'nze.

Ne zi'si zose zolindila mejizo gakwe.”

5 Mulungu yawumba wulanga, yakawa kuwutandaza,

yakawa kuyisikamya yinze na vyose vyono vili mumo.

Nayo yo kawapelaga wehele wanhu wose.

Ninga aluno Muwaha Mulungu yakumulonjela

mwitumami wakwe yakutya,

6 “Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu mono nakucema,

nghakupela no wusume ho wusume

kuyiwona ling'ani yikunozwa kono'nze.

Nane nonozeza muli gwegwe cilagane cangu

ne wanhu wangu wose.

Na muli gwegwe,

mo nowuletela wuzelu wubulile muli makungugo.

7 Nagwe kowanuguza meso i wabofu,

kanyhi kowawopolela wose

wono wekaye mu wutitu mu jela.”

8 “Ane nili wene du,

yo nene Muwaha Mulungu u Mulungu wako.

Hasina kanyhi hamba mulungu yunji wo wose

mono kosuma kuhanga nane mu lukumyo lwangu,

hamba si noyikombolela lukulu milungu

yihanje lilumbo na nene.

9 Ne zinghani zono nazilonga katali,

alu mbeka zakondya kwijela.

Ninga aluno nomulonjelenyi ze nghani mhya,

hamba ziwe zikali si nanza kwijela.”

Lwimbo lo lukumyo

10 Mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu lwimbo lupya,

mwimbe zo lukumyo lwakwe nye nyo yinze yose!

Nanye mono mukujendela mwi'lenga,

mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

Nanye nyo zi'si zo kutali mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu.

11 Vilecenyi nyika hamonga ne vikaya vyayo,

vyose vimukumye Mulungu.

I wanhu wono wekaye ku Kedali,

walecenyi wose wamukumye Mwene.

Nawo wono wekaye mwi'kaya lya Sela,

wembe ne nyemo mu wulawa mu matunda!

12 Nawo wono wekaye mu zi'si zo kutali

wamupele Muwaha Mulungu lilumbo no lukumyo.

13 Muwaha Mulungu kabitaga mu milewo yenha citung'ati.

Mwene yewikanizize lukulu,

kanyhi muhidimbazi ku milewo,

katowaga ndulele yo wulugu,

kalajilaga wusume kuli walugu wakwe.

Mulungu yakwihuwiza nha vyono kowapela wutazi wanhu wakwe

14 Mulungu yakutya,

“Ane nali nyamaye du vilo vinji.

Hamba kuwapitula wanhu wangu.

Ninga luno cipango co witume cakondya kufika.

Holeka aluno nolila nho mucekulu mono

yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

15 Nane nogongomeza matunda ne vigongo,

noganyaza mahanze hamonga ne mabici.

Nonyaza makolongo ga mu malolo, gawe nyika

nonyaza hamonga ne malamba ge malenga.”

16 “Nane nowatonga wanhu wangu wono wali cibofu,

wakole mu nzila yono wakali nayikola

hamba mwizuwa linji.

Nowunoza wutitu wawo, wuwe wuzelu,

ne hanhu hono hali sasala-sasala hawe hatelemeye.

Nawo wuwu wo wihuwizo wangu,

nowebatiliza ho walece kukatala.

17 Na wose wono wetumbiye vihwani viseswe,

na wose wono wakuvitya vihwani vyenjedulwe

yo milungu yawo,

wawo wose wonyetywa no kukozwa cevu.”

Wulemwe we Waisilayeli ku wutanje

18 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Ale hulicizenyi ta anye nyo wanhu we ciziwi!

Mulanjile behi' nye nyo wabofu ho muwone!

19 Mwene howa hoyuli yunji mono yali cibofu yawucizize

wubofu wo mwitumami wangu,

hamba mono yali ciziwi yamucizize mwitumami

wangu mono namutuma?

20 Alu nanye Isilayeli, nha mwakondya kuziwona

nghani nyinji muno,

ninga wulenyi, zina cambuzici kuli'nye?

Kanyhi mwina ne makutu go kuhulicila,

ninga hamba si mukuzitanga yaye.”

21 U Muwaha Mulungu, yo Mulungu mono kezaga

no wuhile wo kuponya,

holeka yendile kuwukumya wulajilize wakwe

hamonga ne mafundo gakwe

kanyhi yendile ne wanhu wakwe wagakamise.

22 Ninga alu wanhu wakwe nha wakondya kutekwa,

wadindiywe mu zijela, waweza wafungwa.

Hambi nha wakondya kuhizwa no kutekwa,

na hasina hamba yunji mono kokuza yawaponye.

23 Alu, wulenyi! Nani wa muli nyenye

mono yakuzihulika zono nghulonga?

Ninga wulenyi! Mozihulika no kuzibata?

24 Mwene nani mono yawalavya Isilayeli ho watekwe?

Yo mweneco Muwaha Mulungu mono sese camusutizya,

Hambi nha cekalaga mu mikalile yono

si yamwendezaga mwene,

hamba kwibata mejizo gakwe gono yacijiza.

25 Holeka nayo yacijidila wukali we likuwo lyakwe,

no wusungu we milewo.

Ne likuwo lyakwe,

likawakolelwa Isilayeli nha moto mukali

ninga 'se hamba kumanya zono zacijelelaga yaye,

hamba kumanya co cose muli zizo.


Isaya 43

Mulungu yakwihuwiza vyono kowaponya wanhu wakwe

1 Nye nyo Isilayeli,

u Muwaha Mulungu mono yawawumba,

yakutya nhavi, “Mulece kogopa,

hambi nha nakondya kumukombozenyi.

Kanyhi nimucemenyi ‘Wuli wangu.’

2 Na kolo mukukola mu malenga matali,

ane nowa nili hamonga nanye.

Ne magayo genyu, hamba si gomugubizenyi yaye.

Na kolo mukukola mu moto, hamba si mopya yaye,

ne majelo mawaha muno, hamba si gomusumizenyi yaye.

3 Hambi 'ne nha yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wenyu,

u Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli mono yakumuponyenyi.

Nane noyilavya Misili, yiwe 'kombo so nyenye,

nayo Kushi na Seba, nazo nozilavya nhavivyo,

ku soko yenyu, anye ho muwe welecele.

4 Ne makungugo gose, nogaleka,

ho mhonye wumi wenyu,

hambi anye nyo Isilayeli,

nha muli wo wuguzi wukamu kuli'ne,

nane nghamwendenyi no kumupelenyi lilumbo.

5 Holeka mulece kogopa,

hambi ane nha nili hamonga nanye!

‘Nane nowakonghola no kuwagomola

wo lulelo lwenyu ku wulawa kutali ku lubavu lwa kucilima,

sunga ko ku lubavu lwa kumwezi.

6 Noyilonjela mhelo ye sumuka yiwalece wawuce kuko,

nayo mhelo ye takama yilece kuwemila yaye.

Muwalekaje wanhu wangu wawuye hamonga

ku wulawa mu ma'si go kutali, na mu yinze yose.

7 Hambi nha wanhu wangu nene

ane nawawumba ho wamhele lukumyo.’ ”

Waisilayeli wo wuhomelezi wo Muwaha Mulungu

8 U Mulungu yakutya,

“Waletenyi ku wuling'anizwe wanhu wangu.

Hambi nha wena meso, ninga hamba si wakuwona yaye,

wena ne makutu, ninga hamba si wakuhulika yaye!

9 Waletenyi makungugo gose ku wuling'anizwe.

Hambi milungu yawo ya hayi yono yosuma

kuzilonga ze vilo vyono vikuza?

Hamba milungu ya hayi ya muli yene

yono yosuma kuzilonga zono zikwijela aluno?

Ale, muyilekaje ta milungu yiyo, yiwahomelele

hono mbeka wali ling'ani

yihomelele i mbeka ye zinghani zawo,

ne wanhu kolo wakuzihulika, watye ‘Heya za mbeka.’ ”

10 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, anye yo nyenye

wuhomelezi wangu.

Ane namuhagulenyi ho muwe wetumami wangu,

ho mumanye 'ne no kunyihuwila

kanyhi ho mumanye nha vyono yo nene du

Mulungu nili wene.

Na kanyhi mumanye nha vyono hasina mulungu

yunji ku wulawa katali, hamba mono

kolowa, langa yo nene du.

11 “Ane yo nene du Muwaha Mulungu,

yo nene du mono nosuma kumuponyenyi.

12 Ane yo nene mono namulonjelenyi

zono zolomwijelelenyi,

hanyuma nghaza nimuponyenyi.

Na hasina hamba mulungu yunji mujenzi mono yetuma zizi,

nanye yo nyenye mono muli wahomelezi wangu.

13 Ane nili Mulungu,

na nojendelela vilo vyose kuwa yo nene du Mulungu.

Na hasina munhu wo wose mono kokwiponya

mu vilungo vyangu.

Kanyhi hasina munhu wo wose mono kosuma

kuzikawaniza zono nitumaga.”
