Wisukule we Waisilayeli ku Babiloni

14 Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli,

u Muwaha Mulungu mono yakuwa kumuponyenyi yakutya nhavi,

“Ane notuma lipuga lye wasilikale ku Babiloni ho wamuponyenyi.

Nogabenanjiza malango ge likaya,

ne lizozo lye nyemo lye wanhu walyo,

lyopituka liwe cililo.

15 Hambi ane, nha yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wenyu mono yelile.

Yo nene mono nakuwumba 'gwe Isilayeli,

kanyhi yo nene no mutemi wenyu.”

16 Katali Muwaha Mulungu yanoza nzila mwi'lenga,

yakanoza wukolo mu malenga mawaha.

17 Yakalyongomeza lipuga liwaha muno lye wasilikale

we vilungo. Lipuga lyono lyali lina matuka go

wulugu hamonga ne zifwalasi.

Wakagwa, hamba si wolokwinucila lukulu kanyhi,

wakajililila lukulu nho wuzelu we tala

yono yakondya kuzimywa.

18 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Mulece kuzikumbucila zinghani zinongozi

zono zakondya kupuluta,

hamba kwihwesa zinghani ze katali.

19 Langa, muzilanjililaje zinghani zimhya

zono nozituma.

Nazo nha zakondya kwijela, nanye mosuma

kuziwona lulu!

Ane nosesa nzila yikolele mwi nyika,

nimupelenyi ne makolongo ge malenga

gahulume mumo.

20 Nane kolo nakondya kutula makolongo gakuhuluma

mu nyika, no kuwapela malenga i wanhu wangu wahagulwa,

ale baho, sunga ne malimu gose ga mu mbago

nago ho gomhela cogopo,

nazo zinyhewe ne zinyacihema

zonghumya nene.

21 Hambi wawa nha wo wanhu wono nenozeza

nene no mweneco,

nawo wowa kwimba zo lukumyo lwangu!”

U wubi we Waisilayeli

22 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ninga anye nyo nghungugo ya mwali Isilayeli mwakondya kunghatala,

kanyhi hamba si mwendaga no kuhemba nene.

23 Hamba si mwasakaga no kundavizya vilavywa

vinyanywe vye zingholo,

hamba si mwali mwendile kumhela cogopo

mu nzila ye makumbiko genyu.

Ane nha si namukatazajenyi so wusaka kuli'nye vilavywa,

hamba kumukatazajenyi so wufusa wuvumba.

24 Nanye hamba si mwangulila wuvumba yaye,

hamba kunyigutya ne mhama ye mitugo zenyu.

Langa anye mwamhela mutegulo muzito muno

wo wubi wenyu,

mukasakaza ne zimbi zono mwetuma.

25 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, ane yo nene Mulungu

mono nghuwunenela wubi wenyu.

Nane nghuzinoza zizi vyono nili Mulungu.

Hamba si nowukumbucila kanyhi no wubi wenyu.”

26 “Alu, cibitane ku mahakama,

muwutegule kuko wusonjezi wenyu!

Mulolose nghani zenyu, mulajile nha vyono

muli ling'ani!

27 U mulezi wenyu mulongozi hamonga

ne walongola wenyu, wose nha wetuma wubi kuli'ne.

28 Sunga ne watemi wenyu,

nawo wakahavufya i hanhu hangu hono helile.

Holeka ku soko yiyo,

nane nghaleta wongomezwe kuli Isilayeli.

Nghawaleka wanhu wangu Isilayeli watovulwe.”


Isaya 44

Muwaha Mulungu yo Mulungu yali wene du

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Wuhulicize 'gwe go nghungugo ya Isilayeli

go mwitumami wangu,

anye nyo wanhu wangu wahagulwa

nyo wana wa Yakobo.

2 Ane yo nene no Muwaha Mulungu mono

namuwumbenyi wulawa wulelwe wenyu.

Holeka mulece kogopa, anye nha muli

wetumami wangu.

Kanyhi muli wanhu wangu, wendece wangu.

3 Nane noyipela malenga yisi yono yina nyhilu,

kanyhi notula makolongo gahulume mu yisi nghavu.

Nane nowajidila zo muhe wangu i wana wenyu.

Kanyhi nowalugutiza we zindelo zenyu.

4 Nawo wocindika viswanu nhe mahanze mamwajile

viswanu malenga, wowa nho mubici wono wucindice

ha mivwilo ye malenga.”

5 “Nawo wanhu wolonga yumonga yumonga wawe kutya,

‘Ane nili wo Muwaha Mulungu.’

Nawo wolokuza wehangulize hamonga

ne wanhu wa Isilayeli.

Nawo wose munhu na lwakwe

kolokwiyandika litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu

mu mhukumhuku yakwe,

no kwiyitya vyono yaweza yumonga

ne wanhu wo Mulungu.”

6 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu mono yatemile, kanyhi

mono kawadimaga Waisilayeli

u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

yakutya nhavi,

“Ane yo nene mulongozi, na wo mundumo,

ane mono nili Mulungu nili wene,

na hasina kanyhi mulungu yunji langa nene du.

7 Wulenyi, hambi ho yuli yunji kanyhi

mono kusuma kuzituma zono ane netuma?

Hamba nani mbi mono yali yasume

kuwila zose nha vyono zolokwijela

ku wulawa katali ha mwamulizo, sunga

za ku vilo vya ku ndumo?

8 Holeka anye wanhu wangu, mulece kogopa!

Hambi nha mumanyile nha vyono ku wulawilila

mu vilo vye katali sunga nalu,

ane nali nakondya kuwila, nha vyono zizi zolokwijela,

kanyhi yo nyenye wahomelezi wangu.

Wulenyi, hambi ho yuli mulungu yunji kanyhi?

Hamba ho yuli mulungu yunji mono nayo yali vilungo

mono ane nghali namuhulika?”

Wuhemba vihwani wukubedegalwa

9 Nawo wanhu wono wakunoza vihwani viseswe, wose wapuwile, sunga ne vihwani vyawo vye milungu vyono wakuviguza ko wuguzi wukamu, navyo visina hamba cambuzi cidodo bwete. Nawo wanhu wawo wono wakuyihemba milungu ye vihwani viseswe, mbeka wabofu, kanyhi wasina wizuce hamba hadodo, kanyhi wokolwa cevu.
10 Kanyhi hamba si yinojeye munhu yenozeze cihwani cenjedulwe, yacinoze ciwe yo mulungu yacihembe!
11 Hambi nha munhu wo wose mono kocihemba, nha kokolwa soni. Nawo wanhu wono wakusesa vivyo vihwani, nha wanhu du, wono wasina cinji co cose. Holeka anye muwalekaje du, hambi nha wose wolokwima ha wuling'anizi, nawo wolokogofywa muno no kukolwa cevu.

12 Nayo u mutyani, nha yakuwa kusola cuma cidodo du, kawa kucinyanya mu moto. Hanyuma yakucinoza cuma cico mu hwani yono yakuyisaka mweneco. Ninga na lono yakutyana, katugaga nzala, ne nyhilu ye malenga, hamonga no mukatalo.
13 Nayo muselemala kapimaga lubawo ne cipimo cakwe. Kawa kuwicila citanjilo ne kalamu. Hanyuma kawa kuyisesa ne visesezo vyakwe, kawa kuyinoza yiwa yina hwani yo munhu ne futa yono yikwendeza, kawa kuciwika cihwani cico mu kaya yakwe.
14 Hanji yadahile kuyitemanga mibici yo muhagata, ho yayitumamile, hamba lunji mibici ye minghola, hamba mubici wo wose, wa mu mbago. Hamba lunji kohadika mubici wo muhagata, kawa kulindila mvula yiwutonyele yiwuwiluse.
15 Na hanyuma kawa kusola libici linji lyo mubici wuwo, so ngwagu, na linji kawa kusesa cihwani, Ne mabici ganji go mubici wuwo kawa kugetumamila nha ngwagu zono kokota ho zimupele civuce, no kutelecela ndigwa zakwe. Ne libici linji lyo mubici wuwo du, kawa kusesa mulungu, kawa kumuhemba.
16 Kanyhi ngwagu zinji, yakupembela moto, kawa kuwitumamila so kukasika nyama, no kuyilya, nayo kawa kwiguta. Hanyuma kawa kwiyoteza ha moto akuno yakutya, “Kaa! Nye civuce ciswanu si! Nye moto wanoga si!”
17 Ne mabici ganji gono gasigaye go mubici wuwo du, kawa kusesa cihwani, na hanyuma yayo ati, kawa kucinamila no kucihemba. Kawa kucilomba yakutya, “Agwe yo gwegwe mulungu wangu, nghukulomba wumhonye!”

18 Na mbeka wanhu we liwala lilyo, wapuwile, hamba si wakuzimanya bwete na zono wakwituma yaye. Hambi meso gawo hamonga ne mahala gawo, nha wafufunize, ho walece kuyimanya i mbeka.
19 Nayo yuyo mono kasesaga vivyo vihwani, yasina hamba lihweso lyo kutya, “Alu mwene mabici ganji ge libici lili, nha nigapembela. No moto wayo nhelecela ndigwa yangu, kanyhi nghasicila ne nyama yangu, niwa kuyilya. Hanyuma libici lyono lisigaye niwa kusesa cihwani. Naluno nicifugamiye cihwani ciseswe co mubici wu wulya!”
20 Ne nghani yiyi, yo nghani yo wulele, yihwanile wubwiga mavu. Hambi i mahweso gakwe go wupufi, nha gakondya kumwajiliza nzila, ho yalece kuyiwona nzila yono yina wutazi. Hamba si yakusuma kumanya, nha vyono cico cihwani cono yebate mu muwoko wakwe, si mulungu yaye.

Muwaha Mulungu yo Muwumbi, kanyhi yo Muponyi

21 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, nhavi,

“Anye nyo nghungugo ya Isilayeli,

mukumbucile ta nghani ayi,

nha vyono anye muli wetumami wangu.

Ane yo nene mono namuwumbenyi muwe wetumami wangu.

Holeka nane hamba si nomusemwenyi yaye.

22 Nakondya kuwupatila lukulu wubi wenyu nhe livunde.

Holeka mumbuyilaje.

Hambi nha yo nene du mono nghumuponyenyi.”

23 Mwimbe ne nyemo 'nye nyo wulanga!

Nanye nyo kusi ye 'si, mwimbe mwihindile muno!

Nanye nyo matunda hamonga ne mabici gose ga mu mbago,

mwimbe ne nyemo kwe mazi mawaha.

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha kawulajila wuwaha wakwe,

mu nzila yo kuwaponya wanhu wa Isilayeli.

24 “Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu wenyu, u muponyi wenyu.

Ane yo nene mono namuwumbenyi.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

u muwumbi we vinhu vyose

yo nene mono nawutandaza wulanga na lono nayiwumbile yinze,

hasina hamba monga mono yanhaziliza.

25 Yo nene mono nivimilaga vitanjilo vye watowa visangu,

no kuwapela lukwale i waganga,

yo nene mono niwalambizaga we mahala,

no kulajila vyono mahala gawo yo nghani ya wulele du.

26 Ninga kolo mwitumami wangu yakuwila,

nane kolo nghumutuma mwitumami wangu

ho yakalajile ze mahweso gangu,

nane niganozaga mahweso gago

hamonga no wuwile wuwo viwa vya mbeka.

Nane nghuyilonjela Yelusalemi,

nha vyono wanhu wowuya wekale mumo kanyhi,

na vyono ne makaya ga Yuda gozengwa kanyhi.

Ne makaya gago, i matongo gayo gokwinulwa.

27 Ane nongaga nghani du, niwa kulinyaza lilenga.

28 Nghumulonjela Kulo, nghutya,

‘Agwe yo gwegwe mono kowufisiliza wuhile wangu,

nagwe konoza zono nghukulajiliza

kololajiliza Yelusalemi yizengwe kanyhi

hamonga no musinji we Nyumba yo Mulungu.’ ”


Isaya 45

Muwaha Mulungu yakumuhagula Kulo

1 Muwaha Mulungu yamuhagula Kulo yawe mutemi!

Kamuwika ho yagasume makungugo

yawawusize watemi vilungo vyawo.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu komudigulila Kulo

milango ye makaya gose.

Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakumulonjela Kulo yakutya,

2 “Ane yo nene du mono nokuwikaniziza nzila yako,

nogatenjelemula matunda hamonga ne vigongo,

noyibenanga ne milango ye vyuma

hamonga ne madindilo gayo gose.

3 Nane nokupela viwutwa vyono vili hatitu

hanhu ha cinyele.

Nagwe baho ho komanyila

vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

Kanyhi na vyono Mulungu we Waisilayeli

yo yakucemile mwi'tagwa lyako.

4 Ane nghakuhagula agwe,

ho wumutaze mwitumami wangu Isilayeli,

wanhu wangu wono nawahagula.

Nane nakondya kukupela lilumbo yiwaha

hamba wuwe agwe si wumanyile 'ne.

5 “Ane yo nene du Muwaha Mulungu

hasina hamba mulungu yunji kanyhi, langa yo nene du.

Nane nokusikamya, hamba wuwe agwe si wumanyile'ne ng'o.

6 Nane nghuzinoza zizi ho wanhu wose ku wulawilila

ku mhelo ye yinze sunga ku lubavu luyagwe

ho wamanye vyono ane yo nene du Muwaha Mulungu,

na vyono hasina hamba mulungu yunji kanyhi

langa yo nene du.

7 Yo nene mono nawuwumba wuzelu no wutitu

yo nene mono ndetaga wumoteswe no wongomele.

Yo nene du Muwaha Mulungu, mono nizitumaga zizi zose.

8 Nane nololeta wusume ku wulawa ku wulanga nha mvula,

nayo yisi yolokwiwikaniza ho yiwubocele

na mu mapango gago wolowa mulamu

hamonga no wiling'ano.

Ane no Muwaha Mulungu mono nolozinoza zizi zijele.”

I vilungo viwaha vyo Mulungu

9 “Wulenyi! Hambi nyungu yono yihwanile zinyungu zose,

hongo yosuma kwisumaniza na mono yayiwumba?

Hambi lilongo nalyo,

lyosuma kumuwuza mono yakuliwumba liwe kutya,

alu wukunoza cici?

Hamba nyungu ma,

yosuma kumutya mono yayiwumbile yiwe kutya, aa,

agwe si wumanyile kuwumba?

10 Hamba ho yuli munhu wo wose,

mono kosuma kwijelela yawatye walezi wakwe,

‘Alu nhawule mukandela nili nhavivi?’

11 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu mono yelile

wa Isilayeli, mono kawikanizaga zose, yakutya, nhavi,

‘Agwe wusina soko yo yose yo kumbuza-mbuziza ze wanangu,

hamba kunonjeleza zono zinojeye nitume!’

12 Hambi nha yo nene mono nayiwumba yinze,

hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wekaye mumo.

Na mu vilungo vyangu, nghawutandaza wulanga.

Kanyhi yo nene mono nghulilajiliza lizuwa, no mwezi,

hamonga ne zinyelezi.

13 Ane yo nene mono namusagahiza Kulo

ho yawufisilize wuhile wangu

yaziwice zose mu mugoloka.

Nane nozigolosa zinzila zakwe zose mono kowa kukola.

Mwene yo kololizenga kanyhi likaya lyangu lya Yelusalemi.

Nayo kowawika welecele i wawingwa wangu wose,

nayo hamba si kolopegwa malipo yaye,

hamba cihumbilizwa co cose ho yetume zizo.”

Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

14 Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela we nghungugo ya Isilayeli yakutya,

“Ne sawo ya mu yisi ya Misili,

na ya mu yisi ya Kushi, yose yowa yenyu nyenye.

Nawo i wanhu watali muno wa ku Seba,

awo wowa wetumami wenyu,

womutyatyenyi akuno wawope ne manyolo.

Wofugama ha mwande yenyu wawe kutya,

‘Mbeka Mulungu yali hamonga nanye,

kanyhi mweneco yo Mulungu du yali wene.

15 Mulungu we Waisilayeli mono yakuwaponya

wanhu wakwe mbeka yo Mulungu mono yehundice yali wene.

16 Ne wanhu wono wasesaga vihwani

wose wolokozwa soni,

na wose hamonga wolopuvywa.

17 Ninga Isilayeli koponywa no Muwaha Mulungu

ne mhonelo yakwe yowa ya cibitilila,

ne wanhu wakwe, hamba si wolokozwa soni yaye.’ ”

18 No Muwaha Mulungu yo Mulungu du yali wene

mono yawuwumba wulanga!

Kanyhi yo yayisimya no kuyiwumba yinze

yakayinoza yisikame no kwikala cibitilila.

Hamba si yayiwumba ho yiwe yitongo du lya caka,

langa yayiwumba yiwe wikalo we wanhu.

Kanyhi yo mweneco mono yakutya,

“Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu du

hamba hasina kanyhi mulungu yunji, langa nene du.

19 Nane hamba si nonjile zizi cinyele, hamba

kuwuhundika yaye wuhile wangu.

Hamba lunji kuwalomba wanhu wa Isilayeli

ho wandime mu yisi nyhiwa yono ndekwe.

Ane yo nene no Muwaha Mulungu, nane nghulonga ze mbeka,

no kuzinoza zo mugoloka zimanyice.”

U Muwaha Mulungu we yinze ne vihwani vya Babiloni

20 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ale! Mwikongholelaje hamonga,

anye nyo wanhu we makungugo gose,

wose mono muponile mu wugwe wo wutemi wa Babiloni,

muze wose ku wuling'anizwe!

Anye nyo wanhu mono mudinghulaga

vihwani vyenyu vye mabici viseswe,

muwa kulomba kuli milungu yono si yomuponyenyi yaye.

Anye nyo wanhu walele!

21 Ale nzonyi muzilonje zenyu ha mahakama,

muwalece wono wasitakwe walongane weneco wose.

Alu mwene mukwihwesa nani mono yawila

mu vilo vye katali vyono zizi zolokwijela?

Yo nene du Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu

mono kawaponyaga wanhu wakwe.

Na hasina mulungu yunji, langa nene du.

22 “Muze kuli'ne, ho mupone

anye nyo wanhu we yinze yose!

Ane no Mulungu nili wene, nili baha.

23 No wihuwizo wangu wa mbeka, hamba si wopituka yaye.

Kanyhi nelahiza, nghehuwiza ko mulomo wangu

nha vyono wanhu wose wolokuza wafugamile nene,

wehuwize nha vyono wowa wali cihuliko kuli'ne.

24 “Nawo wololonga wawe kutya vyono

wusume ne vilungo, vyose vili muli'ne,

ninga wono wasulile'ne, wose wolokozwa soni.

25 Nene no Muwaha Mulungu nolowaponya we ndelo yose

ya Yakobo, nawo wolonghumya no kunumbiza nene du.


Isaya 46

1 “Alu ayi yo ndumilizo ye milungu ya Babiloni!

Hambi Beli na Nebo, vyono wavihembaga katali,

ninga aluno vyeza vili mucanyha ye zindogowe,

vyeza mutegulo muzito mu migongo ye zinimu

zono zikataye.

2 Navyo lu vihwani hamba si vyosuma kwiponya vyeneco.

Hambi nha vyakondya kusolwa no kutegulwa kutali.

Holeka ayi yo ndumilizo ye milungu ya Babiloni!

3 “Ale, ta muhulicize anye nyo wana wa Yakobo,

anye mo masigazwa ge wanhu wangu.

Ane yo nene mono namukocenyi ku wulawa wulelwe wenyu.

4 Ane yo nene Mulungu wenyu, nane nomukamisenyi,

na mu wukombi wenyu sunga zimwi zicindice

ane nomudimenyi.

Hambi nha yo nene mono namuwumbenyi,

holeka nomudimenyi kanyhi nomupelenyi wutazi no kumuponyenyi.”

5 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Alo mwene monyihwananiza na nani?

Hamba ho yuli yunji mono yanyihwanile nene?

6 Wanhu wawopolaga vifuko vyawo wawa kumwaga

zahabu hasi, wakuwa kupima fweza mu vipimo.

Hanyuma wawa kumutuga mutyani yawatyanile mulungu,

hanyuma wawa kwinama ha mwande yo mulungu

yuyo wawa kumuhemba!

7 Na hanyuma yayo wawa kumupika mu mawega gawo

mulungu yuyo wakumudinghula.

Wakumusimya hanhu hanji,

kawa kwima baho du hamba kusuma kutela hanji hamba hadodo.

Na hono munhu yabite baho yakamulombe,

mulungu yuyo we fweza,

si yakusuma kumupitula,

hamba kumupela wutazi wo wose wa mu magayo gakwe.

8 “Holeka anye nyo wabi, muzikumbucilaje zi zino,

muzihwese zono nakondya kwituma.

9 Kanyhi muzikumbucile na zono zejela katali,

ho mumanye nha vyono, ane yo nene du Mulungu,

na vyono hasina mulungu yunji mono yehwanile nene.

10 Nakondya kuwila ku wulawa katali

zono zolokuza na ku wulawa katali

nali nakondya kuzilonga zono zolokwijela.

Nane nghalonga nghatya

vyono mahweso gangu si galasumwa yaye,

langa nokwituma zose du nha vyono

nali nihwesize nitume.

11 Nomucemeleza munhu yaze ku wulawa ku

lubavu lwa kucilima,

nayo koloswepa hasi nha mwewe yazifisilize zose zono nizihwesize.

Nazilonga, nazo si zoleka kwitumwa.

12 “Ale, muhulicizaje ta,

anye nyo wanhu mono musina cihuliko,

mono mwihwesaga nha vyono wiling'ano wuli kutali.

13 Nowusejeleza wiling'ano wangu

nawo hamba si wuli kutali yaye.

No wusume wangu hamba si wocela yaye

noyiponya Yelusalemi,

niwapele ne lilumbo lyawo wanhu wa Isilayeli.”


Isaya 47

Wuling'anizwe wa Babiloni

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Gwe Babiloni, alu wuwuce mwi'goda lyako lyo wutemi,

wutime wikale hasi mu muhangha.

Halulongozi wali winha muhinza muswanu

likaya lyono silisumwaga.

Ninga aluno hamba si kocemwa kanyhi mupolo

hamba wo wulenzi!

Aluno, wawa muwanda!

2 Alu wusole zinzala!

Wusaje wusaje!

Wuwuse citambala cako co kugubika wusu wako,

wuyiwopole ne myenda yako miswanu!

Wukunyaje mwenda wako kusika, ho wulowoce vikolongo!

3 Nawo wanhu nhondo wokuwona vyono wuli cipwi,

wokuwona vyono wenenenyheza,

kanyhi wukuwona cevu.

Nane nolomulipicizenyi,

na hasina munhu mono kolonyimila.”

4 Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli kakondya kucikomboza,

ne litagwa lyakwe, mweneco,

yo Mulungu we mihanga.

5 Muwaha Mulungu yakuyilonjela Babiloni yakutya nhavi,

“Wunyamale twii, mu wutitu,

hambi nha hasina munhu wo wose

mono kokucema kanyhi nha wuli mugolece we likulu, we makungugo!

6 Nali niwakoleywe likuwo wanhu wangu

nghawa kuwatendela nha kotya si wanhu wangu

Nghawawika mu miwoko yako.

Nagwe hamba si wawawonela lusungu yaye,

sunga ne wakombi wose wukawatendela vibi muno.

7 Hambi nha wehwesaga nha vyono kojendelela

kuwa mugolece we likulu vilo vyose,

hamba kuzihwesa zizi zose mu nhumbula yako,

nha vyono zose zina ndumo.

8 “Ale, wuhulicize ta zono nghukulonjela

agwe go mwenda kwiyendeza go mweneco,

mono kwihwesaga vyono wuli mu mulamu,

na kanyhi wupolile.

Kanyhi wukuwa kwilumba ti,

wukutya agwe yo gwegwe du,

hasina yunji mono yehwanile gwegwe.

Na kanyhi kwihwesaga nha vyono si kolowa wuli mucekulu mufwile.

Hamba kufwilwa ne wanago yaye.

9 Ninga wumanye nha vyono,

zizi zose zolokwijela kamonga du mwi'zuwa limonga.

Na ku soko ye cibede cako na so witumamila wuganga wako,

kolofwilwa no mulume wako, hamonga ne wanago.

10 “Hambi nha wawitumbila wubalya wako

vyono wehwesaga nha hasina yunji mono yakukuwona.

Ninga kumbe mahala gako hamonga no wizuce wako,

vikakutonga vibi, nagwe baho,

wukawa kwidunhumula no kwiyitya,

‘Ane yo nene du, na hasina yunji kanyhi mono yehwanile nene.’

11 Holeka ku soko yiyo, magazo gokuziza,

no wuganga wako hamba si wosuma kugemila yaye.

No wongomele wokuziza nhinicizo

nawo wowa wongomele wono wukali hamba nawulota yaye!

12 Alu wuwice wuganga wako hamonga ne zimhiji zako

zono wetumamilaga wulawa wudodo wako,

na lunji zokupela wutazi wudodo,

na lunji kosuma kuwogofya walugu wako.

13 Nalu nha weza wusina kanyhi vilungo

ku soko yo wulanjizwe wono wukupegwa.

Alu, wuwalekaje wawo waganga wako waze wakuponye,

i waganga we zinyelezi, hamonga na wono

wazandikaga zinghani za mu wulanga,

nawo wakulonjele mu mwezi na mwezi,

zinghani zono zokwijelela gwegwe.

14 “Ninga wumanye vyono wawo wose wokwihwana mawaje

no moto wowakongojeza wose!

Nawo hamba si wolosuma kwiponya wali wene yaye

hambi malambi go moto nha golowasumiza,

holowa hasina kanyhi moto wo kota, ho watuje civuce.

15 Na zizo zo swanu zono wolokutendela

wawo waganga wono kwikalaga wuwetumbiye

mu mikalile yako yose.

Ninga wumanye nha vyono wawo wose, wolokuleka wuli wene,

wawe kubita munhu kwakwe, munhu kwakwe,

na holowa hasina hamba monga mono

kolosigala ho yakuponye.”


Isaya 48

Mulungu yo yuyo Muwaha Mulungu, wa mu vilo vyono vikuza

1 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

mono mwalawa mu lulelo lwa Yuda, ta muhulicize,

Anye mwelahiza mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

mukatya nha vyono momuhemba Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

ninga hamba kwituma nha vyono mwalonga.

2 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

mukali mukwilumba mukwiyitya

nha vyono anye muli we likaya lyono lyelile,

na kanyhi mumwitumbiye Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

mono yakucemwa yo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.

3 Ninga nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakumutyenyi nhavi nyo Isilayeli,

“Ane naziwila zinghani zizi wulawa katali,

nha vyono zokuza, nane nghazinoza zijele ko musunho muno.

4 Ninga nali manyile vyono agwe kowa wusina cihuliko,

hambi nha wuli mukavu wihwanile cuma,

kanyhi ne lihanda lyo wusu wako lihwanile shaba.

5 Holeka nane nghakuwilila wulawa katali zono zilakwagana,

nghazilonga zono zilejela,

lono zikali si zinawa ho nghwimile wulece kwihwesa

nha vyono vihwani vyako viseswe,

hamonga ne vihwani vyako vyenjedulwe,

vyo vizinozize zizo zijele.”

6 “Na zose zono nazilonga, alu zikwijela,

holeka yikunojeye wihuwile

nha vyono wuwile wangu wali wa mbeka.

Alu lece ta nghulonjele zimhya zono zikuza,

zinghani zono nali nghali hamba nazilonga ku wulawa katali.

7 Nazo nghuzinoza lulu du, hamba si za katali yaye,

ninga agwe wali wukali nazihulika yaye,

ho wulece kutya vyono wali wuzimanyile

ku wulawa na katali.

8 Ninga nali manyile vyono kowa wuli mwihuwilwa,

hambi nha wali wumanyice vilo vyose

nha vyono wali wuli muhodanji.

Na yiyi yo soko yono yikaleka si wasuma

hamba hadodo kuzihulika zizi,

na yo yaleka wulece kuhulika nghani hamba monga.”

9 “Nane ngheyimila likuwo lyangu ndece kukongomeza,

Nawo wanhu walikumye litagwa lyangu.

10 Nane nghakujeleka mu moto we magazo,

nha vyono fweza yikwemwaga mu moto.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo nghakuwona si wukwiliye yaye.

11 Na zose zono nitumile, nizitumila mu wuhile wangu,

Hamba si nolecela lukulu litagwa lyangu litovulwe

hamba kuleka yunji yahanje nane lukumyo,

hambi lukumyo lulo, nha lwangu du nene nili wene.”

Kulo u mulongola muhagulwa wo Muwaha Mulungu

12 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nye Isilayeli, nyo wanhu wono namucemenyi,

alu ta muhulicize zangu zono nghumulonjelenyi!

Ane yo nene Mulungu, mono nili wo mwamulizo,

na wo mudumo, u Mulungu monga du.

13 Ne miwoko yangu yo yayiwika misinji ye yinze

nghawutandaza no wulanga,

nane kolo nghuzicema wulanga ne yinze,

zikuwa kuza kamonga du ha mwande yangu.

14 “Holeka mwihangulize wose hamonga,

muhulicize zono nghulonga, hambi nha hasina milungu yinji,

yono yosuma kuwila nha vyono munhu mono nimuhaguye,

kosuma kuyihoma Babiloni,

ninga mwene kolokwituma du zono nghusaka yetume.

15 Hambi nha yo nene mono nalonga no kumucema,

nane nomulongoza no kumupela wumoteswe.

16 Alu, musejelele behi nane, muhulice zono nghulonga,

hambi 'ne nha nongaga calinze ku wulawa mwamulizo,

ne zinghani zangu ziwezaga za mbeka vilo vyose.”

Nalu u Muwaha Mulungu yanhuma no kumhela vilungo.

U wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu kuli wanhu wakwe

17 Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli, u Muwaha

Mulungu mono yakumuponyenyi yakutya nhavi,

“Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu,

u Mulungu mono yakuhila kumwijizenyi ziswanu,

no kumutonjenyi nzila yono moyityatya.

18 Alu yali yiwe yili ciba le,

hono mwali mugebate malajilizo gangu!

Holeka mwali muwutuje no wumoteswe wuwaha,

wono wihwanile nhe likolongo lyono si linyalaga!

No wusume wali wumuzizenyi nhe mawingo ge lilenga.

19 Ne wanago wali wenjipe, wawe wenji nho musawawa,

nane nali niwadime ho walece kongomezwa.”

20 Ale muwuce mu Babiloni, mutize!

Muziganule zinghani zizo na nyemo,

muzimanyize hanhu hose muwe kutya,

“U Muwaha Mulungu kakondya kuwaponya Isilayeli

i wetumami wakwe!”

21 Na lono Muwaha Mulungu yawalongoza wanhu wakwe,

no kuwakoza mu ciligati ye nyika mwi'zuwa likali,

hamba si watamiswa yaye ne nyhilu ye malenga.

Hambi mwene Mulungu nha yanoza malenga

galawe mwi'talawe ku soko yawo.

Yatula litalawe, malenga gakalawila mumo.

22 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Hasina mulamu kuli wanhu wabi.”


Isaya 49

Isilayeli wo Wuzelu kuli Makungugo

1 Nanye nyo makungugo go kutali,

anye nyo wanhu mono mwikaye kutali,

muhulicize ta zono nghulonga, nghutya,

Muwaha Mulungu yahagula ane,

yakalitambula litagwa lyangu na lono nali

nghali nalelwa, yakambika niwe mwitumami wakwe.

2 Yakazinoza zinghani zangu ziwe kuhoma nho mumaje.

Kanyhi yakanyhitila no muwoko wakwe.

Yakanoza 'ne niwe nha songa nghali muno

yono yiwikanizwe ku wugume.

3 Hanyuma yakanonjela yakutya,

“Isilayeli, agwe wuli mwitumami wangu

hambi nha ku soko ya gwegwe,

wanhu wose wonghumya.”

4 Nane nghamupitula nghatya,

“Nepiganza bwete, nghetuma sunga nghakatala

so cinhu cono cisina cambuzi.”

Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

nomwitumbila Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo yo koyitetela ling'ani yangu,

kanyhi yo kohumbiliza mu nghango ye metumo gangu.

5 Muwaha Mulungu yahagula 'ne na lono nali

nghali hamba nalelwa,

yakanoza niwe mwitumami wakwe,

ho niwagomole kwakwe wanhu wakwe,

niwakonghole i wanhu wa Isilayeli wono weyagaye.

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha yo yakumhela lilumbo,

kanyhi mwene vyo vilungo vyangu.

6 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakutya,

“Nina mulimo muwaha wono nghuwiciye agwe

go mwitumami wangu.

Ninga si mulimo wo kuwagomola du mu lukumyo

i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wono wasigaye,

langa nokunoza kanyhi wuwe wuzelu kuli makungugo,

i yinze yose ho yisume kuponywa.”

7 Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli,

u Mukombozi wawo yakumulonjela mono yakubedegalwa ne wanhu,

na mono yakusulwa ne makungugo,

na kuli mwitumami we watemi, yakutya nhavi,

“Watemi kolo wakukuwona,

wokwinucila walajile cogopo cawo kuli'gwe,

ne wana we watemi, nawo woziwona zizo,

nawo wokwinama hasi, wakupele lilumbo.”

Na zizi zose zolokwijela,

vyono Muwaha Mulungu kakondya kumuhagula mwitumami wakwe,

U Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli,

mwene kakamisaga wihuwizo wakwe.

Wugomolwe wa Yelusalemi

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela wanhu wakwe yakutya,

“Kolo lizuwa lyo kumuponyenyi lyakondya kuza,

ane nomuponyenyi,

nomupitulenyi cililo cenyu co kusaka wutazi.

Nolomudimenyi no kumucitilenyi,

hamba muwe mwali mwakondya kuwika

cilagane ne wanhu wose co kusaka wutazi.

Kanyhi nolomudimenyi no kumucitilenyi,

so cilagane cono nacinoza ne wanhu wose muli nyenye.

Nane nomuwicenyi mwikale kanyhi mu yisi yenyu

yono yali lyeza yitongo.

9 Nane lulo nolowalonjela wono wali mu jela nitye,

‘Ale, muwuce mubite, mwakondya kuwopolelwa!’

Nawo wono wali mu wutitu, nolowalonjela nitye,

‘Ale, nanye muwuce mulawile ku wuzelu!’

Hambi nha wolowa wenha zingholo zono

zikudima mu matunda,

10 nawo si wolodabuka yaye,

hamba kutuga nyhilu ya malenga.

Nalyo lizuwa hamonga ne livuce lya mu nyika,

si vyolowatamisa yaye,

hambi nha wolowa wali mu wutonji wa mono yawendile.

Mwene kowatonga ku mivwilo ye malenga.

11 “Nane nonoza nzila mbaha yikolele mu matunda,

no kuyinozeleza nzila yono woyikolela wanhu wangu.

12 Nawo wanhu wangu, wolokuza ku wulawilila kutali,

ku wulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka na kumwezi,

na ku wulawilila ku Aswani ku lubavu lwa kutakama.”

13 Ale mwimbe anye nyo wulanga!

Nagwe go yinze wimbe no wusangalale!

Matunda nago gembe na nyemo mbaha!

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha kowasindamika wanhu wakwe,

kowawonela lusungu wanhu wakwe wono wali mu magayo.

14 Ninga i wanhu wa Yelusalemi wakatya,

“U Muwaha Mulungu yakondya kucileka!

Yakondya kucisemwa!”

15 Ninga nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakupitula yakutya,

“Alu mwene mbeka nyina yo munhu kosuma kumusemwa

u mwanagwe mono yamulela, hamba si komwenda yaye?

Ninga hamba yiwe nyina yo mwana yasume kumusemwa

mwanagwe, ane langa si nomusemwelenyi lukulu yaye.

16 Gwe Yelusalemi, ane hamba si nokusemwela lukulu!

Hambi nha nakondya kukwandika litagwa lyako

mu maganza ge miwoko yangu.

17 “Hambi wawo wono wokuzenga kanyhi,

nha wakuza musunho,

nawo wono wakakunoza wuwe yitongo,

wowuka muli gwegwe.

18 Ale, winule meso gako wulanje ako na ako

wuwone cono cikuza!

I wanhu wako weza kwihanguliza hamonga

wakuwuya kono kwawo!

Na mbeka, nha vyono ane nili Mulungu mumi,

nagwe kolowa sangalalila wanhu wako,

hambi nha kolowa nha bibi halusi mono yavwalile wihambo.

19 “Hambi yisi yako nha yali yakondya kulekwa

lyawa yitongo,

ninga aluno yolowa yisi finyu yono si yolowakwila wose

wono wolokuza wekale mumo.

Na wose wono wakunoza wuwe yitongo,

wolowuswa wawe kutali nagwe.

20 Ne wanhu wako wono walelelwa ku wuhamywe,

lizuwa linji wolokutya,

‘Alu yisi yiyi, nha yeza finyu muno,

nase lu ho cikusaka wikalo wugazi muno!’

21 Ale, nagwe baho, kolokwanza kulonga wuli wene wukutya,

‘Alu mwene nani mono yandelela wana wawa?

Hambi nha nali nakondya kufwilwa ne wana wose

nghawa sina hamba mwana monga.

Nali nili ku wuhamywe, nghawa nawingwa,

alu nani mono yawawilusa wana wawa?

Nha nali nalekwa nekala nili wene,

alu wana wawa ho walawa hayi wene?’ ”

22 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga yakulonga

ne wanhu wakwe yakutya,

“Ane nololajila citanjilo kuli makungugo,

nawo wolowagomola wanago kono kaya.

23 Ne watemi nawo wolowa nha wasogo,

nawo i wagolece we likulu wose, wolowa nhe wanyina zako,

hambi nha wolokwinamila

ha mwande yako no kukupela lilumbo,

kanyhi wolokwinenenyheza hali'gwe

wakulajila cogopo cawo hali'gwe.

Nagwe baho ho kolomanyila vyono ane yo nene

Muwaha Mulungu.

Kanyhi hasina munhu wo wose mono yanyitumbiye

mono kolokozwa cevu.”

24 Wule!

Wukwihwesa kosuma kumuboka musilikale mateka gakwe?

Hamba kosuma kuwaboka wafungwa

mu miwoko yo munhu mono yali vilungo?

25 Muwaha Mulungu yakupitula yakutya,

“Na zizo zo zokwijela,

Hambi i wafungwa wo musilikale nha wosolwa wose,

sunga ne mateka go munhu mono yali vilungo,

nago gosolwa gose.

Hambi nha nokwisumaniza no munhu mono

yakwisumaniza nagwe, ho niwaponye wanago.

26 Nane nowanoza wono wakukwiwonela

wewulaje weneco wose.

Nawo wogala no wiwulaji hamonga ne makuwo,

Baho ne wanhu wose ho womanya vyono,

ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Muponyi wako,

ane yo nene u Mukombozi wako we vilungo wa Yakobo.

Mono nghukuponya no kukuleka wuli mwelecele.

Kanyhi ho womanya nha vyono yo nene

Mulungu nili Muponyi wako,

no Mukombozi wako na we vilungo wa Yakobo.”


Isaya 50

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Alu mukwihwesa nha vyono nali nawawinga wanhu wangu,

nha vyono munhu kamupelaga mucekulu wakwe talaka?

Alu ceti ce talaka ho cili hayi lo?

Hamba mukwihwesa nha vyono nali nawaguza ku wuhamywe lo,

nho munhu mono kaguzaga wanagwe ku wuwanda?

Ng'o ye!

Anye mwabita ku wuhamywe ku soko yo wubi wenyu,

mwabita kuko, ku soko ye masutya genyu.”

2 Alu so cici wanhu wangu si wanhuciza

lono nawabitila ho niwaponye?

Na so cici lo, na lono nawacemaga,

hamba si wanyidika yaye?

Hamba wakuwona ane nili musocele

si nosuma kuwaponya lo?

Alo wene si wamanyile nha vyono

ndahile kulilajiliza lilenga linyale lwa kamonga,

no kuganyaza makolongo, yiwe nyika,

ne zisomba za mumo zifwe so wula malenga.

3 “Kanyhi na vyono nosuma kuganoza mavunde gawe matitu,

hawe nha ho cili cililo co kuwalilila wono wafwiye.”

I cihuliko co Mwitumami wo Muwaha Mulungu

4 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu nha yanyijiza zono nolonga,

ho sume kuwasikamya wono wakataye muno.

Nhondo zose hakali mitondo,

kanozaga niwe muhidimbazi kuzihulika zono yakunyijiza.

5 Muwaha Mulungu yamhelile wizuce,

nane nghali namubeza, hamba kumuleka yaye.

6 Nawapitucila mugongo wangu wanhu wono wanhowaga,

na lono wanhovulaga, ane si nawemila yaye,

sunga na lono wanghulaga zindevu zangu no

kunhowa mu wusu wangu.

7 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

matovulo gawo si ganhamisa yaye,

hambi u Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga

nha yamhela wutazi.

Nane nghekamiliza, nghagakangalila.

Hambi nha nali manyile vyono si nokozwa cevu,

8 hambi Mulungu nha yali behi nane.

Na vyono kanyhi kombala ane sina wubi.

Hambi nani kanyhi mono kokwijelela yesumanize nane?

Ale, yaze cibitane ku mahakama yakasitace!

9 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga

nha yo kasumilaga 'ne.

Alo nani kanyhi mono konyitya nili musutyi?

Wono wakusitaka wose nha wotiza,

wosakala wose nho mwenda wono wulijigwe ne makuni.

10 Nanye wose mono mumuhulice Muwaha Mulungu,

no kuzihulika zinghani zo mwitumami wakwe,

mumanye nha vyono mokola mu nzila yo wutitu tuku.

Ninga anye mumwitumbile du Muwaha Mulungu

mumwegamile Mulungu wenyu.

11 Nanye wose mono mwikalaga mukwihwesa zo wuhile

wubi wo wuhodanga lilumbo lye wayenyu,

nyenye yo mokwiyongomeza no

wuhile wenyu wubi nyo weneco.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yo kozinoza zizo ziwe nhavivyo.

Nanye molotuga magayo mawaha muno muno.


Isaya 51

Zinghani zo kuyisindamika Yelusalemi

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ale, muhulicizaje anye wose mono mukuhila

muponywe,

anye wose mono mukunyiziza ho mutuje wutazi.

Alu mwihwesaje ze litalawe lyono mwatijila,

mono mwaseswa mu wulawa mumo,

ne likombo lye mabwe mono mwahimbulwa.

2 Kanyhi mwihwese na zo mulezi wenyu Abulahamu,

na za Sala mono yamulelenyi.

Kanyhi mumanye na vyono lono namucemela Abulahamu,

lulo yali yasina hamba mwana monga,

ninga ane nghamulugutiza, nghamupela ne wana.

Nghawanoza wanagwe wawe wenji muno muno.

3 “Nane noyiwonela lusungu Yelusalemi,

hamonga na wose wono wekaye mumo mu matongo.

Ne yisi yawo hamba yiwe yawa nha nyika,

ninga ane noyinoza yiwe busitani,

yiwe nhe busitani yono nayihadika ku Adeni.

Nayo nyemo hamonga no wusangalale vyowa mumo,

hamonga ne zinyimbo zo kunumbiza, ne malumbo ga kuli'ne.

4 “Alu muhulicizaje ta 'nye nyo wanhu wangu,

muhulicize kuli zono nghulonga.

Ane nghuwejiza i mejizo gangu we makungugo,

ne malajilizo gangu gowaletela wuzelu.

5 Nane nowaziza musunho niwaponye

hambi cipango cangu co wusume nha casejelela.

Nane notema nili wene mu makungugo.

Sunga ne ma'si go kutali,

gose ganindiye nene sunga nilaza kuza.

Hambi nha ganindiye ko witumbilo wuwaha

nize niwaponye.

6 Ale, mugalamile kucanyha muwulanje wulanga,

muyilanje kanyhi ne yinze!

Mumanye vyono wuwo wulanga wokwajilila

nha lyosi yinze nayo,

yolosakala nha mwenda muhuma,

ne wanhu wayo wolofwa nha mahazi.

Ninga wukombozi wono nghuwuleta,

awo langa wokwikala cibitilila.

No wiling'ano wangu si wolowembulwa yaye.

7 “Ale, muhulicizaje ta anye wose

mono muzimanyile ze ling'ani,

anye wose mono mugebate mejizo gangu

mu zinhumbula zenyu.

Mulece kuwa no woga lono wanhu wakumulijenyi no kumutovulenyi,

8 hambi nha wosakala wose nho mwenda wono wulijigwe ne makuni!

Ninga wukombozi wono nghuwuleta,

awo langa wokwikala cibitilila.

No wiling'ano wangu wokwikala cibitilila.”

9 Ale wusinhuce go Muwaha Mulungu wucitaze!

Witumamile vilungo vyako

nha vyono wavitumamila katali, wuciponye.

Hambi nha yo gwegwe mono walitemanga

vipiligwa-piligwa lizoka lya mwi'lenga lyono likucemwa Lahabu.

10 Kanyhi nha yogwegwe mono walinyaza lilenga

wukayinoza nzila muciligati ye malenga

ho walowoce i wanhu wono wawakomboza.

11 Ne wanhu wose wono wawakomboza,

wolofika ku Yelusalemi wena nyemo

akuno wakwimba ne mazi mawaha ko wusangalale.

Nawo wosangalala cibitilila,

kanyhi wolowa wasina wukalale no wusungu cibitilila.

12 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ane, yo nene du, mono nghuwa kumusikamyenyi.

Alu nhawule mukumogopa munhu we nghuli

mono si kekalaga vilo vinji, yehwanile mahanze?

13 Alu mwamusemwa Muwaha Mulungu mono yamuwumbenyi,

mono yawutandaza wulanga, no kusimya misinji ye yinze?

Alu nhawule mukuhila mwikale mu woga,

ku soko yo wukali wa wawo wono wakumwiwonelenyi,

no wukali wawo wono wewikanize wamongomezenyi?

Ninga mumanye vyono wukali

wawo si womupamizenyi anye yaye.

14 Na wawo wono wali mu jela,

nawo wololecelwa mbela mbela,

kanyhi wolokwikala vilo vinji,

hamonga no kulya ndigwa yo yose yono wakuyisaka.

15 “Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wako,

mono nhibulaga lilenga,

niganoza mawingo galyo gawa kubula.

Ne litagwa lyangu,

nene Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga!

16 Yo nene mono nawutandaza wulanga,

nghasimya ne misinji ye yinze,

nghayilonjela Yelusalemi nghatya,

‘Anye muli wanhu wangu!

Nakondya kumwijizenyi mejizo gangu,

kanyhi nghumucitilenyi no muwoko wangu.’ ”

I ndumilizo yo wugazwe wa Yelusalemi

17 Gwe Yelusalemi, ta wusinhuce ye!

Wukulumhuce winuce!

Alu wagala cikombe co wulipicizwe cono Muwaha Mulungu

yakung'weseze mwi'kuwo lyakwe wung'we.

Nagwe 'lu wakondya kung'wa wagala, nalu

weza wakupebesuka so wugale.

18 Nalu hasina munhu wo wose mono kokutonga nzila,

hamba mu wanhu wako,

hasina mono kokwibata muwoko wako yakutonje nzila.

19 Alu magayo mejete gakondya kukuziza gwegwe.

Yisi yako yakondya kuhodangwa no wulugu,

ne wanhu wako nawo wagwilwa ne lizala.

Na hasina hamba monga mono yakukuwonela lusungu.

20 Ne wanhu wako wagwiye mu vipeto vyose vye zinzila

ku soko yo wusocele,

wehwana nhe sawada yono yigwiliye mu mutego wo mupelembi.

Wamemile wutacilwe we likuwo lyo Mulungu.

21 Nye nyo wanhu wa Yelusalemi mono muli mu wugaye,

anye mono mukupebesuka nha mugalile wujimbi,

22 Muwaha Mulungu u Mulungu wenyu mono

yakumudimenyi yakutya nhavi,

“Alu, neza kuciwusa cikombe cono namupelelenyi

mwi'kuwo lyangu.

Alu hodu si mojendelela kanyhi kung'wa yaye,

wujimbi wono wukumunozenyi muwa kupebesuka.

23 Alu, nowang'wesa wawo wono wali kumwiwonelenyi

wawo wono wakamunozenyi mugone hasi mu zinzila,

wakawa kumukanyanjizenyi hasi nha muli manghundi.”


Isaya 52

Mulungu koloyiponya Yelusalemi

1 Gwe Yelusalemi, weze ne vilungo,

wuwuvwale wuwaha wako kanyhi!

Agwe 'gwe kaya lyono lyelile lyo Mulungu,

wivwikaje kanyhi nhojo yako!

Hambi i wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu,

nha si wolokwinjilila lukulu kanyhi mu milango yako.

2 Wikung'unhaje wilejehe 'gwe Yelusalemi,

winukaje wuwuce mu manghundi,

wikale mwi'goda lyako lyo wutemi!

Muwopole manyolo gono gali gamuwopilenyi

anye nyo wanhu wa Sayuni!

3 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga, yakuwalonjela wanhu wawa yakutya, “Anye mwaguzwa bwete ku wawanda, kanyhi mokombozwa bwete, si molavywa hamba sendi monga yaye.
4 Na lono mwabitila kwikala ku Misili nha muli wajenzi, mwabita ko kwiyendela nyenye nyo weneco. Ninga Mwashuli yakamusolenyi kwe vilungo, yakamutegulenyi kutali, hamba si mwalipwa cinhu co cose yaye.
5 Nalu kanyhi zikejela zo zi zilya na ku Babiloni. Anye mwasolwa, ninga hamba si mwalipwa cinji yaye. Na wose wono wakuwa kumutemenyi wakuwa kwilumba no kwivuna hamonga no kulajila lubedegalo kuli'ne.
6 Ninga mu vilo vyono vikuza, agwe ho kolomanyila nha vyono ane yo nene Mulungu mono nalonga kuli'gwe.”

7 Yo nghani yo wizine lono wukuwona munhu yakuza

yakolile mu matunda, yakuleta nghani swanu,

nghani yo mulamu!

Mono yakuganula zo wusume, yakuyitya Sayuni,

“Mulungu wako yo mutemi!”

8 Nawo walindiliza likaya, wazozize hamonga so nyemo!

Hambi nha wakuziwona ne meso gawo weneco,

vyono Muwaha Mulungu yakuwuya ku Sayuni!

9 Nanye nyo matongo ga Yelusalemi, muzoze so nyemo!

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha koliponya likaya lyakwe

no kuwasindamika wanhu wakwe.

10 Muwaha Mulungu kolokwitumamila vilungo vyakwe

vyono vyelile, yawaponye wanhu wakwe,

ne yinze yose yoloziwona zizo.

11 Mumanye vyono wose mwakondya kuwuka mu Babiloni,

anye wose mono muvidinghulaga viya vye Nyumba

yo Mulungu!

Kanyhi mulecele lukulu ku vipamiza vinhu vyavu,

Mutuluce muwuce, akuno mwelile.

12 Ninga muhinzo wuwu,

mowa musina soko yo kuwucila musunho,

kanyhi mowa musina soko yo kwihema.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu u Mulungu wenyu nha yo

komutonjenyi no kumucitilenyi mbavu zose.

U mwitumami mono yatuga magazo

13 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“U mwitumami wangu kolomoteswa mu mulimo wakwe,

nayo kolotuga lilumbo yiwaha muno muno.

14 Ne wanhu wenji watinuswa muno wali weza kumuwona,

hambi mumbwa yakwe

nha yali yihodanjice sunga yikawa vikamu kwijela nha munhu.

15 Ninga aluno makungugo menji gomwizina

sunga ne watemi si wololonga cinji yaye so wizine.

Nawo wololanga no kwizuka cinhu cono wali

wakali nacimanya yaye.”


Isaya 53

1 Nawo wanhu wolopitula watye

“Alo nani 'mbi mono yazihuwila zono cazilonga?

Hamba nani mono yawuwona muwoko wo Muwaha Mulungu muli zizi?

2 Hambi nha wali wo wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu,

u mwitumami wakwe yawiluce nhe cilimwa

cono cihimbize milela mu yisi nghavu.

Yasina futa hamba wuswanu ho cimulanje,

hakawa hasina cinji ciswanu muli mwene,

hamba cono cocikwega nase ho cimulanje.

3 Camuzwajiza kanyhi cikamulema

nayo yakangalila magazo no wusungu.

Ninga hasina munhu wo wose mono yali yamulanjile

si camucelelela, cikamuwona si cinji yaye.

4 “Ninga mwene yakangalila magazo gono gali

ganojeye gawe getu sese,

wusungu wono wali wucinojeye ciwutuje.

Nase cehwesa vyono magazo gakwe,

wali wulipicizwe wono Mulungu yawuleta.

5 Ninga yahomwa so wubi wetu

yakatowangwa so mahodango getu gono cetuma,

Nase cikuponywa so wulipicizwe wakwe wono yawutuga.

Na so wutamiswe wakwe, ase mo ciponyezwe.

6 Ase wose cinha zingholo zono zajiliye,

munhu yakubita na nzila yakwe mweneco.

Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakamulipiciza so sese,

u wulipicizwe wono wali wucinojeye sese co weneco.

7 “Yatendelwa vibi muno,

ninga yakangalila ko winenenyhezi, yakanyamala twii.

Yakawa nhe nyana ye ngholo yono yikutegulwa kuwulagwa,

kanyhi kotya nha ngholo yono yikunyonyolwa

wukunzeje wayo, hamba si yalonga cinji ng'o.

8 Yakebatwa, yakaling'anizwa, yakategulwa yawulagwe,

na hali hasina mono yawucelelela wusungu wakwe,

mwene yawulagwa so wubi we wanhu wetu.

9 Yakawikwa mu citenjelo hamonga ne wanhu wabi,

yakazikwa hamonga ne wagoli,

hamba yawe mwene yali si yetumile wubi,

hamba kulonga zo wulambi yaye.”

10 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nene no mweneco nali nyendile yagazwe,

ne lifwa lyakwe,

lyali lyo likumbiko lyono likuleta wunenelwe wo wubi.

Nayo kowawona wanagwe, kanyhi kokwikala vilo vinji.

Na muli mwene, wuhile wangu mo wofisilizizwa.

11 Nayo yalaza kuwa yapuluta mu magazo,

hanyuma kolowa na nyemo,

baho ho komanyila vyono kumbe magazo gakwe si gali ga bwete yaye.

U mwitumami wangu mwihuwilwa mono nyendezwe nayo,

yuyo yo kowudinghula wuling'anizwe wa wenji,

nane nowanenela ku soko yakwe.

12 Holeka nane nomupela lilumbo yiwaha,

hamonga ne wanhu we lilumbo liwaha,

kanyhi hamonga ne wanhu wono wali vilungo.

Hambi mwene nha yelavya wumi wakwe mweneco

sunga ko ku wufwe.

Yakawalilizwa hamonga ne wanhu wabi.

Yakawudinghula wubi wa wenji,

yakawalombela ho wanenelwe.”


Isaya 54

U wendo wo Muwaha Mulungu kuli Isilayeli

1 Agwe Yelusalemi, wali wihwanile mucekulu mugumba

mono si kalelaga,

ninga luno kokwimba muno ko wusangalale.

Hambi nha kowa wina wana wenji muno

wucizize no mucekulu mono yena no mulume wakwe.

2 Holeka luno wonjeleze wugazi we lihema lyako lyono wikaye,

wuyitalimhye ne milejehe yayo,

wuzisikamye ne zimambo zayo!

3 Kanyhi koyigazimhya ne mipaka yako mbavu zose,

hambi wanhu wako wono webatwe ne

makungugo ganji, nha wolowuya wose.

Ne makaya gono galekwa, gose gomema wanhu.

4 Holeka wulece kogopa,

hambi nha si kokozwa cevu hamba kupuvywa yaye kanyhi.

No wu wula wihuwilwa wako,

kowusemwa nho mucekulu mono yakali mudodo,

no wumondwe wo wufwile wako

hamba si kowukumbucila kanyhi yaye.

5 Hambi u muwumbi wako, nha yo mulume wako,

ne litagwa lyakwe yakucemwa Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.

U Muwaha Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli yo kolokuponya,

hambi mwene nha yo mutemi we yinze yose.

6 Agwe Isilayeli,

wehwana no mucekulu mono yakali mudodo,

mono yalecigwe no mulume wakwe,

mono yeza yali mu wusungu wuwaha.

Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakukucemeleza

wumuwuyile, yakutya nhavi,

7 “Ane nali nghulecile cipango cidodo du

ninga so wendo wangu wuwaha kuli'gwe

holeka nokukongholeza nghugomole kanyhi.

8 Na mwi'kuwo lyangu,

holeka nghehundika wusu wangu hali'gwe, cipango cidodo,

ninga nokulajila zo wendo wangu cibitilila.”

Nhavivi vyo yakutya Muwaha Mulungu Mukombozi wako.

9 “Nane mu cipango ca Noha,

nehuwiza nha vyono si nololetela lukulu

kanyhi liwazi mono'nze.

Naluno kanyhi nghwihuwiza

nha vyono si nolokukolelwa likuwo kanyhi yaye,

hamba kukutacila, hamba kukulipiciza ng'o.

10 Matunda ne vigongo, vyolotenjelemulwa,

ninga wendo wangu kuli'gwe si wolomalika yaye.

Nane nowukamisa sunga cibitilila wihuwizo wangu

wo mulamu kuli'gwe.”

Nhavivi vyo yakutya Muwaha Mulungu

mono yakukuwonela lusungu.

Yelusalemi vyono yolowa hanyuma

11 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Gwe Yelusalemi, mbeka wagazika muno,

'gwe kaya lyono lisina wutazi,

ninga 'ne noyizenga kanyhi misinji yayo

ne mabwe go wuguzi wukamu.”

12 Noyizenga minala yako ne vibwe vyo wuguzi wukamu,

milango yako nayo ne mabwe gono gakung'alala nha moto.

Aga nago majinji gose gono gakupilime

nogazenga nhavivyo ne vibwe vyo wuguzi wukamu.

13 Nane no mweneco nowejiza wanhu wako,

kanyhi nowamotya wekale wapolile.

14 Nagwe kosikamywa mu wiling'ano na mu ling'ani,

nagwe hamba si kokwiwonelwa kanyhi

hamba kuwa no woga yaye.

15 Na hono yunji yaze yakuhome,

yuyo kowa kuzinoza zizo mweneco du

ninga ane si nimukomboye yaye.

Kanyhi munhu wo wose mono kokwisumaniza nagwe,

munhu yoyo kolemwa mweneco du.

16 Ane nha yo nene mono namuwumba mutyani,

mono kafugutaga moto we makala,

kawa kutyana vitwalo,

kanyhi yo nene mono namuwumba musilikale,

mono kavitumamilaga no kuwulajila vitwalo vivyo.

17 “Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

howa hasina citwalo co cose

cono cosuma kukutamisa yaye.

Nagwe kowa wina yo kumupitula munhu

wo wose mono yakukusitaka.

Ane nowadima wetumami wangu

nowapela no wusume.”

Vyo yakutya Muwaha Mulungu.


Isaya 55

I cipegwa co lusungu lo Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Malenga gali baha,

holeka muze wose mono mukaluce!

Nanye wose mono musina pesa,

muze mugule ndigwa mubite mukalye!

Muze mung'we divayi ne mele,

ninga movituga bwete du!

2 Alu nhawulenyi mukwitumamila zipesa zenyu

mu wugula vinhu vyono visina cambuzi?

Hamba nhawulenyi mukwitumamila misahala yenyu,

ninga hamba si mukwiguta yaye?

Alu nane nghumutyenyi nhavi,

ta muhulicizaje nane zono nghulonga ye,

holeka nanye moyisangalalila

ndigwa swanu yono yicizize ndigwa zose.”

3 “Holeka 'lu muhulicize 'ne,

anye nyo wanhu wangu munyizize nene.

Munyizize nene holeka mowumipa!

Nane nonoza nanye cilagane ce cibitilila,

kanyhi nomulugutizenyi nha vyono namwihuwiza Dawudi.

4 Hambi nha namunoza Dawudi yawe mutemi,

no mulongola kuli makungugo gose.

Kanyhi muli mwene,

mo nghalajilila vilungo vyangu kuli makungugo.

5 Langa ta,

nanye mologacema makungugo gono si mugamanyile,

gono nago hamba si gamumanyenyi nyenye,

nago golomuzizenyi no kwilunza nanye!

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulugu wako u Mulungu mono nelile wa Isilayeli,

yo nene mono nolozinoza zizi zose zitumwe.

Nane nolomupelenyi lilumbo no lukumyo.”

6 Holeka mumupitucile Muwaha Mulungu,

mumulombe lono yakali yali behi.

7 Nawo i wanhu wabi, wayilekaje mikalile yawo mibi

wagapitule mahweso gawo mabi,

wamuwuyile Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu.

Hambi mwene nha yali lusungu,

kanyhi mwene kawanenelaga wanhu mbela.

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Mahweso gangu si gokwihwana ne mahweso genyu,

ne zinzila zangu nazo,

si zokwihwana ne zinzila zenyu ng'o.

9 Nha vyono muwuwonaga wulanga wuli

kutali muno kucanyha ye yisi,

ale nhavivyo vyo genha ne mahweso gangu,

nago gali kutali muno ne mahweso genyu.”

10 “Hambi zinghani zangu nazo,

nha zihwanile seluji ne mvula,

yono yilawaga mu mavunde yiwa kutonya mu yisi

yiwa kuviwilusa vilimwa no kulavya mbeyu

yono hanyuma yohadikwa,

na yinji yiwa kwitumamilwa so ndigwa.

11 Holeka yo nhavivyo sunga ne nghani yono nghuyilonga,

si yo mbuyila bwete caka yilece kwituma

zono niziwikanize mu mahweso gangu

ninga langa yokwituma zose du zono niyitumile yitume.”

12 “Holeka nanye molowuka mu Babiloni musangalaye,

kanyhi molotegulwa kunze ye likaya ko mulamu.

Nago matunda ne vigongo mwi'pango lilyo

vyolobahuka so wimbe,

imibici nayo yolozoza ne nyemo mbaha.

13 I mibici nayo yolocindika yihomele

hono ho gali mabijili,

nago mapangalale golocindika gahomele

hono ho gali mamivya.

Naco cico co colowa citanjilo cono colokwikala cibitilila,

colowa co cikumbucizo ca zono ane

no Muwaha Mulungu nakondya kuzitumama.”


Isaya 56

I wanhu wo Mulungu wolohanga na we makungugo gose

1 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela i wanhu wakwe yakutya,

“Ale, mwitumaje zo wiling'ano na ze ling'ani,

holeka nane nomuponyenyi mbela.

2 Nane nhondo zose nowamotya wanhu wose

wono wayikamise Sabato,

hamba kuyavufya yaye,

kanyhi nowamotya muno wono si wakwituma zimbi.”

3 No munhu mujenzi mono yakondya kwilunza

hamonga ne wanhu wo Mulungu,

alece kutya, “Muwaha Mulungu si konghombola

ho nimuhembe hamonga ne wanhu wakwe, aa ng'o.”

Hamba munhu mupaswe,

yalece kutya ku soko vyono si kosuma kutuga wana,

holeka si kosuma kwilunza ne wanhu wo Mulungu, aa ng'o.

4 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela i wapaswe,

yakuwatya munhu we liwala lilyo, vyono,

“Hono wuzikamise Sabato zangu,

no kumhela cogopo,

hamonga no kwituma zono zinyendezaga,

kanyhi no kucikamisa ko wihuwilwa cilagane cangu,

5 ale, litagwa lyako lyokumbucilwa mu Nyumba yangu

hamonga ne wanhu wangu,

kuciza na hono wali wuwe wina wana we cilume na we cicekulu.

Holeka hamba si kosemwelwa lukulu yaye.”

6 Kanyhi Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela awalya

i wajenzi wono wakondya kwilunza ne wanhu wakwe,

wono wamwendile no kumwitumamila,

kanyhi wono webate ne Sabato,

kanyhi wakamisize ko wihuwilwa i cilagane cakwe, yakutya,

7 “Anye nanye nomutegulenyi ku Sayuni kwi'tunda lyangu lyono lyelile.

Nimusangalazizenyi mu Nyumba yangu yo wuhembelo,

mbocele sunga ne makumbiko genyu

gono mowa kundavizya mu cikumbicilo cangu.

Ne Nyumba yangu,

yocemwa Nyumba yo wuhembelo

we wanhu wose we makungugo gose.”

8 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

mono yawagomola wanhu wakwe Isilayeli

ku kaya ku wulawa ku wuhamywe,

yakondya kwihuwiza nha vyono kowaleta na

wanhu wanji ho vihangane nawo.

I walongola we Waisilayeli wakuhoswa

9 Muwaha Mulungu yawalonjela wanhu we makungugo ganji weze nhe malimu, wawamele wanhu wakwe.
10 Yakatya, “Alu walongola wose wono wanojeye wawasugule wanhu wangu weza wali cibofu! Kanyhi hamba si wakumanya cinji co cose. Wehwana nhe mabwa gono si gafwemaga, nalu wene wagonile nhongo du wali mu ndoto. Nazo nhongo ho zili muzinzi lecenyi!
11 Kanyhi wehwana i mabwa malaku gono si gegutaga yaye. Na kanyhi walongola wawa, mbeka wasina hamba wizuce bwete. Hambi nha wene wose, munhu yakwituma nha vyono yakusaka mweneco du, no kulondola cambuzi cakwe du mweneco.
12 Nawo mu wung'wela wawo wo wujimbi wakutya, ‘Nye, lecenyi ta wayangu, ciwung'we wujimbi zuwali nha vyono cosuma! Holeka mitondo kolo wela, lyowa lizuwa yiswanu si nhe zuwali.’ ”


Isaya 57

U wuhemba vihwani we Waisilayeli wukuhoswa

1 I wanhu waswanu wakufwa,

hasina yunji mono yakwizuka hamba kuzicelelela zizo.

Ninga kolo wafwa,

howa hasina kanyhi wusungu wono wowatamisa.

2 Na wose wono wekaye mu mikalile miswanu,

wawo wacehaga no mulamu kolo wakondya kufwa.

3 Ninga anye nyo wabi,

muze ho muling'anizwe!

Hambi nha muli wabi kuciza no muhawi,

hamba no musenyha hamba no mucekulu malaya.

4 Alu mwene 'nye, muli wa nani nyenye lo,

mono mukunoza nha nzwaji?

Hamba 'lu muli nani mono mweza mwina lwedu

no kukwamvya zinimi zenyu?

5 Anye mono mukwisagahiza zo wusulumizi wenyu,

no kugona hasi ye mibici yenyu ye minghola i misiwanulwe,

muwa kwituma zo wusenyha.

Anye mono mulavyaga i wana wenyu

gawa makumbiko mu mapalika ge matalawe,

na mu malolo, behi ne vikolongo vye malenga.

6 Kanyhi muwa kusola ne mabwe

gono gatelemeye ga mwikolongo,

muwa kugahemba nha milungu.

Hanyuma muwa kusola ne divayi,

muwa kugalavizya cilavywa,

hamonga ne vilavywa vyo wusaje.

Ninga mulece kwihwesa nha vyono nane

nghwendezwa ne metumo genyu gago, aa ng'o.

7 Sunga na hanji, mukubita mucanyha ye matunda matali,

muwa kulavya makumbiko genyu kuko,

muwa kunoza na zo wusenyha wenyu mumo mu matunda.

8 Sunga muwa kusimya milungu yenyu

ye cikomaza ku gati ye milango yenyu ya kunze.

Hambi nha mwakondya kundeka 'ne.

Mukuyivula myenda yenyu muwa kugona

mu vitanda vyenu vigazi muno,

hamonga 'ne wahinza wenyu wono

muwapelile pesa ho wagonane nanye,

muwa kwiyigusha wusulumizi wenyu.

9 Mukwitajila wugutika hamonga ne mafuta ge mhunjile,

muwa kubita kumuhemba mulungu Moleki.

Kanyhi mukutuma wanhu wabite sunga mu ma'si go kutali,

sunga ko ku yisi ye wafu,

ho wakamulondolelenyi milungu yono mowa kuyihemba.

10 Mukwikataza bwete nyo weneco so wulondola milungu yinji,

hamba si mukupela yaye.

Hambi nha mukali kwihwesa nha vyono milungu yiyo ye cikomaza

yomupelenyi vilungo ho mulece kutimba.

11 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Alu mwene yiyi yo milunguci waco yono yikumogofyenyi,

sunga muwe kunonjela ane zo wulambi,

no kusemwa 'ne?

Hamba vyono nali nyamaye vilo vinji si nghulonga cinji,

holeka nanye mweza hamba si mukumhela cogopo yaye?

12 Anye mukwihwesa nha vyono,

zono mukwituma zo swanu,

ninga nozigubula calinze ze metumo genyu,

ne vihwani vyenyu hamba si vyosuma kumutazenyi yaye.

13 Na kolo mukusaka wutazi, ale,

cowona hono vivyo vihwani vyenyu vyomutazenyi!

Hambi cihumbi ce libeho nha covipemhelusiza kutali vihwani vyose!

Ninga i wanhu wono wanyitumbiye,

wawo du wo wolokwikala mu yisi,

kanyhi wo wolowa kuhemba nene mu Nyumba yangu.”

U wihuwizo wo Mulungu wo kuleta wutazi, no kuponya

14 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Muwalekaje wanhu wangu wambuyile.

Muviwusaje vikobazo vyose mu nzila yawo yono wokola!

Muyinozeleze nzila yawo, muyiwikanize.

15 “Hambi ane, nha yonene u Mulungu mono yelile,

mono nikalaga cibitilila.

Nikalaga i hanhu hono henuce na hono helile.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

nikalaga hamonga ne wanhu wenenenyhezi,

kanyhi wono wakondizye kunena.

Ho niwasikamye vilungo vyawo vya mu nhumbula,

hamonga no witumbilo.

16 Yo nene mono nawapela wanhu wangu wumi,

holeka si nosuma kujendelela kuwahosa

hamba kuwakolelwa likuwo cibitilila.

17 Nali niwakoleywe likuwo,

ku soko yo wubi wawo hamonga ne masulumizi gawo,

holeka nane nghawalipiciza no kuwaleka.

Nawo ho wakacila kuwa wasina cihuliko hamba nhavi,

wakajendelela du kwituma nha vyono wasakaga weneco.

18 “Nane nakondya kuziwona zono wetuma,

ninga ane nowaponya du.

Kanyhi nowatonga,

niwapele no wutazi,

hamonga na zizo,

nowasindamika wono wakulila.

19 Hambi nha nakondya kuwulavya mulamu

kuli wose wono wali behi na wono wekaye kutali.

Holeka nowaponya wanhu wangu.

20 Ninga i wanhu wabi,

wehwanile ne lilenga lyono lisagahaye,

lyono mawingo galyo si gatulaga yaye,

ne malenga galyo galapusaga madabaga hamonga ne makucilu.

21 Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

‘Muli wanhu wabi,

musina wuswanu wo mutulano yaye.’ ”


Isaya 58

U wuleka kulya we mbeka

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Alu wuzoze kwe lizi yiwaha munghango vyono kosuma!

Wuwalonjele wanhu wangu Isilayeli zo wubi wawo!

2 Hambi wene nha wahembaga'ne nhondo zose,

wakwiyitya vyono wena wuhile muno

wo kumanya zinzila zangu,

no kwibata wulajilize wangu.

Wakulonga wakutya vyono wakusaka muno

wapegwe malajilizo ge ling'ani,

na kanyhi vyono wendile muno ho wahembe nene.”

3 Ninga kanyhi akuno wanhu wakuwuza wakutya,

“Alu nhawulenyi cilece kulya zindigwa,

kumbe nayo Muwaha Mulungu nha si yakuwona yaye,

hamba nhawule cikale cisina ndigwa lono mwene si yakucitonganiza?”

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakuwapitula yakutya,

“Ninga i mbeka yoyi,

vyono anye mwi'pango lyono mukuleka kulya,

mwiyendezaga du nyo weneco,

no kuwewonela wetumami wenyu.

4 Hambi u wuleka kulya wenyu,

nha wukuwa munozenyi muwa muli wagazi,

muwa kwiwanganila no kwitowa.

Alu mukwihwesa nha vyono wuleka kulya we liwala lili,

wonoza ane nimuhulicenyi wulombe wenyu hongo?

5 Hambi lono mukuleka kulya,

nha mukwikapisa nyo weneco,

hanyuma muwa kuyigundamya mitwe yenyu yipamiza ho hasi,

yihwana nhe madulwe.

Na hanyuma muwa kutandika myenda ye magunila hasi,

muwa kwikupila ne mavu,

hanyuma muwa kugona mucanyha yago.

Alo wuwo wo wuleka kulya hongo?

Nanye mukwihwesa nha vyono nane

nokwendezwa ne zinghani zizo hongo?

6 “Ninga u wuleka kulya wono nyendile'ne wowu:

Muwusize lukulu manyolo go wiwonela wanji,

hamonga no wu wula ling'ani wenyu.

Muwalecele wawe welecele wono wakwiwonelwa.

7 Muwagawile zindigwa wono wali nzala,

muwalete ku zikaya zenyu i waciwa wono wasina wikalo.

Muwagawile vivwalo wono wasina co kuvwala.

Na kanyhi mweze wahidimbazi kuwataza wandugu zenyu.

8 “Na holeka baho no wuswanu wangu wong'alala

muli'nye nhe lizuwa lyono likusosomoka hakali mitondo,

ne zinghomele zenyu nazo zoponywa mbela mbela.

Nane nowa nili hamonga nanye nhondo zose ho nimuponyenyi.

Ne nhojo yangu yomucitilenyi mbavu zose.

9 Na lono mukulomba,

nane nomupitulenyi wulombe wenyu.

Na hono munyheme, nane nokwidika.

“Na hono mbeka mulecele lukulu kuwewonela wanji,

hamonga no wukonyeza wanhu na cidole ko kuwabedegala,

no kulecela lukulu makani gose mabi,

10 na hono muwe kuwagawila zindigwa wono wali nzala,

hamonga no kuwapela vyono viwakwiliye wono wanjiye,

ale, wutitu tuku wono wumemile muli 'nye,

wopituka wuwe wuzelu wa mu misi mugati.

11 Nane nomutonjenyi nhondo zose

no kumumotyenyi ne vinhu viswanu.

Nomunozenyi muwe ne vilungo hamonga

ne miwili miswanu yono yisina wunyoga-nyoga.

Nanye mowa mwihwanile busitani

yono mwajile malenga menji,

yono si yonyalila kanyhi lukulu.

12 Ne wanhu wenyu nhondo,

ho wozenga kanyhi i matongo gono gali galekwa vilo vinji,

wolozenga mucanyha ye misinji milongozi.

Nanye baho nhondo,

ho molomanyika nha vyono yo nyenye

mono muganozeleze wupya majinji,

no kuzinozeleza zinzila ze likaya,

ho zisume kukoleka kanyhi.”

I cambuzi co wibata Sabato

13 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Hono muyinoze Sabato yiwe yelile,

na kanyhi hono mulece kuyitumamila

so wiyendeza ne zenyu nyo weneco,

na hono mulyeze lizuwa lyangu

lyono lyelile no kulipela cogopo,

mulece kujenda mihinzo hamba kwituma milimo yenyu,

hamba kulonganjika nghani zono si zinojeye mwi'zuwa lilyo,

14 ale, nanye ho moyisangalalila

viswanu i nyemo yo kunyitumamila nene.

Nane nomunozenyi muwe ne lilumbo yiwaha mono'nze yose.

Nanye moyisangalalila muno muno

yisi yono namupela mulezi wenyu Yakobo.

Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Isaya 59

U muwizi yakuwuhosa wubi we wanhu

1 Ninga mulece kwihwesa

nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu musocele,

mono si kosuma kumuponyenyi,

hamba lunji yali ciziwi mono si kosuma kumuhulicenyi

lono mukumucemeleza mukusaka wutazi!

2 Heya, ku soko yo wubi wenyu,

holeka nayo siyakumuhulicenyi yaye.

Hambi nha wubi wenyu wono wukumutemanulenyi

no Mulungu lono mukujela kumuhemba.

3 Hambi anye nha muli wasutyi ku soko yo wulambi wenyu,

no wugazi wenyu,

hamonga no wuwulaji wenyu.

4 Mwendile muno kubita mu mahakama,

ninga kuno ho musina ling'ani yo yose ku lubavu lwenyu.

Hambi anye nha mwitumbilaga wulambi wenyu du,

ho musume calo cenyu.

Mwikalaga mwina mahweso go wutamisa wayenyu du.

5 No wihehemeze wenyu wono muwunozaga mugati yayo,

wina wuwulaji, nhe magangha ge lizoka lyono lina sumu.

Hono wulitule ligangha,

mugati yalyo lyolawila lizoka!

6 Ninga wihehemeze wenyu wuwo si wuswanu yaye,

kanyhi wusina cambuzi hamba cidodo bwete,

wihwanile mwenda we nhandabuli!

7 Kanyhi anye mwikalaga mukwihwesa

zimbi du nhondo zose,

no kuhidimbala kuzituma.

Hamba si mogopaga yaye ku wuwulaga

i wanhu wono wasina masutya.

Na hanhu ho hose hono mukubita,

mulekaga matongo du no wuhodanjice wuwaha,

8 na kolo mwafika baho,

hawezaga hasina kanyhi munhu mono kowa yena mulamu.

Hambi nha mwitumaga zose zono si zili ling'ani yaye.

Kanyhi mwinozezaga nzila yo wungwele,

na hasina munhu wo wose

mono kosuma kuyikola nzila yiyo, yawe mutelevu.

I wanhu wakuwunena wubi wawo

9 Nawo wanhu wakupitula wakutya,

“Alu nhondo camanya,

kumbe yo soko yono yikuleka Mulungu nayo

si yakuciponya nase kuli wanhu wono wakuciwonela.

Nase citumbiye wuzelu ho cisume kujenda viswanu,

ninga luno nha kutitu tuku du,

10 nase luno ceza kutunduwajila nha we cibofu.

Hamba mu misi mugati,

ase vyo cikwikobala du nha kotya cilo,

kotya nha cili mu citi ce yisi ye wafu.

11 Cimemile woga no wuhangawe.

Cina wuhile no Mulungu yaciponye

mu wiwonelwe wuwu hamonga ne mabi gaga,

ninga hasina hambe'pitulo bwete.

12 “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

masutya getu gono cikusutizye nha menji muno.

No wubi wetu wukucihomelela.

Nase cigamanyile masutya getu gose.

13 Cakubeza, cikakulema,

si casaka hamba no kukutyatya bwete.

Kanyhi cikuvumila nha vyono mbeka cawewonela wanji,

kanyhi cikakuleka.

Mbeka mahweso getu mabi,

ne zinghani zetu zose za wulambi.

14 No wuling'anizi wuswanu cawukumhila kutali,

ne ling'ani yose hamba si yili muli'se yaye.

Hambi i mbeka nha yigwiye mu nzila,

na zo mugoloka hamba si zosuma kwinjila yaye.

15 Heya i mbeka yili hasina,

na mono kalekaga zo wubi,

kewonaga nha yabenile wulajilize.”

Muwaha Mulungu yakwiwikaniza yawaponye wanhu wakwe

Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakondya kuziwona zizi zose,

nayo hamba si yakwendezwa yaye

lono yakuwona vyono hasina wiling'ano.

16 Nayo yakwizina kuwona vyono hasina munhu

hamba monga mono yakuwataziza wono wakwiwonelwa.

Holeka nayo luno kokwitumamila vilungo vyakwe yawaponye,

yawe no wusume.

17 Mwene kolowuvwala wiling'ano

nhe koti lyo wulugu lye cisilikale,

kanyhi kokwitumamila vilungo vyakwe viwe nha yo kofiya.

Kolokwipiligaza muno muno ho yaviwice vinhu vyose

mu wiling'ano no kugalipiciza

i mabi gose gono gawatamisaga wanhu.

18 Kolowalipiciza i walugu wakwe

mu nghango ya vyono wetuma,

hamonga na wose wono wekaye mu zi'si zo kutali.

19 Ne wanhu wose wono wekaye

ku lubavu lwa kucilima wose wolomupela cogopo,

hamonga ne vilungo vyakwe viwaha.

Hambi nha kolokuza nhe likolongo

lyono likuhuluma nhe libeho lye vilungo.

20 Muwaha Mulungu yakuwatya nhavi wanhu wakwe,

“Ane nolokuza ku Yelusalemi nimudimenyi,

no kumuponyenyi nyenye wose mono mukunena zo wubi wenyu.

21 Hambi nha nakondya kunoza cilagane nanye.

Nane nakondya kumujidilenyi muhe wangu,

nghamwijizenyi ne mejizo gangu ho gawe muli'nye cibitilila.

Holeka ku wulawilila lulu,

munojeye muhulice'ne,

kanyhi muwejize ne wana wenyu hamonga ne zindelo zenyu,

vyono wose wanojeye weze ne cihuliko kuli'ne ku wulawa lulu sunga cibitilila.”


Isaya 60

U lukumyo lwa hanyuma lwa Yelusalemi

1 Gwe Yelusalemi, wusinhuce wumulice

nhe lizuwa, hambi nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu

nha yakubulilila!

2 I makungugo ganji gogubikwa no wutitu,

ninga agwe, u wuzelu wo Muwaha Mulungu wokubulilila,

ne nhojo yakwe yowa muli'gwe.

3 Ne makungugo gose gokwejelwa ku wuzelu wako

Nawo watemi wokwegwa ne lizuwa lyako lipya.

4 Alu ta, wupituce wulanje ku zimbavu zako zose

wuciwone cono cikuza,

hambi i wanhu wako wose nha weza

kwikongholela kono kaya!

Nawo i wanago we cilume, wowuya ku wulawa

ku zi'si zo kutali,

nawo i wanago we cicekulu wolowa wadinghuywe

nhe wana wadodo.

5 Nagwe koloziwona zizi, ko wusangalale wuwaha

Ne nhumbula yako yolomema nyemo hamonga no wucicime.

Ne sawo yose ye makungugo gose,

kololetelwa gwegwe.

Sunga ne wugoli wawo wose wolokuziza gwegwe

ku wulawa ku nhumbi ye lilenga.

6 Sunga ne matewa mawaha ge zingamiya

nago golokuziza gwegwe

ku wulawa ku Midiyani na ku Efa.

Zolokuza ku wulawa Sheba, zikuletele

zahabu hamonga no wuvumba.

Ne wanhu wawo, wose wologanula ze nghani swanu

yono Muwaha Mulungu yetumile!

7 Kanyhi wolokuletela ne zingholo zose za ku Kedali

hamonga na za ku Nebayoti,

wuzitumamile so makumbiko,

zilavywe mu cikumbicilo, zimwendeze Muwaha Mulungu.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu koloyinoza Nyumba yakwe

yo wuhembelo yiwe no lukumyo luwaha kuciza vyono yali.

8 Alo ngalawaci zizi nyinji, zono zikuza

ziyagaye mwi'lenga nhe mavunde,

kotya nziwa zono zikuwuya mu manhuli gazo?

9 Zo ngalawa zono zikulawilila mu ma'si go kutali

zikuwagomola i wanhu wo Mulungu kono kwawo.

Wakuza wena hamonga ne fweza na zahabu

ho walipele lilumbo litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu.

U Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli,

mono yaganoza makungugo gose,

gawapele cogopo i wanhu wakwe.

10 Muwaha Mulungu yakuyilonjela Yelusalemi

yakutya nhavi,

“I wajenzi wo wolokuzenjela majinji gako,

nawo i watemi wawo, wolokwitumamila gwegwe.

Hamba yiwe ane nakulipiciziza mwi'kuwo lyangu,

ninga aluno nokulajila zo wendo wangu

no lusungu lwangu kuli'gwe.

11 Ne milango yako yolowa kwikala yili mwazi,

i watemi we makungugo ho wasume

kukuletela zisawo zawo.

12 Ne makungugo gono si gokwitumamila gwegwe,

ane nogongomezeza lukulu.”

13 “Ne mibici yose ye minanasi, na ye minghulwa,

hamonga ne mibici yinji yinji,

hamonga sunga ne mibici miswanu muno

yono yikulawa mu mbago ya Lebanoni,

yololetwa yose, ho yihanozeleze viswanu ha Yelusalemi.

Yiyinoze Nyumba yangu yo wuhembelo yiwe swanu,

no kulinoza likaya lyangu liwe no lukumyo luwaha.

14 Ne wana we cilume we wanhu wose wono wekalaga

wakumwiwonelenyi, wolomuzizenyi nyenye,

wamwinamilenyi ha mwande yenyu,

wakulajila ze cogopo cawo kuli'nye.

Sunga na wa walya wose wono wekalaga wakumubedegalenyi,

wolokuza wafugame ha mwande yenyu.

Nawo wolomucemenyi watye, ‘Likaya lyo Muwaha Mulungu,’

‘Sayuni, likaya lyo Mulungu mono yelile wa Isilayeli.’ ”

15 “Na ku wulawa lulu, hamba si kololecelwa lukulu kanyhi;

hamba kusulwa yaye ng'o,

hamba si kolowa kanyhi likaya yiciwa lyono

lihodanjice yaye.

Hambi ane nha nolokunoza wuwe ne lilumbo yiwaha

kanyhi wuwe wunojezize

wuwe hanhu hono hamemile nyemo cibitilila

na cibitilila.

16 Nago makungugo gose, hamonga ne watemi wawo,

wose wokucelelela muno muno

wowa nho nyina yo munhu vyono kamukocaga mwanagwe.

Nagwe baho ho komanyila nha vyono ane

no Muwaha Mulungu nakuponya.

Na vyono Mulungu we vilungo wa Isilayeli

yo Mukombozi wako.”

17 “Nane nolomupelenyi zahabu yihomele shaba

nayo fweza ne shaba, vihomele cuma ne mabici.

Naco cuma cihomele mabwe.

Ne watemi wenyu, nawo hamba si wolomwiwonelenyi yaye.

Hambi ane nha nolowanoza wateme

kwe ling'ani no mulamu.

18 Ne mazi go wuwulaji, hamba si golohulikwa

kanyhi yaye.

Hamba wongomezwe si woloyiziza yisi yenyu

kanyhi yaye.

Hambi ane nha nolomudimenyi

kanyhi nolowa nili 'jinji lyo kumucitilenyi.

Nanye molonghumya 'ne, vyono nakondya kumuponyenyi.

19 “Kanyhi ne lizuwa hamba si lyolomumulicilenyi

mu misi, hamba mwezi ku cilo.

Hambi ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nha yo nene

mono nolowa wuzelu wenyu cibitilila.

Wuzelu wo lukumyo lwangu wo wolomumulicilenyi.

20 Ne vilo vyenyu vyo wusungu, vyoloduma,

ane no Muwaha Mulungu yo nolowa wuzelu wenyu cibitilila,

mono nikalaga cibitilila si nhe lizuwa no mwezi.

21 Ne wanhu wako wolowa kwituma zo mugoloka,

nane nowapela yisi cibitilila.

Hambi nha yo nene mono nawawumba no kuwanoza,

ho ndajile wuwaha wangu kuli wanhu wose.

22 Holeka ku soko yiyo, sunga ne mhuga ndodo

ya muli'nye kanyhi yono yisoceye,

yolowa nghungugo mbaha muno kanyhi yili vilungo

kolo vipango vyayo vyafika.

Nane nozinoza zizi zijele mbela mbela,

ane no Muwaha Mulungu!”


Isaya 61

Luganuzi luswanu lo Wukombozi

1 Muwaha Mulungu,

kamemya'ne no muhe wakwe.

Kasiwanula ho niwaganulile luganuzi luswanu i waciwa,

niwaponye wono wadejepe mu nhumbula

niwanzize zo wulecelwe wawo awalya i wahamywa,

no wulecelwe wa kuli wafungwa mu jela.

2 Kanhuma ho niwalonjele vyono lipango lyafika,

lyono u Muwaha Mulungu kowaponyezya

wanhu wakwe, no kuwasumila kuli walugu wawo.

Kanhuma ho niwasindamice wono wali mu wusungu.

3 Ho niwasangalaze no kuwapela nyemo

wanhu wose wono wali mu Sayuni,

wono wali wali mu wusungu no wukalale

wawuce mu wukalale, wembe lwimbo lo lukumyo.

Kanyhi wawe nhe mibici i mihadikwe no

Muwaha Mulungu mweneco.

Kanyhi wawe kwituma zo mugoloka.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu ho yakumizywe muli zono yetuma.

4 Kanyhi wologazenga ne makaya gono

gali gakondya kuwa matongo.

5 Nye nyo wanhu wangu, i wajenzi wo wolomwitumamilenyi

kanyhi wo wolowa wo wadimi we mitugo zenyu,

no kumulimilenyi ne migunda yenyu,

hamonga no kuyilanjilila ne migunda yenyu ye zabibu.

6 Nanye molowa mumanyice vyono muli

wakohani wo Muwaha Mulungu

i wetumami wo Mulungu wetu.

Kanyhi moloyisangalalila sawo ye makungugo,

akuno mukwilumba nha vyono yeza yenyu nyenye.

7 Ne cevu cenyu colowucila lukulu.

Hambi lu nha molowa mwikaye mu yisi yenyu nyo weneco.

Ne sawo yenyu yolokonjelezeka kali kejete.

Ne nyemo yenyu yolowa ye cibitilila.

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ane nyendile ze ling'ani, kanyhi nizisulile

zo wuhizi na zo wiwonezi.

Nane nowahumbiliza wanhu wangu ko wihuwilwa.

Kanyhi nonoza nawo cilagane ce cibitilila.

9 Kanyhi wolowa wamanyice muno muli makungugo.

Na muli wose mono kowawona, komanya nha vyono

wo wanhu wono nakondya kuwamotya.”

10 Yelusalemi yisangalaliye muli zono

Muwaha Mulungu yazitumile.

Yehwana nha bibi halusi mono yenozeleze

yabite ku wutozwe.

Mulungu kakondya kuyivwika Yelusalemi

mwenda we mhonelo no wusume.

11 Nha vyono zimbeyu zicindikaga ziwa kuwiluka,

ale nhavivyo no Muwaha Mulungu nayo

vyo kowaponya wanhu wakwe.

Ne makungugo gose gomukumya mwene.


Isaya 62

1 Nane nolonga du ho niyisikamye Yelusalemi.

Hamba si nonyamala yaye, sunga kolo yakondya kuponywa,

no wukombozwe wayo weza kumulika

nha tala mu mapango ge cilo.

2 Gwe Yelusalemi, i makungugo gowuwona wiling'ano wako,

Ne watemi wayo, nawo wowuwona lukumyo lwako.

Nagwe kocemwa litagwa yipya,

i litagwa lyono kowa wapegwa no Muwaha Mulungu mweneco.

3 Nagwe kolowa wuli mukumbo wo wulenzi

wo Muwaha Mulungu.

4 Na baho, hamba si kocemelwa lukulu kanyhi

“Mulekwa.” Hamba ne yisi yako wayitye

“Mucekulu Mulekwa.”

Langa litagwa lyako lipya lyolowa “U Mulungu

Kendezwa Nayo.”

Nayo yisi yako yolocemwa “U mutolwe Musangalavu.”

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha kendezwa nagwe.

Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kowa nha yo mulume we yisi yako.

5 Kokwihwana nho muzelelo mono yakumusola

muhinza mono si yamanyile walume, yawe bibi halusi wakwe.

Holeka nagwe, yuyo mono yakuwumba,

yuyo yo mono kokutola agwe.

Kanyhi nha vyono bwana halusi kendezwaga

na bibi halusi wakwe,

Ale nhavivyo no Mulungu wako nayo

vyo kokwendezwa nagwe.

6 Gwe Yelusalemi, nane nghuwiciye walindilizi

mu majinji gako.

Nawo hamba si wowa kunyamala misi ne cilo.

Langa wowa kumukumbuciza Muwaha Mulungu

zo wihuwizo wakwe.

Hamba si womuleceleza yasemwe yaye.

7 Kanyhi hamba si womuleka yaceheze yaye,

sunga kolo yakondya kuyigomolela Yelusalemi

lilumbo lyayo.

No kulinoza liwe yikaya lyo lukumyo mono yinze yose.

8 Muwaha Mulungu kakondya kwihuwiza ko wilahize,

nayo kowufisiliza wihuwizo wakwe

kwe vilungo vyakwe.

“Ne walugu wako hamba si wowulila lukulu no wuhemba wako.

Ne wajenzi nawo, hamba si woying'wela lukulu ne divayi yako.

9 Langa gwegwe go mweneco du, mono

wuhadice no kududula wuhemba,

yo gwegwe mono kowulya no kumukumya Muwaha Mulungu.

Kanyhi gwegwe mono wali kuzilanjilila zabibu

hamonga no kuzidudula,

Yo gwegwe du mono koying'wa yiyo divayi

mu luwa za mu Nyumba yangu.”

10 Holeka 'lu anye wanhu wa mu Yelusalemi,

ale mulawile kunze ye likaya, mukayinozeleze

nzila yono woyikola, i wanhu wono wakuwuya kono kaya!

Muyinozeleze nzila mbaha, mugawuse mabwe gose!

Muwicile ne citanjilo, makungugo gose ho gamanye,

11 Nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela wanhu

wose wa mono'nze yakutya,

“Ale, muwalonjele i wanhu wa mu Yelusalemi

nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yakuza kumuponyenyi,

Yakuwaleteleza hamonga ne wanhu wono

yakondya kuwaponya.”

12 Nanye lulo molocemwa “Wanhu wo Mulungu mono Yelile.

I wanhu wono Muwaha Mulungu yawakomboze.”

Nayo Yelusalemi yolocemwa, “Likaya lyono Mulungu yalyendile.

Likaya lyono Mulungu si yalileka yaye.”


Isaya 63

Wusume wo Muwaha Mulungu muli Makungugo

1 “Alo yuyo nani mono yakulawa kwi'kaya lya Bozila mu Edomu?

Alo yo nani lo mono yavwalile myenda ye nhojo,

midunghu kasu.

Mono yakujenda-jenda mu wuwaha we vilungo vyakwe?

Ayo yo Muwaha Mulungu mono yali vilungo ku wuponye,

yo yakuza kwanza zo wusume wakwe.

2 Alo nhawule lo myenda yakwe midunghu kasu,

yihwanile myenda yo munhu mono yali kuminya zabibu, yanoze divayi?”

3 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakupitula yakutya,

“Nakondya kuwakanyanjizanga i makungungo nha zabibu,

ninga hasina hamba munhu monga mono yazize kunhaza.

Nane niwakanyanjiziza mwi'kuwo lyangu,

ne sakami yawo yimizucila mu myenda yangu yose.

4 Nane nghatya, vyono,

i cipango co kuwaponezya wanhu wangu,

cakondya kufika, co cipango luno co kuwalipiciza i walugu wawo.

5 Ninga nghezina muno,

nali neza kuwona vyono hasina hamba munhu monga mono kosuma kunhaza.

Ninga likuwo lyangu du lyo limhelile vilungo vyo kwitumila zizo,

sunga niwa kutuga wusume nili wene.

6 Na mwi'kuwo lyangu nghagakanyanjiza makungugo gose nghagongomeza.

Nghayimwaga hasi i sakami yo wumi wawo.”

U wuswanu wo Muwaha Mulungu kuli Isilayeli

7 Nane nolukumya lusungu lo Muwaha Mulungu,

na kanyhi nghumukumya muno so zose zono yakondya kucitendela.

Hambi nha kawamotya muno i wanhu wa Isilayeli

ku soko yo lusungu lwakwe no wendo wakwe.

8 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha yatya,

“Awa nha wanhu wangu,

hamba si wonghonga yaye.”

Holeka ku soko yiyo, yakawaponya,

9 ku wulawa mu magayo gawo.

Hamba si yali malayika yaye mono yawaponya,

langa Muwaha Mulungu mweneco du yo yawaponya.

Yawaponya ku soko yo wendo wakwe no lusungu lwakwe.

Hambi nha yawadima sunga na mu vilo vyono vyapuluta,

10 ninga wene wakamubeza Mulungu,

wakamukalaza muhe wakwe mono yelile.

Holeka nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakawapitucila yakawa mulugu wawo, yakawahoma.

11 Baho nhondo nawo wanhu wakwe,

wakakumbucila i vilo vye katali,

vya Musa u mwitumami wo Muwaha Mulungu,

wakawa kwiwuza wakutya,

“Alu mwene yali hayi u Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yawaponya i walongola we wanhu wakwe mwi'lenga?

Hamba yali hayi mwene u Muwaha Mulungu mono yamupela Musa u muhe wakwe?

12 Hamba yali hayi mwene Muwaha Mulungu mono yetumila zimbaha muli Musa,

kwe vilungo vyakwe vinji,

yakagatemanula i malenga ge lilenga,

yakawalowoseza wanhu hakavu haciligati ye malenga,

ho yetujile lilumbo lye cibitilila?”

13 Nawo wajenda wasina mahwele nhe zifwalasi za mu nyika,

hamba si wekobaza yaye,

hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha yo yawatongaga.

14 Wakawa nha ng'ombe zikutegulwa kudima mwi'hanze lidece lya mwi'lolo.

Na mu nzila yiyo,

u Muwaha Mulungu, yakawapela wucehe wanhu wakwe.

Yawatonga wanhu wakwe, na mu nzila yiyo,

yakaliletela lilumbo yiwaha litagwa lyakwe.

Lilombo lyo kulomba lusungu no wutazi kuli Mulungu

15 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wucilangaje ase ku wulawa kuko ku wulanga ku wikalo wako,

mu wele wako no lukumyo.

Alo wupiligazi wako wa kuli'se, wuli hayi lo?

Hamba vilungo vyako, vili hayi lu?

No wendo wako wa kuli'se,

hamonga no lusungu lwako, vili hayi lo?

Wulece kucisemwa yaye.

16 Hambi'gwe nha wuli sogo yetu.

Sunga ne walezi wetu Abulahamu na Yakobo,

si wacimanyile ase yaye.

Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe sogo yetu.

Yo gwegwe mono kuciponyaga nhondo zose.

17 Alu socici lu wukucileceleza cipatuce mu zinzila zako?

Hamba socici lo,

wukucileceleza ciwe cisina cihuliko no kukuleka?

Alu wuwuye ku soko ye wetumami wako na ku soko ye wanhu

wono wawezaga wako nhondo zose.

18 Ase co wanhu wako wasiwanulwe,

cali cakondya kusolwa ne walugu wetu kwa cidaha cidodo du,

nawo wakawukanyajizanga wuhembelo wako.

19 Nagwe wukacitendela kotya nha si wali wucitemile yaye,

kotya nha si cali cili wanhu wako ng'o.


Isaya 64

1 Alo socici lo si wukuwudemula wulanga, wutime hasi?

Ne matunda gali gakuwone, nago gacicime so woga.

2 Gali gacicime nhe malenga gono matelece mu moto mukali.

Holeka wuze wilajile vilungo vyako kuli walugu wako,

wuganoze makungugo gacicime ha mwande yako!

3 Hambi mu madaha ganji lono waziza,

wuketuma zimbaha zono si cali cizilanjiliye,

i matunda gakawona gakacicima so woga.

4 Kanyhi hasina hamba munhu monga mono yamuwona,

hamba kuhulika zo Mulungu yunji wo wose mono yehwanile gwegwe,

mono ketumaga metumo gono genha gaga,

kuli wose wono wamwitumbiye.

5 Kanyhi kuwabocelaga wose wono wasangalilaga mu wituma ziswanu,

wono wakumanya vyono,

agwe wukuwahila wetume zo wuhile wako.

Nagwe wali weza kucikolelwa likuwo,

nase cikajendelela kwituma wubi.

Na ku soko ye likuwo lyako liwaha,

nase cikajendelela du kwituma zimbi ku wulawa katali.

6 Nase wose cili wanyo wubi,

sunga ne metumo getu gono gakwijela nha maswanu,

gose gatipiye du.

Na ku soko yo wubi wetu,

ceza cihwanile nhe mahamha gono ganyalile gakuputwa no kupemheluswa ne mbeho.

7 Heza hasina hamba yumonga mono yakukulomba,

hamba mono yakulondola wutazi kuli'gwe.

Hambi nha wihundice hali'se,

kanyhi wakondya kucileka ku soko yo wubi wetu.

8 Ninga 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe du sogo yetu.

Ase cihwanile lilongo,

nagwe wihwanile u muwumbi we zinyungu.

Agwe yo gwegwe mono waciwumba,

9 holeka dedede wulece kucikolelwa likuwo lyako,

hamba kuwukumbucila wubi wetu cibitilila.

Hambi'se nha cili wanhu wako,

holeka cikukunena wuciwonele lusungu.

10 Alu lanje, makaya gako i masiwanulwe,

vyono gehwana nyika,

nayo Yelusalemi yaweza yitongo lyono lilecigwe du.

11 Nayo Nyumba yako yo wuhembelo yono yasiwanulwa,

i hanhu hono hali haswanu muno,

hono i walezi wetu wakukumizyaga gwegwe,

alu hakondya kunyanywa no moto.

Ne hanhu hose hono cali cihendile muno, alu geza matongo du.

12 Alu na muli zizi zose,

agwe ho kojendelela kwiyimila du?

Alu mwene, ho sikocitaza ng'o,

ho kocileka cijendelele kutamiswa

mu nghango yono hamba si cosuma kuyikangalila?


Isaya 65

U wulipicize wo Mulungu kuli Wabedegazi

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, nhavi,

“Ane nali niwikanize niwapitule wulombe wawo wanhu wangu,

ninga nawo hamba si wakulomba yaye.

Nali niwikanize wanondole wambone,

ninga nawo hamba kujela bwete kunondola.

Sunga ne likungugo nalyo lyono si lyasaka kunomba yaye,

hamba niwe 'ne nali niwikanize nhondo zose niwapitule nitye,

‘Ane yone nili baha, nane nomutazenyi.’

2 Nane nha niwikanize du nhondo zose

ho niwabocele wanhu wangu wono wasina cihuliko,

kanyhi wendile kwituma zimbi nha vyono wendile weneco,

no kubita kono wendile.

3 Kanyhi wasina hamba cevu yaye lono wakunghoza likuwo.

Hambi nha wendile kulavya makumbiko ge wanhu

wono si wamumanyile Mulungu mu zibusitani zisiwanulwe,

hamonga no kufusa wuvumba mu vikumbicilo vye wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu.

4 Na mu mapango ge cilo,

wawa kubita mu vitenjelo na mu mapango,

wawa kuwuza mahweso kuli myuhe ya wono wafwa.

Wawa kulya nyama ya ngubi,

no kung'wa muhuzi we nyama hanza-hanze ne mboga,

yono mutelece mu wulawa mu nyama,

yono ndavye mu makumbiko ge wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu.

5 Hanyuma, wawa kuwalonjela wanhu wanji wakutya,

‘Mulece kucisejelela behi,

hambi 'se nha si cinojeye cipamizwe na nyenye,

hambi 'se nha celile!’

Holeka nane,

si nosuma kwikalana ne wanhu we liwala lilyo.

Ne likuwo lyangu kuli wene,

nha lyacile kotya moto wono si wuzimaga.

6 “Nane nakondya kuwawikaniziza wulipicizwe wawo,

no wuling'anizwe wawo, wakondya kwandikwa.

Nane hamba si nozisemwa yaye zono wetumile,

ninga langa nowalipiciza du,

7 ku soko yo wubi wawo,

hamonga no wubi we walezi wawo.

Lanje vyono wakufusa wuvumba

mu vikumbicilo vye wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu,

vyono vizenje mu vigongo visiwanulwe,

no kunhovulanga.

Holeka nane,

nowalipiciza du nha vyono genha metumo gawo gono gapuluta.”

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Hambi nha hasina munhu mono kazihodangaga zi zabibu ziswanu,

langa waziminyaga no kuzinoza divayi.

Ale yo nhavivyo, nane hamba si nowongomezeza lukulu wanhu wangu wose ng'o,

langa nowasigaza wose wono wakunyitumamila.

9 Nowamotya i Waisilayeli we nghungugo ya Yuda,

ne wana wawo, woloyihala yisi yangu ye matunda.

I wanhu wangu wahagulwa,

wono wakunyitumamila, wawo wo wolokwikala mumo.

10 Nawo wolowa kuhemba nene,

ne zingholo zawo hamonga ne zing'ombe zawo,

wolozilongoza wazitegule ku wudimo ku yisi ya Shaloni

yo wuhihi ku lubavu lwa kumwezi,

na mu nyika ya mwi'lolo lye magayo, ku lubavu lwa kucilima.

11 “Ninga holowa no wisimajile kuli'nye mono mukundeka,

no kuyibedegala Sayuni,

i litunda lyangu lisiwanulwe,

muwa kumuhemba Gadi na Meni,

i milungu yono yisina cambuzi co cose,

yono yiwiciywe wongomele.

12 Nanye vyo mokongomela nhavivyo mu nzila yo kuwulagwa,

vyono lono nali kumucemenyi,

hamba si mwasaka kunyidika yaye,

hamba kuhuliciza lono nghulonga.

Hambi nha mweyihagulila nyo weneco

muwe musina cihuliko kuli'ne no kwituma zimbi.

13 Ninga nane nghumulonjelenyi nha vyono,

wose wono wena cihuliko kuli'ne no kuhemba nene,

wawo wolotuga ndigwa nyinji hamonga na co kung'wa.

Ninga anye molowa muli nzala kanyhi mukaluce.

Wene wolowa wasangalaye, ninga anye molokozwa cevu.

14 Wene wolokwimba so nyemo,

ninga anye mololila so wukalale wa mu nhumbula.

15 I wanhu wangu wahagulwa,

wololitumamila litagwa lyenyu nhe lijito.

Nane no Muwaha Mulungu,

nomuwulajenyi wose.
